// ============================================================
// FILE: ./src/main/java/com/ecms_web_application/OC01/controller/AuthController.java
// ============================================================
package com.ecms_web_application.OC01.controller;

import com.ecms_web_application.OC01.config.CookieUtil;
import com.ecms_web_application.OC01.dto.request.auth.*;
import com.ecms_web_application.OC01.dto.response.RequestResponse;
import com.ecms_web_application.OC01.dto.response.auth.LoginResponse;
import com.ecms_web_application.OC01.dto.response.auth.RegisterResponse;
import com.ecms_web_application.OC01.dto.response.auth.UserInfoResponse;
import com.ecms_web_application.OC01.exception.ErrorHandler;
import com.ecms_web_application.OC01.service.AuthenticationService;
import jakarta.servlet.http.HttpServletRequest;
import jakarta.servlet.http.HttpServletResponse;
import jakarta.validation.Valid;
import lombok.RequiredArgsConstructor;
import lombok.extern.slf4j.Slf4j;
import org.springframework.http.HttpStatus;
import org.springframework.http.ResponseEntity;
import org.springframework.web.bind.annotation.*;


import java.time.Duration;
import java.time.LocalDateTime;

/**
 * Controller xử lý xác thực.
 * - AUTH-01: Đăng ký            (POST /api/auth/register)
 * - AUTH-02: Đăng nhập          (POST /api/auth/login)
 * - AUTH-03: Đăng xuất          (POST /api/auth/logout)
 * - AUTH-04: Refresh token      (POST /api/auth/refresh-token)
 * - AUTH-05: Lấy user hiện tại  (GET  /api/auth/me)
 * - AUTH-06: Đổi mật khẩu       (POST /api/auth/change-password)
 * - AUTH-07: Quên mật khẩu      (POST /api/auth/forgot-password)
 * - AUTH-08: Xác minh OTP       (POST /api/auth/verify-otp)
 * - AUTH-09: Đặt lại mật khẩu   (POST /api/auth/reset-password)
 */
@RestController
@RequestMapping("/api/auth")
@RequiredArgsConstructor
@Slf4j
public class AuthController {

    private final AuthenticationService authenticationService;
    private final CookieUtil cookieUtil;

    // ============================================================
    // AUTH-01: REGISTER
    // ============================================================
    @PostMapping("/register")
    public ResponseEntity<RequestResponse> register(@Valid @RequestBody RegisterRequest request) {
        RegisterResponse data = authenticationService.register(request);
        RequestResponse response = new RequestResponse(data, "Đăng ký thành công");
        return ResponseEntity.status(HttpStatus.CREATED).body(response);
    }

    // ============================================================
    // AUTH-02: LOGIN
    // ============================================================
    @PostMapping("/login")
    public ResponseEntity<RequestResponse> login(@Valid @RequestBody LoginRequest request,
                                                 HttpServletResponse httpResponse) {
        LoginResponse data = authenticationService.login(request);
        setAuthCookies(httpResponse, data);
        RequestResponse response = new RequestResponse(data, "Đăng nhập thành công");
        return ResponseEntity.ok(response);
    }

    // ============================================================
// AUTH-03: LOGOUT
// ============================================================
    @PostMapping("/logout")
    public ResponseEntity<RequestResponse> logout(HttpServletRequest httpRequest,
                                                  HttpServletResponse httpResponse) {

        // Lấy refresh token hiện tại từ cookie
        String refreshToken = cookieUtil.getRefreshTokenFromCookies(httpRequest).orElse(null);

        // Revoke CHỈ session này + clear context + xóa cookie
        authenticationService.logout(refreshToken, httpResponse);

        RequestResponse response = new RequestResponse("Đăng xuất thành công");
        return ResponseEntity.ok(response);
    }
    // ============================================================
    // AUTH-04: REFRESH TOKEN
    // ============================================================
    @PostMapping("/refresh-token")
    public ResponseEntity<RequestResponse> refreshToken(HttpServletRequest httpRequest,
                                                        HttpServletResponse httpResponse) {
        // Lấy refresh token từ cookie
        String refreshToken = cookieUtil.getRefreshTokenFromCookies(httpRequest)
                .orElseThrow(() -> new ErrorHandler(
                        HttpStatus.UNAUTHORIZED, "Refresh token not found"));

        // Sinh access token mới
        LoginResponse data = authenticationService.refreshToken(refreshToken);
        setAuthCookies(httpResponse, data);

        RequestResponse response = new RequestResponse(data, "Làm mới token thành công");
        return ResponseEntity.ok(response);
    }

    // ============================================================
    // AUTH-05: GET CURRENT USER
    // ============================================================
    @GetMapping("/me")
    public ResponseEntity<RequestResponse> getCurrentUser() {
        UserInfoResponse data = authenticationService.getCurrentUserInfo();
        RequestResponse response = new RequestResponse(data, "Lấy thông tin thành công");
        return ResponseEntity.ok(response);
    }

    /** USER-02: PUT /api/users/me */
    @PutMapping("/me")
    public ResponseEntity<RequestResponse> updateProfile(
            @Valid @RequestBody UpdateProfileRequest request) {
        UserInfoResponse data = authenticationService.updateProfile(request);
        return ResponseEntity.ok(new RequestResponse(data, "Cập nhật thông tin thành công"));
    }
    // ============================================================
    // AUTH-06: CHANGE PASSWORD
    // ============================================================
    @PostMapping("/change-password")
    public ResponseEntity<RequestResponse> changePassword(
            @Valid @RequestBody ChangePasswordRequest request,
            HttpServletResponse httpResponse) {
        authenticationService.changePassword(request);

        // Đổi pass → xóa cookie (buộc login lại)
        cookieUtil.clearCookies(httpResponse);

        RequestResponse response = new RequestResponse("Đổi mật khẩu thành công, vui lòng đăng nhập lại");
        return ResponseEntity.ok(response);
    }

    // ============================================================
    // AUTH-07: FORGOT PASSWORD
    // ============================================================
    @PostMapping("/forgot-password")
    public ResponseEntity<RequestResponse> forgotPassword(
            @Valid @RequestBody ForgotPasswordRequest request) {
        authenticationService.forgotPassword(request);
        RequestResponse response = new RequestResponse("OTP đã được gửi đến email của bạn");
        return ResponseEntity.ok(response);
    }

    // ============================================================
    // AUTH-08: VERIFY OTP
    // ============================================================
    @PostMapping("/verify-otp")
    public ResponseEntity<RequestResponse> verifyOtp(
            @Valid @RequestBody VerifyOtpRequest request) {
        String resetToken = authenticationService.verifyOtp(request);
        RequestResponse response = new RequestResponse(resetToken, "Xác minh OTP thành công");
        return ResponseEntity.ok(response);
    }

    // ============================================================
    // AUTH-09: RESET PASSWORD
    // ============================================================
    @PostMapping("/reset-password")
    public ResponseEntity<RequestResponse> resetPassword(
            @Valid @RequestBody ResetPasswordRequest request) {
        authenticationService.resetPassword(request);
        RequestResponse response = new RequestResponse("Đặt lại mật khẩu thành công, vui lòng đăng nhập");
        return ResponseEntity.ok(response);
    }


    // ============================================================
    // HELPER: Set cookies cho access + refresh token
    // ============================================================
    private void setAuthCookies(HttpServletResponse httpResponse, LoginResponse data) {
        // Cookie access token
        int accessTtlSeconds = (int) Duration.between(
                LocalDateTime.now(),
                data.getExpiresAt()
        ).getSeconds();
        cookieUtil.addAccessTokenCookie(httpResponse, data.getAccessToken(), accessTtlSeconds);

        // Cookie refresh token (30 ngày)
        int refreshTtlSeconds = 30 * 24 * 60 * 60;
        cookieUtil.addRefreshTokenCookie(httpResponse, data.getRefreshToken(), refreshTtlSeconds);
    }
}

// ============================================================
// FILE: ./src/main/java/com/ecms_web_application/OC01/controller/CategoryController.java
// ============================================================
package com.ecms_web_application.OC01.controller;

import com.ecms_web_application.OC01.dto.request.CategoryCreateRequest;
import com.ecms_web_application.OC01.dto.request.CategoryUpdateRequest;
import com.ecms_web_application.OC01.dto.response.CategoryResponse;
import com.ecms_web_application.OC01.dto.response.CategoryTreeResponse;
import com.ecms_web_application.OC01.dto.response.RequestResponse;
import com.ecms_web_application.OC01.service.CategoryService;
import jakarta.validation.Valid;
import lombok.RequiredArgsConstructor;
import lombok.extern.slf4j.Slf4j;
import org.springframework.data.domain.Page;
import org.springframework.data.domain.PageRequest;
import org.springframework.data.domain.Pageable;
import org.springframework.data.domain.Sort;
import org.springframework.http.HttpStatus;
import org.springframework.http.ResponseEntity;
import org.springframework.web.bind.annotation.*;

import java.util.List;

/**
 * Category API — /api/categories
 *
 * Read (PUBLIC):
 *  - GET    /                       → search (paging + filter)
 *  - GET    /{id}                   → chi tiết
 *  - GET    /tree                   → cây đầy đủ
 *  - GET    /tree/{rootId}          → cây con từ root
 *  - GET    /roots                  → danh mục gốc
 *  - GET    /{parentId}/children    → danh mục con trực tiếp
 *
 * Write (PROTECTED — cần permission):
 *  - POST   /                       → tạo mới
 *  - PUT    /{id}                   → cập nhật
 *  - DELETE /{id}                   → xóa
 */
@RestController
@RequestMapping("/api/categories")
@RequiredArgsConstructor
@Slf4j
public class CategoryController {

    private final CategoryService categoryService;

    // ============================================================
    // READ
    // ============================================================

    /** GET /api/categories — phân trang + filter */
    @GetMapping
    public ResponseEntity<RequestResponse> search(
            @RequestParam(required = false) String search,
            @RequestParam(required = false) Boolean isActive,
            @RequestParam(required = false) Long parentId,
            @RequestParam(defaultValue = "0") int page,
            @RequestParam(defaultValue = "20") int size,
            @RequestParam(defaultValue = "name") String sortBy,
            @RequestParam(defaultValue = "asc") String sortDir) {

        Sort sort = "desc".equalsIgnoreCase(sortDir)
                ? Sort.by(sortBy).descending()
                : Sort.by(sortBy).ascending();
        Pageable pageable = PageRequest.of(page, size, sort);

        Page<CategoryResponse> data = categoryService.search(search, isActive, parentId, pageable);
        return ResponseEntity.ok(new RequestResponse(data, "Lấy danh sách category thành công"));
    }

    /** GET /api/categories/{id} */
    @GetMapping("/{id}")
    public ResponseEntity<RequestResponse> getById(@PathVariable Long id) {
        CategoryResponse data = categoryService.getById(id);
        return ResponseEntity.ok(new RequestResponse(data, "Lấy category thành công"));
    }

    /** GET /api/categories/tree — cây đầy đủ */
    @GetMapping("/tree")
    public ResponseEntity<RequestResponse> getFullTree() {
        List<CategoryTreeResponse> data = categoryService.getFullTree();
        return ResponseEntity.ok(new RequestResponse(data, "Lấy cây category thành công"));
    }

    /** GET /api/categories/tree/{rootId} — cây con từ root */
    @GetMapping("/tree/{rootId}")
    public ResponseEntity<RequestResponse> getTreeFromRoot(@PathVariable Long rootId) {
        List<CategoryTreeResponse> data = categoryService.getTreeFromRoot(rootId);
        return ResponseEntity.ok(new RequestResponse(data, "Lấy cây category thành công"));
    }

    /** GET /api/categories/roots */
    @GetMapping("/roots")
    public ResponseEntity<RequestResponse> getRootCategories() {
        List<CategoryResponse> data = categoryService.getRootCategories();
        return ResponseEntity.ok(new RequestResponse(data, "Lấy root category thành công"));
    }

    /** GET /api/categories/{parentId}/children */
    @GetMapping("/{parentId}/children")
    public ResponseEntity<RequestResponse> getChildren(@PathVariable Long parentId) {
        List<CategoryResponse> data = categoryService.getChildren(parentId);
        return ResponseEntity.ok(new RequestResponse(data, "Lấy category con thành công"));
    }

    // ============================================================
    // WRITE
    // ============================================================

    /** POST /api/categories */
    @PostMapping
    public ResponseEntity<RequestResponse> create(@Valid @RequestBody CategoryCreateRequest request) {
        CategoryResponse data = categoryService.create(request);
        return ResponseEntity.status(HttpStatus.CREATED)
                .body(new RequestResponse(data, "Tạo category thành công"));
    }

    /** PUT /api/categories/{id} */
    @PutMapping("/{id}")
    public ResponseEntity<RequestResponse> update(
            @PathVariable Long id,
            @Valid @RequestBody CategoryUpdateRequest request) {
        CategoryResponse data = categoryService.update(id, request);
        return ResponseEntity.ok(new RequestResponse(data, "Cập nhật category thành công"));
    }

    /** DELETE /api/categories/{id} */
    @DeleteMapping("/{id}")
    public ResponseEntity<RequestResponse> delete(@PathVariable Long id) {
        categoryService.delete(id);
        return ResponseEntity.ok(new RequestResponse("Xóa category thành công"));
    }
}

// FILE: ./src/main/java/com/ecms_web_application/OC01/controller/ChatController.java
// ============================================================
package com.ecms_web_application.OC01.controller;

import com.ecms_web_application.OC01.dto.request.chat.*;
import com.ecms_web_application.OC01.dto.response.RequestResponse;
import com.ecms_web_application.OC01.dto.response.chat.*;
import com.ecms_web_application.OC01.service.ChatService;
import jakarta.validation.Valid;
import lombok.RequiredArgsConstructor;
import org.springframework.data.domain.Page;
import org.springframework.http.HttpStatus;
import org.springframework.http.ResponseEntity;
import org.springframework.web.bind.annotation.*;
import java.time.LocalDateTime;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.Map;

@RestController
@RequestMapping("/api/chat")
@RequiredArgsConstructor
public class ChatController {

    private final ChatService chatService;

    @PostMapping("/conversations")
    public ResponseEntity<RequestResponse> create(@Valid @RequestBody CreateConversationRequest r) {
        return ResponseEntity.status(HttpStatus.CREATED)
                .body(new RequestResponse(chatService.createConversation(r), "Tao conversation thanh cong"));
    }

    @GetMapping("/conversations")
    public ResponseEntity<RequestResponse> list(
            @RequestParam(defaultValue = "0") int page,
            @RequestParam(defaultValue = "20") int size) {
        Page<ConversationResponse> d = chatService.getMyConversations(page, size);
        return ResponseEntity.ok(new RequestResponse(d, "OK"));
    }

    @GetMapping("/conversations/{id}")
    public ResponseEntity<RequestResponse> get(@PathVariable Long id) {
        return ResponseEntity.ok(new RequestResponse(chatService.getConversationById(id), "OK"));
    }

    @PatchMapping("/conversations/{id}/name")
    public ResponseEntity<RequestResponse> rename(@PathVariable Long id, @RequestBody Map<String,String> b) {
        chatService.updateConversationName(id, b.get("name"));
        return ResponseEntity.ok(new RequestResponse("OK"));
    }

    @PatchMapping("/conversations/{id}/permission")
    public ResponseEntity<RequestResponse> perm(@PathVariable Long id, @RequestBody Map<String,String> b) {
        chatService.updateMessagePermission(id, b.get("permission"));
        return ResponseEntity.ok(new RequestResponse("OK"));
    }

    @GetMapping("/conversations/{id}/participants")
    public ResponseEntity<RequestResponse> participants(@PathVariable Long id) {
        List<ParticipantResponse> d = chatService.getParticipants(id);
        return ResponseEntity.ok(new RequestResponse(d, "OK"));
    }

    @PostMapping("/conversations/{id}/participants")
    public ResponseEntity<RequestResponse> addParts(@PathVariable Long id,
            @Valid @RequestBody AddParticipantRequest r) {
        return ResponseEntity.ok(new RequestResponse(chatService.addParticipants(id, r), "OK"));
    }

    @DeleteMapping("/conversations/{id}/participants/{accountId}")
    public ResponseEntity<RequestResponse> removePart(@PathVariable Long id, @PathVariable Long accountId) {
        chatService.removeParticipant(id, accountId);
        return ResponseEntity.ok(new RequestResponse("OK"));
    }

    @PatchMapping("/conversations/{id}/participants/{accountId}/role")
    public ResponseEntity<RequestResponse> setRole(@PathVariable Long id, @PathVariable Long accountId,
            @RequestBody Map<String,String> b) {
        chatService.updateParticipantRole(id, accountId, b.get("role"));
        return ResponseEntity.ok(new RequestResponse("OK"));
    }

    @PatchMapping("/conversations/{id}/participants/{accountId}/mute")
    public ResponseEntity<RequestResponse> mute(@PathVariable Long id, @PathVariable Long accountId,
            @RequestBody Map<String,Object> b) {
        LocalDateTime mu = b.get("mutedUntil") != null ? LocalDateTime.parse(b.get("mutedUntil").toString()) : null;
        String reason = b.get("reason") != null ? b.get("reason").toString() : null;
        chatService.muteParticipant(id, accountId, mu, reason);
        return ResponseEntity.ok(new RequestResponse("OK"));
    }

    @PatchMapping("/conversations/{id}/participants/{accountId}/unmute")
    public ResponseEntity<RequestResponse> unmute(@PathVariable Long id, @PathVariable Long accountId) {
        chatService.unmuteParticipant(id, accountId);
        return ResponseEntity.ok(new RequestResponse("OK"));
    }

    @PatchMapping("/conversations/{id}/participants/{accountId}/ban")
    public ResponseEntity<RequestResponse> ban(@PathVariable Long id, @PathVariable Long accountId,
            @RequestBody Map<String,String> b) {
        chatService.banParticipant(id, accountId, b.get("reason"));
        return ResponseEntity.ok(new RequestResponse("OK"));
    }

    @PatchMapping("/conversations/{id}/participants/{accountId}/unban")
    public ResponseEntity<RequestResponse> unban(@PathVariable Long id, @PathVariable Long accountId) {
        chatService.unbanParticipant(id, accountId);
        return ResponseEntity.ok(new RequestResponse("OK"));
    }

    @PatchMapping("/conversations/{id}/transfer-ownership")
    public ResponseEntity<RequestResponse> transfer(@PathVariable Long id, @RequestBody Map<String,Long> b) {
        chatService.transferOwnership(id, b.get("newOwnerAccountId"));
        return ResponseEntity.ok(new RequestResponse("OK"));
    }

    @PostMapping("/conversations/{id}/messages")
    public ResponseEntity<RequestResponse> send(@PathVariable Long id,
            @Valid @RequestBody SendMessageRequest r) {
        return ResponseEntity.status(HttpStatus.CREATED)
                .body(new RequestResponse(chatService.sendMessage(id, r), "OK"));
    }

    @GetMapping("/conversations/{id}/messages")
    public ResponseEntity<RequestResponse> messages(@PathVariable Long id,
            @RequestParam(defaultValue = "0") int page,
            @RequestParam(defaultValue = "50") int size) {
        List<MessageResponse> d = chatService.getMessages(id, page, size);
        return ResponseEntity.ok(new RequestResponse(d, "OK"));
    }

    @PatchMapping("/conversations/{id}/read")
    public ResponseEntity<RequestResponse> read(@PathVariable Long id,
            @RequestBody(required = false) Map<String,Long> b) {
        chatService.markAsRead(id, b != null ? b.get("lastReadMessageId") : null);
        return ResponseEntity.ok(new RequestResponse("OK"));
    }

    @PutMapping("/messages/{id}")
    public ResponseEntity<RequestResponse> edit(@PathVariable Long id, @RequestBody Map<String,String> b) {
        return ResponseEntity.ok(new RequestResponse(chatService.editMessage(id, b.get("content")), "OK"));
    }

    @DeleteMapping("/messages/{id}")
    public ResponseEntity<RequestResponse> del(@PathVariable Long id) {
        chatService.deleteMessage(id);
        return ResponseEntity.ok(new RequestResponse("OK"));
    }

    @PatchMapping("/conversations/{id}/pin")
    public ResponseEntity<RequestResponse> pin(@PathVariable Long id, @RequestParam boolean pinned) {
        chatService.pinConversation(id, pinned);
        return ResponseEntity.ok(new RequestResponse("OK"));
    }

    @PatchMapping("/conversations/{id}/mute-by-user")
    public ResponseEntity<RequestResponse> muteByUser(@PathVariable Long id, @RequestParam boolean muted) {
        chatService.muteConversationByUser(id, muted);
        return ResponseEntity.ok(new RequestResponse("OK"));
    }

    @PatchMapping("/conversations/{id}/archive")
    public ResponseEntity<RequestResponse> archive(@PathVariable Long id, @RequestParam boolean archived) {
        chatService.archiveConversation(id, archived);
        return ResponseEntity.ok(new RequestResponse("OK"));
    }

    @GetMapping("/users/search")
    public ResponseEntity<RequestResponse> search(@RequestParam String keyword) {
        List<UserSearchResponse> d = chatService.searchUsers(keyword);
        return ResponseEntity.ok(new RequestResponse(d, "OK"));
    }
}

package com.ecms_web_application.OC01.controller;

import com.ecms_web_application.OC01.dto.request.course.*;
import com.ecms_web_application.OC01.dto.response.RequestResponse;
import com.ecms_web_application.OC01.dto.response.course.CourseDetailResponse;
import com.ecms_web_application.OC01.dto.response.course.CourseResponse;
import com.ecms_web_application.OC01.service.CourseService;
import jakarta.validation.Valid;
import lombok.RequiredArgsConstructor;
import lombok.extern.slf4j.Slf4j;
import org.springframework.data.domain.Page;
import org.springframework.data.domain.PageRequest;
import org.springframework.data.domain.Pageable;
import org.springframework.data.domain.Sort;
import org.springframework.http.HttpStatus;
import org.springframework.http.ResponseEntity;
import org.springframework.web.bind.annotation.*;
import org.springframework.security.access.prepost.PreAuthorize;

@RestController
@RequestMapping("/api/courses")
@RequiredArgsConstructor
@Slf4j
public class CourseController {

    private final CourseService courseService;

    @GetMapping
    public ResponseEntity<RequestResponse> search(
            @RequestParam(required = false) String status,
            @RequestParam(required = false) String type,
            @RequestParam(required = false) Long teacherId,
            @RequestParam(required = false) Long categoryId,
            @RequestParam(required = false) String search,
            @RequestParam(defaultValue = "0") int page,
            @RequestParam(defaultValue = "20") int size,
            @RequestParam(defaultValue = "createdAt") String sortBy,
            @RequestParam(defaultValue = "desc") String sortDir) {

        Sort sort = "asc".equalsIgnoreCase(sortDir)
                ? Sort.by(sortBy).ascending() : Sort.by(sortBy).descending();
        Pageable pageable = PageRequest.of(page, size, sort);
        Page<CourseResponse> data = courseService.search(status, type, teacherId, categoryId, search, pageable);
        return ResponseEntity.ok(new RequestResponse(data, "Lấy danh sách khóa học thành công"));
    }

    @GetMapping("/{id}")
    public ResponseEntity<RequestResponse> getById(@PathVariable Long id) {
        CourseDetailResponse data = courseService.getById(id);
        return ResponseEntity.ok(new RequestResponse(data, "Lấy khóa học thành công"));
    }

    @PostMapping
    public ResponseEntity<RequestResponse> create(@Valid @RequestBody CourseCreateRequest request) {
        CourseResponse data = courseService.create(request);
        return ResponseEntity.status(HttpStatus.CREATED)
                .body(new RequestResponse(data, "Tạo khóa học thành công"));
    }

    @PutMapping("/{id}")
    public ResponseEntity<RequestResponse> update(
            @PathVariable Long id,
            @Valid @RequestBody CourseUpdateRequest request) {
        CourseResponse data = courseService.update(id, request);
        return ResponseEntity.ok(new RequestResponse(data, "Cập nhật thành công"));
    }

    @PutMapping("/{id}/offline-info")
    public ResponseEntity<RequestResponse> updateOfflineInfo(
            @PathVariable Long id,
            @Valid @RequestBody CourseOfflineInfoRequest request) {
        CourseResponse data = courseService.updateOfflineInfo(id, request);
        return ResponseEntity.ok(new RequestResponse(data, "Cập nhật thông tin lớp Offline thành công"));
    }

    @PostMapping("/{id}/submit-review")
    public ResponseEntity<RequestResponse> submitForReview(@PathVariable Long id) {
        CourseResponse data = courseService.submitForReview(id);
        return ResponseEntity.ok(new RequestResponse(data, "Đã gửi duyệt"));
    }

    @PostMapping("/{id}/review")
    public ResponseEntity<RequestResponse> review(
            @PathVariable Long id,
            @Valid @RequestBody CourseReviewRequest request) {
        CourseResponse data = courseService.review(id, request);
        String msg = Boolean.TRUE.equals(request.getApproved())
                ? "Đã phê duyệt khóa học" : "Đã từ chối khóa học";
        return ResponseEntity.ok(new RequestResponse(data, msg));
    }

    @PostMapping("/{id}/archive")
    public ResponseEntity<RequestResponse> archive(@PathVariable Long id) {
        CourseResponse data = courseService.archive(id);
        return ResponseEntity.ok(new RequestResponse(data, "Đã lưu trữ khóa học"));
    }

    @PostMapping("/{id}/cancel")
    public ResponseEntity<RequestResponse> cancel(
            @PathVariable Long id,
            @RequestParam String reason) {
        CourseResponse data = courseService.cancel(id, reason);
        return ResponseEntity.ok(new RequestResponse(data, "Đã hủy khóa học"));
    }

    @PostMapping("/{sourceId}/copy")
    public ResponseEntity<RequestResponse> copy(
            @PathVariable Long sourceId,
            @Valid @RequestBody CourseCopyRequest request) {
        CourseDetailResponse data = courseService.copy(sourceId, request);
        return ResponseEntity.status(HttpStatus.CREATED)
                .body(new RequestResponse(data, "Copy khóa học thành công"));
    }

    /** FR_CRS_009B: đổi GV chính của khóa. Chỉ ADMIN. */
    @PatchMapping("/{id}/teacher")
    @PreAuthorize("hasRole('ADMIN')")
    public ResponseEntity<RequestResponse> changeTeacher(
            @PathVariable Long id,
            @RequestParam Long teacherId) {
        CourseResponse data = courseService.changeTeacher(id, teacherId);
        return ResponseEntity.ok(new RequestResponse(data, "Đã đổi GV chính của khóa"));
    }

    @DeleteMapping("/{id}")
    public ResponseEntity<RequestResponse> softDelete(@PathVariable Long id) {
        courseService.softDelete(id);
        return ResponseEntity.ok(new RequestResponse("Xóa mềm khóa học thành công"));
    }

    @DeleteMapping("/{id}/hard")
    public ResponseEntity<RequestResponse> hardDelete(@PathVariable Long id) {
        courseService.hardDelete(id);
        return ResponseEntity.ok(new RequestResponse("Xóa cứng khóa học thành công"));
    }
}

package com.ecms_web_application.OC01.controller;

import com.ecms_web_application.OC01.dto.request.course.ResourceCreateRequest;
import com.ecms_web_application.OC01.dto.request.course.ResourceUpdateRequest;
import com.ecms_web_application.OC01.dto.response.RequestResponse;
import com.ecms_web_application.OC01.dto.response.course.CourseResourceResponse;
import com.ecms_web_application.OC01.dto.response.course.ResourceTreeResponse;
import com.ecms_web_application.OC01.service.CourseResourceService;
import jakarta.validation.Valid;
import lombok.RequiredArgsConstructor;
import lombok.extern.slf4j.Slf4j;
import org.springframework.http.HttpStatus;
import org.springframework.http.ResponseEntity;
import org.springframework.web.bind.annotation.*;

import java.util.List;

@RestController
@RequestMapping("/api/courses/{courseId}/resources")
@RequiredArgsConstructor
@Slf4j
public class CourseResourceController {

    private final CourseResourceService resourceService;

    @GetMapping("/tree")
    public ResponseEntity<RequestResponse> getTree(@PathVariable Long courseId) {
        List<ResourceTreeResponse> data = resourceService.getTreeByCourse(courseId);
        return ResponseEntity.ok(new RequestResponse(data, "Lấy cây nội dung thành công"));
    }

    @GetMapping("/{id}")
    public ResponseEntity<RequestResponse> getById(
            @PathVariable Long courseId, @PathVariable Long id) {
        CourseResourceResponse data = resourceService.getById(id);
        return ResponseEntity.ok(new RequestResponse(data, "Lấy resource thành công"));
    }

    @PostMapping
    public ResponseEntity<RequestResponse> create(
            @PathVariable Long courseId,
            @Valid @RequestBody ResourceCreateRequest request) {
        CourseResourceResponse data = resourceService.create(courseId, request);
        return ResponseEntity.status(HttpStatus.CREATED)
                .body(new RequestResponse(data, "Tạo resource thành công"));
    }

    @PutMapping("/{id}")
    public ResponseEntity<RequestResponse> update(
            @PathVariable Long courseId,
            @PathVariable Long id,
            @Valid @RequestBody ResourceUpdateRequest request) {
        CourseResourceResponse data = resourceService.update(id, request);
        return ResponseEntity.ok(new RequestResponse(data, "Cập nhật resource thành công"));
    }

    @DeleteMapping("/{id}")
    public ResponseEntity<RequestResponse> delete(
            @PathVariable Long courseId, @PathVariable Long id) {
        resourceService.delete(id);
        return ResponseEntity.ok(new RequestResponse("Xóa resource thành công"));
    }
    @PutMapping("/reorder")
    public ResponseEntity<RequestResponse> reorder(
            @PathVariable Long courseId,
            @jakarta.validation.Valid @RequestBody com.ecms_web_application.OC01.dto.request.course.ResourceReorderRequest request) {
        resourceService.reorder(courseId, request);
        return ResponseEntity.ok(new RequestResponse("Cập nhật thứ tự thành công"));
    }
}

// FILE: ./src/main/java/com/ecms_web_application/OC01/controller/FileController.java
// ============================================================
package com.ecms_web_application.OC01.controller;

import com.ecms_web_application.OC01.dto.response.RequestResponse;
import com.ecms_web_application.OC01.exception.ErrorHandler;
import com.ecms_web_application.OC01.generic.FileStorageService;
import lombok.RequiredArgsConstructor;
import lombok.extern.slf4j.Slf4j;
import org.springframework.http.HttpStatus;
import org.springframework.http.ResponseEntity;
import org.springframework.web.bind.annotation.PostMapping;
import org.springframework.web.bind.annotation.RequestMapping;
import org.springframework.web.bind.annotation.RequestParam;
import org.springframework.web.bind.annotation.RestController;
import org.springframework.web.multipart.MultipartFile;

import java.io.IOException;
import java.util.Map;

@RestController
@RequestMapping("/api/files")
@RequiredArgsConstructor
@Slf4j
public class FileController {

    private final FileStorageService fileStorageService;

    /**
     * POST /api/files/upload
     * Form-data: file (MultipartFile)
     * Query: ?folder=avatars (tuỳ chọn)
     */
    @PostMapping("/upload")
    public ResponseEntity<RequestResponse> upload(
            @RequestParam("file") MultipartFile file,
            @RequestParam(value = "folder", required = false) String folder) {

        // 1. Validate file cơ bản
        if (file == null || file.isEmpty()) {
            throw new ErrorHandler(HttpStatus.BAD_REQUEST, "File không được để trống");
        }

        // 2. Giới hạn dung lượng (5MB)
        long MAX_SIZE = 5 * 1024 * 1024;
        if (file.getSize() > MAX_SIZE) {
            throw new ErrorHandler(HttpStatus.BAD_REQUEST,
                    "File không được vượt quá 5MB");
        }

        // 3. Chỉ cho phép ảnh
        String contentType = file.getContentType();
        if (contentType == null || !contentType.startsWith("image/")) {
            throw new ErrorHandler(HttpStatus.BAD_REQUEST,
                    "Chỉ chấp nhận file ảnh");
        }

        try {
            // 4. Lưu file, trả path có prefix /uploads/
            String url = fileStorageService.storeFileWithPrefix(file, folder);

            return ResponseEntity.ok(
                    new RequestResponse(Map.of("url", url), "Upload thành công"));
        } catch (IOException e) {
            log.error("Upload file thất bại", e);
            throw new ErrorHandler(HttpStatus.INTERNAL_SERVER_ERROR,
                    "Upload file thất bại");
        }
    }
}

// FILE: ./src/main/java/com/ecms_web_application/OC01/controller/InstructorSpecializationController.java

package com.ecms_web_application.OC01.controller;

import com.ecms_web_application.OC01.config.SecurityUtils;
import com.ecms_web_application.OC01.dto.request.SpecializationAddRequest;
import com.ecms_web_application.OC01.dto.response.InstructorSpecializationResponse;
import com.ecms_web_application.OC01.dto.response.RequestResponse;
import com.ecms_web_application.OC01.service.InstructorSpecializationService;
import jakarta.validation.Valid;
import lombok.RequiredArgsConstructor;
import lombok.extern.slf4j.Slf4j;
import org.springframework.http.HttpStatus;
import org.springframework.http.ResponseEntity;
import org.springframework.web.bind.annotation.*;

import java.util.List;

/**
 * Instructor Specialization API — /api/instructors
 *
 * Self (USER):
 *  - GET    /me/specializations
 *  - POST   /me/specializations
 *  - DELETE /me/specializations/{categoryId}
 *  - PATCH  /me/specializations/{categoryId}/primary
 *  - DELETE /me/specializations/primary
 *  - DELETE /me/specializations
 *
 * Admin (PROTECTED):
 *  - GET    /{accountId}/specializations
 *  - POST   /{accountId}/specializations
 *  - DELETE /{accountId}/specializations/{categoryId}
 *  - GET    /{accountId}/specializations/primary
 *  - GET    /category/{categoryId}/specializations
 */
@RestController
@RequestMapping("/api/instructors")
@RequiredArgsConstructor
@Slf4j
public class InstructorSpecializationController {

    private final InstructorSpecializationService specializationService;

    // ============================================================
    // SELF (/me/specializations)
    // ============================================================

    /** GET /api/instructors/me/specializations */
    @GetMapping("/me/specializations")
    public ResponseEntity<RequestResponse> getMySpecializations() {
        Long accountId = SecurityUtils.requireCurrentAccountId();
        List<InstructorSpecializationResponse> data = specializationService.getByAccount(accountId);
        return ResponseEntity.ok(new RequestResponse(data, "Lấy chuyên môn thành công"));
    }

    /** POST /api/instructors/me/specializations */
    @PostMapping("/me/specializations")
    public ResponseEntity<RequestResponse> addMySpecialization(
            @Valid @RequestBody SpecializationAddRequest request) {
        Long accountId = SecurityUtils.requireCurrentAccountId();
        InstructorSpecializationResponse data = specializationService.add(accountId, request);
        return ResponseEntity.status(HttpStatus.CREATED)
                .body(new RequestResponse(data, "Thêm chuyên môn thành công"));
    }

    /** DELETE /api/instructors/me/specializations/{categoryId} */
    @DeleteMapping("/me/specializations/{categoryId}")
    public ResponseEntity<RequestResponse> removeMySpecialization(@PathVariable Long categoryId) {
        Long accountId = SecurityUtils.requireCurrentAccountId();
        specializationService.remove(accountId, categoryId);
        return ResponseEntity.ok(new RequestResponse("Xóa chuyên môn thành công"));
    }

    /** PATCH /api/instructors/me/specializations/{categoryId}/primary */
    @PatchMapping("/me/specializations/{categoryId}/primary")
    public ResponseEntity<RequestResponse> setMyPrimary(@PathVariable Long categoryId) {
        Long accountId = SecurityUtils.requireCurrentAccountId();
        specializationService.setPrimary(accountId, categoryId);
        return ResponseEntity.ok(new RequestResponse("Đặt chuyên môn chính thành công"));
    }

    /** DELETE /api/instructors/me/specializations/primary */
    @DeleteMapping("/me/specializations/primary")
    public ResponseEntity<RequestResponse> clearMyPrimary() {
        Long accountId = SecurityUtils.requireCurrentAccountId();
        specializationService.clearPrimary(accountId);
        return ResponseEntity.ok(new RequestResponse("Bỏ chuyên môn chính thành công"));
    }

    /** DELETE /api/instructors/me/specializations */
    @DeleteMapping("/me/specializations")
    public ResponseEntity<RequestResponse> removeAllMy() {
        Long accountId = SecurityUtils.requireCurrentAccountId();
        specializationService.removeAll(accountId);
        return ResponseEntity.ok(new RequestResponse("Xóa tất cả chuyên môn thành công"));
    }

    // ============================================================
    // ADMIN — theo accountId
    // ============================================================

    /** GET /api/instructors/{accountId}/specializations */
    @GetMapping("/{accountId}/specializations")
    public ResponseEntity<RequestResponse> getByAccount(@PathVariable Long accountId) {
        List<InstructorSpecializationResponse> data = specializationService.getByAccount(accountId);
        return ResponseEntity.ok(new RequestResponse(data, "Lấy chuyên môn thành công"));
    }

    /** POST /api/instructors/{accountId}/specializations */
    @PostMapping("/{accountId}/specializations")
    public ResponseEntity<RequestResponse> addForAccount(
            @PathVariable Long accountId,
            @Valid @RequestBody SpecializationAddRequest request) {
        InstructorSpecializationResponse data = specializationService.add(accountId, request);
        return ResponseEntity.status(HttpStatus.CREATED)
                .body(new RequestResponse(data, "Thêm chuyên môn thành công"));
    }

    /** DELETE /api/instructors/{accountId}/specializations/{categoryId} */
    @DeleteMapping("/{accountId}/specializations/{categoryId}")
    public ResponseEntity<RequestResponse> removeForAccount(
            @PathVariable Long accountId,
            @PathVariable Long categoryId) {
        specializationService.remove(accountId, categoryId);
        return ResponseEntity.ok(new RequestResponse("Xóa chuyên môn thành công"));
    }

    /** GET /api/instructors/{accountId}/specializations/primary */
    @GetMapping("/{accountId}/specializations/primary")
    public ResponseEntity<RequestResponse> getPrimaryOfAccount(@PathVariable Long accountId) {
        List<InstructorSpecializationResponse> data = specializationService.getPrimaryOfAccount(accountId);
        return ResponseEntity.ok(new RequestResponse(data, "Lấy chuyên môn chính thành công"));
    }

    // ============================================================
    // ADMIN — theo categoryId
    // ============================================================

    /** GET /api/instructors/category/{categoryId}/specializations */
    @GetMapping("/category/{categoryId}/specializations")
    public ResponseEntity<RequestResponse> getByCategory(@PathVariable Long categoryId) {
        List<InstructorSpecializationResponse> data = specializationService.getByCategory(categoryId);
        return ResponseEntity.ok(new RequestResponse(data, "Lấy danh sách giảng viên thành công"));
    }
}

package com.ecms_web_application.OC01.controller;

import com.ecms_web_application.OC01.dto.request.LeadAssignRequest;
import com.ecms_web_application.OC01.dto.request.LeadCreateRequest;
import com.ecms_web_application.OC01.dto.request.LeadInteractionCreateRequest;
import com.ecms_web_application.OC01.dto.request.LeadPlacementTestCreateRequest;
import com.ecms_web_application.OC01.dto.request.LeadUpdateRequest;
import com.ecms_web_application.OC01.dto.response.LeadDetailResponse;
import com.ecms_web_application.OC01.dto.response.LeadInteractionResponse;
import com.ecms_web_application.OC01.dto.response.LeadPlacementTestResponse;
import com.ecms_web_application.OC01.dto.response.LeadResponse;
import com.ecms_web_application.OC01.dto.response.RequestResponse;
import com.ecms_web_application.OC01.service.LeadService;
import jakarta.validation.Valid;
import lombok.RequiredArgsConstructor;
import lombok.extern.slf4j.Slf4j;
import org.springframework.data.domain.Page;
import org.springframework.data.domain.PageRequest;
import org.springframework.data.domain.Pageable;
import org.springframework.data.domain.Sort;
import org.springframework.http.HttpStatus;
import org.springframework.http.ResponseEntity;
import org.springframework.security.access.prepost.PreAuthorize;
import org.springframework.web.bind.annotation.*;

import java.util.List;

@RestController
@RequestMapping("/api/leads")
@RequiredArgsConstructor
@Slf4j
public class LeadController {

    private final LeadService leadService;

    /**
     * POST /api/leads — Tạo mới Lead
     */
    @PostMapping
    @PreAuthorize("hasAnyRole('ADMIN', 'INSTRUCTOR')")
    public ResponseEntity<RequestResponse> create(@Valid @RequestBody LeadCreateRequest request) {
        LeadResponse data = leadService.create(request);
        return ResponseEntity.status(HttpStatus.CREATED)
                .body(new RequestResponse(data, "Tạo Lead thành công"));
    }

    /**
     * GET /api/leads — Lấy danh sách Lead (phân trang + lọc theo status, courseId, sourceId, consultant, inQueue, tìm kiếm)
     */
    @GetMapping
    @PreAuthorize("hasAnyRole('ADMIN', 'INSTRUCTOR')")
    public ResponseEntity<RequestResponse> getAll(
            @RequestParam(required = false) String search,
            @RequestParam(required = false) String status,
            @RequestParam(required = false) Long courseId,
            @RequestParam(required = false) Long sourceId,
            @RequestParam(required = false) Long consultantId,
            @RequestParam(required = false) Boolean inQueue,
            @RequestParam(defaultValue = "0") int page,
            @RequestParam(defaultValue = "20") int size,
            @RequestParam(defaultValue = "createdAt") String sortBy,
            @RequestParam(defaultValue = "desc") String direction
    ) {
        Sort sort = "asc".equalsIgnoreCase(direction)
                ? Sort.by(sortBy).ascending()
                : Sort.by(sortBy).descending();
        Pageable pageable = PageRequest.of(page, size, sort);

        Page<LeadResponse> data = leadService.search(search, status, courseId, sourceId, consultantId, inQueue, pageable);
        return ResponseEntity.ok(new RequestResponse(data, "Lấy danh sách Lead thành công"));
    }

    /**
     * GET /api/leads/{id} — Lấy thông tin cơ bản Lead theo ID
     */
    @GetMapping("/{id}")
    @PreAuthorize("hasAnyRole('ADMIN', 'INSTRUCTOR')")
    public ResponseEntity<RequestResponse> getById(@PathVariable Long id) {
        LeadResponse data = leadService.getById(id);
        return ResponseEntity.ok(new RequestResponse(data, "Lấy thông tin Lead thành công"));
    }

    /**
     * GET /api/leads/{id}/detail — Lấy thông tin chi tiết Lead kèm tương tác, test và lịch sử
     */
    @GetMapping("/{id}/detail")
    @PreAuthorize("hasAnyRole('ADMIN', 'INSTRUCTOR')")
    public ResponseEntity<RequestResponse> getDetailById(@PathVariable Long id) {
        LeadDetailResponse data = leadService.getDetailById(id);
        return ResponseEntity.ok(new RequestResponse(data, "Lấy chi tiết Lead thành công"));
    }

    /**
     * PUT /api/leads/{id} — Cập nhật thông tin Lead
     */
    @PutMapping("/{id}")
    @PreAuthorize("hasAnyRole('ADMIN', 'INSTRUCTOR')")
    public ResponseEntity<RequestResponse> update(
            @PathVariable Long id,
            @Valid @RequestBody LeadUpdateRequest request
    ) {
        LeadResponse data = leadService.update(id, request);
        return ResponseEntity.ok(new RequestResponse(data, "Cập nhật Lead thành công"));
    }

    /**
     * PUT /api/leads/{id}/assign — Phân công / chuyển người phụ trách Lead
     */
    @PutMapping("/{id}/assign")
    @PreAuthorize("hasAnyRole('ADMIN', 'INSTRUCTOR')")
    public ResponseEntity<RequestResponse> assignConsultant(
            @PathVariable Long id,
            @Valid @RequestBody LeadAssignRequest request
    ) {
        LeadResponse data = leadService.assignConsultant(id, request);
        return ResponseEntity.ok(new RequestResponse(data, "Phân công tư vấn viên thành công"));
    }

    /**
     * POST /api/leads/{id}/interactions — Ghi nhận tương tác tư vấn
     */
    @PostMapping("/{id}/interactions")
    @PreAuthorize("hasAnyRole('ADMIN', 'INSTRUCTOR')")
    public ResponseEntity<RequestResponse> logInteraction(
            @PathVariable Long id,
            @Valid @RequestBody LeadInteractionCreateRequest request
    ) {
        LeadInteractionResponse data = leadService.logInteraction(id, request);
        return ResponseEntity.status(HttpStatus.CREATED)
                .body(new RequestResponse(data, "Ghi nhận tương tác tư vấn thành công"));
    }

    /**
     * GET /api/leads/{id}/interactions — Lấy lịch sử tương tác của Lead
     */
    @GetMapping("/{id}/interactions")
    @PreAuthorize("hasAnyRole('ADMIN', 'INSTRUCTOR')")
    public ResponseEntity<RequestResponse> getInteractions(@PathVariable Long id) {
        List<LeadInteractionResponse> data = leadService.getInteractions(id);
        return ResponseEntity.ok(new RequestResponse(data, "Lấy lịch sử tương tác thành công"));
    }

    /**
     * POST /api/leads/{id}/placement-tests — Xếp lịch hoặc ghi nhận kết quả đánh giá đầu vào
     */
    @PostMapping("/{id}/placement-tests")
    @PreAuthorize("hasAnyRole('ADMIN', 'INSTRUCTOR')")
    public ResponseEntity<RequestResponse> recordPlacementTest(
            @PathVariable Long id,
            @Valid @RequestBody LeadPlacementTestCreateRequest request
    ) {
        LeadPlacementTestResponse data = leadService.recordPlacementTest(id, request);
        return ResponseEntity.status(HttpStatus.CREATED)
                .body(new RequestResponse(data, "Ghi nhận đánh giá đầu vào thành công"));
    }

    /**
     * GET /api/leads/{id}/placement-tests — Lấy danh sách đánh giá đầu vào của Lead
     */
    @GetMapping("/{id}/placement-tests")
    @PreAuthorize("hasAnyRole('ADMIN', 'INSTRUCTOR')")
    public ResponseEntity<RequestResponse> getPlacementTests(@PathVariable Long id) {
        List<LeadPlacementTestResponse> data = leadService.getPlacementTests(id);
        return ResponseEntity.ok(new RequestResponse(data, "Lấy danh sách đánh giá đầu vào thành công"));
    }

    /**
     * DELETE /api/leads/{id} — Kết thúc / đóng tư vấn Lead
     */
    @DeleteMapping("/{id}")
    @PreAuthorize("hasRole('ADMIN')")
    public ResponseEntity<RequestResponse> delete(@PathVariable Long id) {
        leadService.delete(id);
        return ResponseEntity.ok(new RequestResponse("Đóng tư vấn Lead thành công"));
    }
}

package com.ecms_web_application.OC01.controller;

import com.ecms_web_application.OC01.dto.request.NotificationRequest;
import com.ecms_web_application.OC01.dto.request.NotificationSearchRequest;
import com.ecms_web_application.OC01.dto.response.NotificationResponse;
import com.ecms_web_application.OC01.dto.response.RequestResponse;
import com.ecms_web_application.OC01.service.NotificationService;
import jakarta.validation.Valid;
import lombok.RequiredArgsConstructor;
import org.springframework.data.domain.Page;
import org.springframework.http.HttpStatus;
import org.springframework.http.ResponseEntity;
import org.springframework.security.access.prepost.PreAuthorize;
import org.springframework.web.bind.annotation.*;

@RestController
@RequestMapping("/api/notifications")
@RequiredArgsConstructor
public class NotificationController {

    private final NotificationService notificationService;

    @GetMapping
    public ResponseEntity<RequestResponse> inbox(NotificationSearchRequest req) {
        Page<NotificationResponse> d = notificationService.getMyInbox(req);
        return ResponseEntity.ok(new RequestResponse(d, "OK"));
    }

    @GetMapping("/{id}")
    public ResponseEntity<RequestResponse> get(@PathVariable Long id) {
        return ResponseEntity.ok(new RequestResponse(notificationService.getNotificationDetail(id), "OK"));
    }

    @GetMapping("/unread-count")
    public ResponseEntity<RequestResponse> unread() {
        return ResponseEntity.ok(new RequestResponse(notificationService.getUnreadCount(), "OK"));
    }

    @PatchMapping("/{id}/read")
    public ResponseEntity<RequestResponse> markRead(@PathVariable Long id) {
        notificationService.markAsRead(id);
        return ResponseEntity.ok(new RequestResponse("OK"));
    }

    @PatchMapping("/read-all")
    public ResponseEntity<RequestResponse> markAll() {
        notificationService.markAllAsRead();
        return ResponseEntity.ok(new RequestResponse("OK"));
    }

    @DeleteMapping("/{id}")
    public ResponseEntity<RequestResponse> del(@PathVariable Long id) {
        notificationService.deleteNotification(id);
        return ResponseEntity.ok(new RequestResponse("OK"));
    }

    @DeleteMapping
    public ResponseEntity<RequestResponse> delAll() {
        notificationService.deleteAllNotifications();
        return ResponseEntity.ok(new RequestResponse("OK"));
    }

    @PostMapping("/send")
    @PreAuthorize("hasRole('ADMIN')")
    public ResponseEntity<RequestResponse> send(@Valid @RequestBody NotificationRequest req) {
        notificationService.sendNotification(req);
        return ResponseEntity.status(HttpStatus.CREATED).body(new RequestResponse("OK"));
    }
}

package com.ecms_web_application.OC01.controller;

import com.ecms_web_application.OC01.dto.response.RequestResponse;
import com.ecms_web_application.OC01.dto.response.course.ProgressResponse;
import com.ecms_web_application.OC01.service.ProgressService;
import lombok.RequiredArgsConstructor;
import lombok.extern.slf4j.Slf4j;
import org.springframework.data.domain.Page;
import org.springframework.data.domain.PageRequest;
import org.springframework.data.domain.Pageable;
import org.springframework.data.domain.Sort;
import org.springframework.http.ResponseEntity;
import org.springframework.web.bind.annotation.*;

import java.util.List;

@RestController
@RequestMapping("/api/progress")
@RequiredArgsConstructor
@Slf4j
public class ProgressController {

    private final ProgressService progressService;

    @GetMapping("/my-courses")
    public ResponseEntity<RequestResponse> getMyCourses(
            @RequestParam(defaultValue = "0") int page,
            @RequestParam(defaultValue = "20") int size) {
        Pageable pageable = PageRequest.of(page, size,
                Sort.by(Sort.Direction.DESC, "lastAccessedAt"));
        Page<ProgressResponse> data = progressService.getMyCourses(pageable);
        return ResponseEntity.ok(new RequestResponse(data, "Lấy tiến độ các khóa thành công"));
    }

    @GetMapping("/courses/{courseId}")
    public ResponseEntity<RequestResponse> getMyProgressInCourse(@PathVariable Long courseId) {
        ProgressResponse data = progressService.getMyProgressInCourse(courseId);
        return ResponseEntity.ok(new RequestResponse(data, "Lấy tiến độ khóa thành công"));
    }

    @GetMapping("/courses/{courseId}/resources")
    public ResponseEntity<RequestResponse> getResourceProgresses(@PathVariable Long courseId) {
        List<ProgressResponse> data = progressService.getResourceProgresses(courseId);
        return ResponseEntity.ok(new RequestResponse(data, "Lấy tiến độ resource thành công"));
    }

    @PatchMapping("/resources/{resourceId}")
    public ResponseEntity<RequestResponse> updateResourceProgress(
            @PathVariable Long resourceId,
            @RequestParam(required = false) Integer percentage,
            @RequestParam(required = false) Long timeSpentSeconds) {
        ProgressResponse data = progressService.updateResourceProgress(
                resourceId, percentage, timeSpentSeconds);
        return ResponseEntity.ok(new RequestResponse(data, "Cập nhật tiến độ thành công"));
    }

    @PostMapping("/resources/{resourceId}/complete")
    public ResponseEntity<RequestResponse> markCompleted(@PathVariable Long resourceId) {
        ProgressResponse data = progressService.markResourceCompleted(resourceId);
        return ResponseEntity.ok(new RequestResponse(data, "Đánh dấu hoàn thành thành công"));
    }
}

package com.ecms_web_application.OC01.controller;

import com.ecms_web_application.OC01.dto.request.course.QuestionCreateRequest;
import com.ecms_web_application.OC01.dto.response.RequestResponse;
import com.ecms_web_application.OC01.dto.response.course.QuestionResponse;
import com.ecms_web_application.OC01.service.QuestionService;
import jakarta.validation.Valid;
import lombok.RequiredArgsConstructor;
import lombok.extern.slf4j.Slf4j;
import org.springframework.data.domain.Page;
import org.springframework.data.domain.PageRequest;
import org.springframework.data.domain.Pageable;
import org.springframework.data.domain.Sort;
import org.springframework.http.HttpStatus;
import org.springframework.http.ResponseEntity;
import org.springframework.web.bind.annotation.*;

@RestController
@RequestMapping("/api/courses/{courseId}/questions")
@RequiredArgsConstructor
@Slf4j
public class QuestionController {

    private final QuestionService questionService;

    @GetMapping
    public ResponseEntity<RequestResponse> search(
            @PathVariable Long courseId,
            @RequestParam(required = false) Long lessonId,
            @RequestParam(required = false) String questionType,
            @RequestParam(required = false) String status,
            @RequestParam(required = false) String hashtag,
            @RequestParam(required = false) String search,
            @RequestParam(defaultValue = "0") int page,
            @RequestParam(defaultValue = "20") int size) {

        Pageable pageable = PageRequest.of(page, size, Sort.by("orderIndex").ascending());
        Page<QuestionResponse> data = questionService.search(
                courseId, lessonId, questionType, status, hashtag, search, pageable);
        return ResponseEntity.ok(new RequestResponse(data, "Lấy danh sách câu hỏi thành công"));
    }

    @GetMapping("/{id}")
    public ResponseEntity<RequestResponse> getById(
            @PathVariable Long courseId, @PathVariable Long id) {
        QuestionResponse data = questionService.getById(id);
        return ResponseEntity.ok(new RequestResponse(data, "Lấy câu hỏi thành công"));
    }

    @PostMapping
    public ResponseEntity<RequestResponse> create(
            @PathVariable Long courseId,
            @Valid @RequestBody QuestionCreateRequest request) {
        QuestionResponse data = questionService.create(courseId, request);
        return ResponseEntity.status(HttpStatus.CREATED)
                .body(new RequestResponse(data, "Tạo câu hỏi thành công"));
    }

    @PutMapping("/{id}")
    public ResponseEntity<RequestResponse> update(
            @PathVariable Long courseId,
            @PathVariable Long id,
            @Valid @RequestBody QuestionCreateRequest request) {
        QuestionResponse data = questionService.update(id, request);
        return ResponseEntity.ok(new RequestResponse(data, "Cập nhật câu hỏi thành công"));
    }

    @DeleteMapping("/{id}")
    public ResponseEntity<RequestResponse> delete(
            @PathVariable Long courseId, @PathVariable Long id) {
        questionService.delete(id);
        return ResponseEntity.ok(new RequestResponse("Xóa câu hỏi thành công"));
    }
}
