package com.ecms_web_application.OC01.entity;

import com.ecms_web_application.OC01.entity.enums.ChatStatusEnum;
import com.ecms_web_application.OC01.entity.enums.ParticipantRoleEnum;
import jakarta.persistence.*;
import lombok.Getter;
import lombok.Setter;
import org.hibernate.annotations.JdbcTypeCode;
import org.hibernate.type.SqlTypes;

import java.time.LocalDateTime;

@Setter
@Getter
@Entity
@Table(name = "chat_participant",
       uniqueConstraints = @UniqueConstraint(
           name = "uk_participant_conv_acc",
           columnNames = {"conversation_id", "account_id"}))
public class ChatParticipant {

    @Id
    @GeneratedValue(strategy = GenerationType.IDENTITY)
    private Long id;

    @Column(name = "conversation_id", nullable = false)
    private Long conversationId;

    @Column(name = "account_id", nullable = false)
    private Long accountId;

    @Enumerated(EnumType.STRING)
    @JdbcTypeCode(SqlTypes.NAMED_ENUM)
    @Column(name = "role_in_conversation",
            columnDefinition = "participant_role_enum",
            nullable = false)
    private ParticipantRoleEnum roleInConversation = ParticipantRoleEnum.MEMBER;

    @Enumerated(EnumType.STRING)
    @JdbcTypeCode(SqlTypes.NAMED_ENUM)
    @Column(name = "chat_status",
            columnDefinition = "chat_status_enum",
            nullable = false)
    private ChatStatusEnum chatStatus = ChatStatusEnum.ACTIVE;

    @Column(name = "muted_until")
    private LocalDateTime mutedUntil;

    @Column(name = "muted_reason", length = 255)
    private String mutedReason;

    @Column(name = "ban_reason", length = 255)
    private String banReason;

    @Column(name = "is_admin", nullable = false)
    private Boolean isAdmin = false;

    @Column(name = "last_read_message_id")
    private Long lastReadMessageId;

    @Column(name = "unread_count", nullable = false)
    private Integer unreadCount = 0;

    @Column(name = "last_message_preview", length = 255)
    private String lastMessagePreview;

    @Column(name = "is_muted_by_user", nullable = false)
    private Boolean isMutedByUser = false;

    @Column(name = "is_pinned", nullable = false)
    private Boolean isPinned = false;

    @Column(name = "is_archived", nullable = false)
    private Boolean isArchived = false;

    @Column(name = "left_at")
    private LocalDateTime leftAt;

    @Column(name = "joined_at", updatable = false)
    private LocalDateTime joinedAt;

    @Column(name = "updated_at")
    private LocalDateTime updatedAt;

    @PrePersist
    void onCreate() {
        LocalDateTime now = LocalDateTime.now();
        if (joinedAt == null) joinedAt = now;
        updatedAt = now;
        if (roleInConversation == null) roleInConversation = ParticipantRoleEnum.MEMBER;
        if (chatStatus == null) chatStatus = ChatStatusEnum.ACTIVE;
        if (unreadCount == null) unreadCount = 0;
        if (isAdmin == null) isAdmin = false;
        if (isMutedByUser == null) isMutedByUser = false;
        if (isPinned == null) isPinned = false;
        if (isArchived == null) isArchived = false;
    }

    @PreUpdate
    void onUpdate() {
        updatedAt = LocalDateTime.now();
    }
}

// ============================================================
// FILE: ./src/main/java/com/ecms_web_application/OC01/entity/Course.java
// ============================================================
package com.ecms_web_application.OC01.entity;

import com.ecms_web_application.OC01.entity.enums.ClassStatus;
import com.ecms_web_application.OC01.entity.enums.CourseStatus;
import com.ecms_web_application.OC01.entity.enums.CourseType;
import com.ecms_web_application.OC01.entity.enums.ProgressType;
import jakarta.persistence.*;
import lombok.Getter;
import lombok.Setter;
import org.hibernate.annotations.JdbcTypeCode;
import org.hibernate.type.SqlTypes;
import java.math.BigDecimal;
import java.time.LocalDate;
import java.time.LocalDateTime;
import java.time.LocalTime;
import java.util.List;

@Setter @Getter
@Entity
@Table(name = "course",
    uniqueConstraints = {
        @UniqueConstraint(name = "uk_course_code",         columnNames = "code"),
        @UniqueConstraint(name = "uk_course_slug",         columnNames = "slug"),
        @UniqueConstraint(name = "uk_course_display_code", columnNames = "display_code")
    })
public class Course {

    @Id @GeneratedValue(strategy = GenerationType.IDENTITY)
    private Long id;

    @Column(nullable = false, length = 50) private String code;
    @Column(name = "display_code", length = 50) private String displayCode;
    @Column(nullable = false, length = 255) private String title;
    @Column(nullable = false, length = 255) private String slug;
    @Column(name = "short_description", length = 500) private String shortDescription;
    @Column(columnDefinition = "TEXT") private String description;
    @Column(name = "thumbnail_url", length = 500) private String thumbnailUrl;

    @Enumerated(EnumType.STRING)
    @Column(name = "course_type", length = 10, nullable = false)
    private CourseType courseType = CourseType.ONLINE;

    @ManyToOne(fetch = FetchType.LAZY)
    @JoinColumn(name = "category_id") private Category category;

    @JdbcTypeCode(SqlTypes.JSON)
    @Column(columnDefinition = "jsonb") private List<String> outcomes;

    @Column(nullable = false, precision = 10, scale = 2) private BigDecimal price = BigDecimal.ZERO;
    @Column(name = "old_price", precision = 10, scale = 2) private BigDecimal oldPrice;
    @Column(name = "is_free", nullable = false) private Boolean isFree = false;

    @Enumerated(EnumType.STRING)
    @Column(name = "progress_type", length = 30, nullable = false)
    private ProgressType progressType = ProgressType.COMPLETION_BASED;

    @Column(name = "min_students") private Integer minStudents;
    @Column(name = "max_students") private Integer maxStudents;
    @Column(name = "current_students", nullable = false) private Integer currentStudents = 0;
    @Column(name = "start_date") private LocalDate startDate;
    @Column(name = "end_date")   private LocalDate endDate;

    @ManyToOne(fetch = FetchType.LAZY)
    @JoinColumn(name = "teacher_id") private Account teacher;

    @ManyToOne(fetch = FetchType.LAZY)
    @JoinColumn(name = "room_id") private Room room;

    @JdbcTypeCode(SqlTypes.JSON)
    @Column(name = "schedule_days", columnDefinition = "jsonb")
    private List<Integer> scheduleDays;

    @Column(name = "session_start_time") private LocalTime sessionStartTime;
    @Column(name = "session_end_time")   private LocalTime sessionEndTime;

    @Enumerated(EnumType.STRING)
    @Column(name = "class_status", length = 20) private ClassStatus classStatus;

    @Enumerated(EnumType.STRING)
    @Column(nullable = false, length = 20)
    private CourseStatus status = CourseStatus.DRAFT;

    @ManyToOne(fetch = FetchType.LAZY, optional = false)
    @JoinColumn(name = "created_by", nullable = false) private Account createdBy;

    @Column(name = "copied_from_course_id") private Long copiedFromCourseId;
    @Column(name = "deleted_at") private LocalDateTime deletedAt;
    @Column(name = "created_at", updatable = false) private LocalDateTime createdAt;
    @Column(name = "updated_at") private LocalDateTime updatedAt;

    @Column(name = "learning_mode", length = 20) private String learningMode;

    @JdbcTypeCode(SqlTypes.JSON)
    @Column(name = "allowed_room_ids", columnDefinition = "jsonb") private List<Long> allowedRoomIds;

    @PrePersist void onCreate() {
        LocalDateTime now = LocalDateTime.now();
        if (createdAt == null) createdAt = now;
        if (updatedAt == null) updatedAt = now;
        if (price == null) price = BigDecimal.ZERO;
        if (isFree == null) isFree = false;
        if (currentStudents == null) currentStudents = 0;
        if (courseType == null) courseType = CourseType.ONLINE;
        if (progressType == null) progressType = ProgressType.COMPLETION_BASED;
        if (status == null) status = CourseStatus.DRAFT;
    }
    @PreUpdate void onUpdate() { updatedAt = LocalDateTime.now(); }
}

// ============================================================
// FILE: ./src/main/java/com/ecms_web_application/OC01/entity/CourseResource.java
// ============================================================
package com.ecms_web_application.OC01.entity;

import com.ecms_web_application.OC01.entity.enums.QuizCategory;
import com.ecms_web_application.OC01.entity.enums.ResourceStatus;
import com.ecms_web_application.OC01.entity.enums.ResourceType;
import jakarta.persistence.*;
import lombok.Getter;
import lombok.Setter;
import org.hibernate.annotations.JdbcTypeCode;
import org.hibernate.type.SqlTypes;
import java.math.BigDecimal;
import java.time.LocalDateTime;
import java.util.Map;

@Setter @Getter
@Entity
@Table(name = "resource",
    indexes = {
        @Index(name = "idx_resource_course",      columnList = "course_id"),
        @Index(name = "idx_resource_parent",      columnList = "parent_id"),
        @Index(name = "idx_resource_course_type", columnList = "course_id,resource_type")
    })
public class CourseResource {

    @Id @GeneratedValue(strategy = GenerationType.IDENTITY)
    private Long id;

    @ManyToOne(fetch = FetchType.LAZY, optional = false)
    @JoinColumn(name = "course_id", nullable = false) private Course course;

    @ManyToOne(fetch = FetchType.LAZY)
    @JoinColumn(name = "parent_id") private CourseResource parent;

    @Enumerated(EnumType.STRING)
    @Column(name = "resource_type", nullable = false, length = 20)
    private ResourceType resourceType;

    @Column(nullable = false, length = 255) private String title;
    @Column(columnDefinition = "TEXT") private String description;
    @Column(name = "order_index", nullable = false) private Integer orderIndex = 0;
    @Column(name = "is_required", nullable = false) private Boolean isRequired = false;
    @Column(name = "is_free_preview", nullable = false) private Boolean isFreePreview = false;
    @Column private Integer duration = 0;
    @Column(columnDefinition = "TEXT") private String content;
    @Column(name = "thumbnail_url", length = 500) private String thumbnailUrl;
    @Column(name = "file_name",  length = 255) private String fileName;
    @Column(name = "file_url",   length = 500) private String fileUrl;
    @Column(name = "file_size") private Long fileSize;
    @Column(name = "file_format", length = 50) private String fileFormat;
    @Column(name = "mime_type",   length = 100) private String mimeType;
    @Column(name = "max_attempts") private Integer maxAttempts;
    @Column(name = "passing_score", precision = 5, scale = 2) private BigDecimal passingScore;
    @Column(name = "time_limit") private Integer timeLimit;
    @Column(precision = 5, scale = 2) private BigDecimal points = BigDecimal.ZERO;
    @Column private LocalDateTime deadline;

    @JdbcTypeCode(SqlTypes.JSON)
    @Column(columnDefinition = "jsonb") private Map<String, Object> settings;

    @Enumerated(EnumType.STRING)
    @Column(nullable = false, length = 20)
    private ResourceStatus status = ResourceStatus.DRAFT;

    @ManyToOne(fetch = FetchType.LAZY)
    @JoinColumn(name = "created_by") private Account createdBy;

    @Column(name = "available_from")  private LocalDateTime availableFrom;
    @Column(name = "available_until") private LocalDateTime availableUntil;

    @Enumerated(EnumType.STRING)
    @Column(name = "quiz_category", length = 20) private QuizCategory quizCategory;

    @Column(name = "created_at", updatable = false) private LocalDateTime createdAt;
    @Column(name = "updated_at") private LocalDateTime updatedAt;

    @Column(name = "hashtag_filter", length = 500) private String hashtagFilter;
    @Column(name = "total_questions") private Integer totalQuestions;
    @Column(name = "shuffle_questions") private Boolean shuffleQuestions = false;

    @PrePersist void onCreate() {
        LocalDateTime now = LocalDateTime.now();
        if (createdAt == null) createdAt = now;
        if (updatedAt == null) updatedAt = now;
        if (orderIndex == null) orderIndex = 0;
        if (isRequired == null) isRequired = false;
        if (isFreePreview == null) isFreePreview = false;
        if (duration == null) duration = 0;
        if (points == null) points = BigDecimal.ZERO;
        if (status == null) status = ResourceStatus.DRAFT;
    }
    @PreUpdate void onUpdate() { updatedAt = LocalDateTime.now(); }
}

/**
 * Lý do revoke session.
 */

    PASSWORD_CHANGE,  // Đổi mật khẩu → revoke tất cả session cũ
    ADMIN_REVOKE,     // Admin kick user ra
    EXPIRED,          // Hết hạn tự nhiên (dọn dẹp)
    ROTATED           // Refresh token rotation — token cũ bị thay bằng token mới
}

// FILE: ./src/main/java/com/ecms_web_application/OC01/entity/Guardian.java
// ============================================================
package com.ecms_web_application.OC01.entity;

import jakarta.persistence.*;
import lombok.Getter;
import lombok.Setter;

/**
 * Entity đại diện cho người giám hộ của học viên (Guardians).
 * Bảng: guardians
 */
@Getter
@Setter
@Entity
@Table(name = "guardians")
public class Guardian {

    @Id
    @GeneratedValue(strategy = GenerationType.IDENTITY)
    @Column(name = "id")
    private Long id;

    @Column(name = "student_id", nullable = false)
    private Long studentId;

    @Column(name = "full_name", nullable = false, length = 255)
    private String fullName;

    @Column(name = "relationship", nullable = false, length = 100)
    private String relationship;

    @Column(name = "phone", nullable = false, length = 20)
    private String phone;

    @Column(name = "email", length = 255)
    private String email;
}


// ============================================================
// FILE: ./src/main/java/com/ecms_web_application/OC01/entity/InstructorSpecialization.java
// ============================================================
package com.ecms_web_application.OC01.entity;

import jakarta.persistence.*;
import lombok.Getter;
import lombok.Setter;

import java.time.LocalDateTime;

/**
 * Bảng trung gian many-to-many giữa Account (giảng viên) và Category (chuyên môn).
 *
 * Mỗi giảng viên có thể có nhiều chuyên môn, nhưng chỉ TỐI ĐA 1 chuyên môn chính
 * (đảm bảo bằng partial unique index ở DB).
 */
@Setter
@Getter
@Entity
@Table(name = "instructor_specialization")
@IdClass(InstructorSpecializationId.class)
public class InstructorSpecialization {

    @Id
    @ManyToOne(fetch = FetchType.LAZY)
    @JoinColumn(name = "account_id", nullable = false)
    private Account account;

    @Id
    @ManyToOne(fetch = FetchType.LAZY)
    @JoinColumn(name = "category_id", nullable = false)
    private Category category;

    @Column(name = "is_primary", nullable = false)
    private Boolean isPrimary = false;

    @Column(name = "years_of_exp")
    private Short yearsOfExp;

    @Column(name = "created_at", updatable = false)
    private LocalDateTime createdAt;

    // ===== Hooks =====
    @PrePersist
    void onCreate() {
        if (this.createdAt == null) this.createdAt = LocalDateTime.now();
        if (this.isPrimary == null) this.isPrimary = false;
    }
}

// FILE: ./src/main/java/com/ecms_web_application/OC01/entity/Lead.java
// ============================================================
package com.ecms_web_application.OC01.entity;

import jakarta.persistence.*;
import lombok.Getter;
import lombok.Setter;

import java.time.LocalDateTime;

/**
 * Entity đại diện cho Lead (Khách hàng tiềm năng).
 * Bảng: leads
 */
@Getter
@Setter
@Entity
@Table(name = "leads")
public class Lead {

    @Id
    @GeneratedValue(strategy = GenerationType.IDENTITY)
    @Column(name = "id")
    private Long id;

    @Column(name = "full_name", nullable = false, length = 255)
    private String fullName;

    @Column(name = "email", length = 255)
    private String email;

    @Column(name = "phone", length = 20)
    private String phone;

    @Column(name = "course_id")
    private Long courseId;

    @Column(name = "student_id")
    private Long studentId;

    @Column(name = "status", nullable = false, length = 30)
    private String status = "NEW";

    @Column(name = "converted_at")
    private LocalDateTime convertedAt;

    @Column(name = "source_id")
    private Long sourceId;

    @Column(name = "assigned_consultant_id")
    private Long assignedConsultantId;

    @Column(name = "note", columnDefinition = "TEXT")
    private String note;

    @Column(name = "created_at", nullable = false, updatable = false)
    private LocalDateTime createdAt;

    @Column(name = "updated_at", nullable = false)
    private LocalDateTime updatedAt;

    // ===== Hooks =====
    @PrePersist
    void onCreate() {
        LocalDateTime now = LocalDateTime.now();
        if (this.createdAt == null) this.createdAt = now;
        if (this.updatedAt == null) this.updatedAt = now;
        if (this.status == null || this.status.isBlank()) this.status = "NEW";
    }

    @PreUpdate
    void onUpdate() {
        this.updatedAt = LocalDateTime.now();
    }
}

    @Column(name = "id")
    private Long id;

    @Column(name = "lead_id", nullable = false)
    private Long leadId;

    @Column(name = "test_status", nullable = false, length = 50)
    private String testStatus = "SCHEDULED";

    @Column(name = "scheduled_at")
    private LocalDateTime scheduledAt;

    @Column(name = "test_score", precision = 5, scale = 2)
    private BigDecimal testScore;

    @Column(name = "recommended_level", length = 100)
    private String recommendedLevel;

    @Column(name = "notes", columnDefinition = "TEXT")
    private String notes;

    @Column(name = "evaluated_at")
    private LocalDateTime evaluatedAt;

    @Column(name = "created_at", nullable = false, updatable = false)
    private LocalDateTime createdAt;

    @PrePersist
    void onCreate() {
        if (this.createdAt == null) {
            this.createdAt = LocalDateTime.now();
        }
        if (this.testStatus == null || this.testStatus.isBlank()) {
            this.testStatus = "SCHEDULED";
        }
    }

package com.ecms_web_application.OC01.entity;

import com.ecms_web_application.OC01.entity.enums.NotificationPriorityEnum;
import jakarta.persistence.*;
import lombok.Getter;
import lombok.Setter;
import org.hibernate.annotations.JdbcTypeCode;
import org.hibernate.type.SqlTypes;

import java.time.LocalDateTime;
import java.util.Map;

@Setter
@Getter
@Entity
@Table(name = "notification")
public class Notification {

    @Id
    @GeneratedValue(strategy = GenerationType.IDENTITY)
    private Long id;

    @Column(name = "sender_account_id")
    private Long senderAccountId;

    @Column(name = "notification_type", length = 50)
    private String notificationType;

    @Column(name = "title", nullable = false, length = 255)
    private String title;

    @Column(name = "content", columnDefinition = "TEXT")
    private String content;

    @Column(name = "reference_type", length = 50)
    private String referenceType;

    @Column(name = "reference_id")
    private Long referenceId;

    @JdbcTypeCode(SqlTypes.JSON)
    @Column(name = "extra_data", columnDefinition = "jsonb")
    private Map<String, Object> extraData;

    @Enumerated(EnumType.STRING)
    @JdbcTypeCode(SqlTypes.NAMED_ENUM)
    @Column(name = "priority",
            columnDefinition = "notification_priority_enum",
            nullable = false)
    private NotificationPriorityEnum priority = NotificationPriorityEnum.NORMAL;

    @Column(name = "expires_at")
    private LocalDateTime expiresAt;

    @Column(name = "created_at", nullable = false, updatable = false)
    private LocalDateTime createdAt;

    @Column(name = "deleted_at")
    private LocalDateTime deletedAt;

    @PrePersist
    void onCreate() {
        if (createdAt == null) createdAt = LocalDateTime.now();
        if (priority == null) priority = NotificationPriorityEnum.NORMAL;
    }
}

// FILE: ./src/main/java/com/ecms_web_application/OC01/entity/NotificationDelivery.java
// ============================================================
package com.ecms_web_application.OC01.entity;

import com.ecms_web_application.OC01.entity.enums.DeliveryChannelEnum;
import com.ecms_web_application.OC01.entity.enums.DeliveryStatusEnum;
import jakarta.persistence.*;
import lombok.Getter;
import lombok.Setter;
import org.hibernate.annotations.JdbcTypeCode;
import org.hibernate.type.SqlTypes;

import java.time.LocalDateTime;
import java.util.Map;

@Setter
@Getter
@Entity
@Table(name = "notification_delivery",
       uniqueConstraints = @UniqueConstraint(
           name = "uk_recipient_channel",
           columnNames = {"recipient_id", "channel"}))
public class NotificationDelivery {

    @Id
    @GeneratedValue(strategy = GenerationType.IDENTITY)
    private Long id;

    @Column(name = "recipient_id", nullable = false)
    private Long recipientId;

    @Enumerated(EnumType.STRING)
    @JdbcTypeCode(SqlTypes.NAMED_ENUM)
    @Column(name = "channel",
            columnDefinition = "delivery_channel_enum",
            nullable = false)
    private DeliveryChannelEnum channel;

    @Enumerated(EnumType.STRING)
    @JdbcTypeCode(SqlTypes.NAMED_ENUM)
    @Column(name = "status",
            columnDefinition = "delivery_status_enum",
            nullable = false)
    private DeliveryStatusEnum status = DeliveryStatusEnum.PENDING;

    @Column(name = "attempt_count", nullable = false)
    private Integer attemptCount = 0;

    @Column(name = "sent_at")
    private LocalDateTime sentAt;

    @Column(name = "error_message", length = 500)
    private String errorMessage;

    @JdbcTypeCode(SqlTypes.JSON)
    @Column(name = "provider_response", columnDefinition = "jsonb")
    private Map<String, Object> providerResponse;

    @Column(name = "created_at", updatable = false)
    private LocalDateTime createdAt;

    @Column(name = "updated_at")
    private LocalDateTime updatedAt;

    @PrePersist
    void onCreate() {
        LocalDateTime now = LocalDateTime.now();
        if (createdAt == null) createdAt = now;
        updatedAt = now;
        if (status == null) status = DeliveryStatusEnum.PENDING;
        if (attemptCount == null) attemptCount = 0;
    }

    @PreUpdate
    void onUpdate() {
        updatedAt = LocalDateTime.now();
    }
}

// FILE: ./src/main/java/com/ecms_web_application/OC01/entity/NotificationRecipient.java
// ============================================================
package com.ecms_web_application.OC01.entity;

import com.ecms_web_application.OC01.entity.enums.EmailStatusEnum;
import com.ecms_web_application.OC01.entity.enums.PushStatusEnum;
import jakarta.persistence.*;
import lombok.Getter;
import lombok.Setter;
import org.hibernate.annotations.JdbcTypeCode;
import org.hibernate.type.SqlTypes;

import java.time.LocalDateTime;

@Setter
@Getter
@Entity
@Table(name = "notification_recipient",
       uniqueConstraints = @UniqueConstraint(
           name = "uk_notif_recipient",
           columnNames = {"notification_id", "account_id"}))
public class NotificationRecipient {

    @Id
    @GeneratedValue(strategy = GenerationType.IDENTITY)
    private Long id;

    @Column(name = "notification_id", nullable = false)
    private Long notificationId;

    @Column(name = "account_id", nullable = false)
    private Long accountId;

    @Column(name = "is_read", nullable = false)
    private Boolean isRead = false;

    @Column(name = "read_at")
    private LocalDateTime readAt;

    /** NULL = chưa xóa. Set NOW() = đã xóa (soft delete cá nhân). */
    @Column(name = "deleted_at")
    private LocalDateTime deletedAt;

    @Enumerated(EnumType.STRING)
    @JdbcTypeCode(SqlTypes.NAMED_ENUM)
    @Column(name = "email_status", columnDefinition = "email_status_enum")
    private EmailStatusEnum emailStatus;

    @Column(name = "email_sent_at")
    private LocalDateTime emailSentAt;

    @Column(name = "email_error", length = 500)
    private String emailError;

    @Column(name = "email_attempts", nullable = false)
    private Integer emailAttempts = 0;

    @Enumerated(EnumType.STRING)
    @JdbcTypeCode(SqlTypes.NAMED_ENUM)
    @Column(name = "push_status", columnDefinition = "push_status_enum")
    private PushStatusEnum pushStatus;

    @Column(name = "push_sent_at")
    private LocalDateTime pushSentAt;

    @Column(name = "push_attempts", nullable = false)
    private Integer pushAttempts = 0;

    @Column(name = "created_at", nullable = false, updatable = false)
    private LocalDateTime createdAt;

    /**
     * Read-only relation để JOIN FETCH khi query inbox.
     * insertable/updatable = false → Hibernate KHÔNG ghi qua relation này,
     * chỉ dùng để navigate khi đọc.
     */
    @ManyToOne(fetch = FetchType.LAZY)
    @JoinColumn(name = "notification_id", insertable = false, updatable = false)
    private Notification notification;

    @PrePersist
    void onCreate() {
        if (createdAt == null) createdAt = LocalDateTime.now();
        if (isRead == null) isRead = false;
        if (emailAttempts == null) emailAttempts = 0;
        if (pushAttempts == null) pushAttempts = 0;
    }

    public boolean isDeleted() {
        return deletedAt != null;
    }
}

package com.ecms_web_application.OC01.entity;

import jakarta.persistence.*;
import lombok.Getter;
import lombok.Setter;

import java.time.LocalDateTime;
import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.List;

@Setter
@Getter
@Entity
@Table(name = "permission")
public class Permission {

    @Id
    @GeneratedValue(strategy = GenerationType.IDENTITY)
    @Column(name = "id")
    private Long id;

    @Column(name = "method", nullable = false, length = 10)
    private String method;

    @Column(name = "endpoint_pattern", nullable = false, length = 255)
    private String endpointPattern;

    @Column(name = "module", length = 100)
    private String module;

    @Column(name = "description", length = 255)
    private String description;

    @Column(name = "created_at", nullable = false, updatable = false)
    private LocalDateTime createdAt;

    @Column(name = "updated_at", nullable = false)
    private LocalDateTime updatedAt;

    // ===== Relations =====
    @OneToMany(mappedBy = "permission", fetch = FetchType.LAZY)
    private List<AccountPermission> accountPermissions = new ArrayList<>();

    // ===== Hooks =====
    @PrePersist
    void onCreate() {
        LocalDateTime now = LocalDateTime.now();
        if (this.createdAt == null) this.createdAt = now;
        if (this.updatedAt == null) this.updatedAt = now;
    }

    @PreUpdate
    void onUpdate() {
        this.updatedAt = LocalDateTime.now();
    }
}

// FILE: ./src/main/java/com/ecms_web_application/OC01/entity/Progress.java
// ============================================================
package com.ecms_web_application.OC01.entity;

import com.ecms_web_application.OC01.entity.enums.ProgressScope;
import com.ecms_web_application.OC01.entity.enums.ProgressStatus;
import jakarta.persistence.*;
import lombok.Getter;
import lombok.Setter;
import org.hibernate.annotations.JdbcTypeCode;
import org.hibernate.type.SqlTypes;
import java.math.BigDecimal;
import java.time.LocalDateTime;
import java.util.Map;

@Setter @Getter
@Entity
@Table(name = "progress",
    uniqueConstraints = @UniqueConstraint(
        name = "uk_progress",
        columnNames = {"account_id", "course_id", "progress_type", "reference_id"}))
public class Progress {

    @Id @GeneratedValue(strategy = GenerationType.IDENTITY)
    private Long id;

    @ManyToOne(fetch = FetchType.LAZY, optional = false)
    @JoinColumn(name = "account_id", nullable = false) private Account account;

    @ManyToOne(fetch = FetchType.LAZY, optional = false)
    @JoinColumn(name = "course_id", nullable = false) private Course course;

    @Enumerated(EnumType.STRING)
    @Column(name = "progress_type", nullable = false, length = 50)
    private ProgressScope progressType;

    @Column(name = "reference_id") private Long referenceId;
    @Column(name = "reference_type", length = 50) private String referenceType;

    @Enumerated(EnumType.STRING)
    @Column(length = 50) private ProgressStatus status = ProgressStatus.NOT_STARTED;

    @Column(name = "progress_percentage", precision = 5, scale = 2)
    private BigDecimal progressPercentage = BigDecimal.ZERO;

    @Column(name = "total_time_spent") private Integer totalTimeSpent = 0;
    @Column(precision = 5, scale = 2) private BigDecimal score;
    @Column(name = "max_score", precision = 5, scale = 2) private BigDecimal maxScore;
    @Column private Integer attempts = 1;
    @Column(name = "is_passed") private Boolean isPassed;
    @Column(columnDefinition = "TEXT") private String notes;
    @Column(name = "started_at") private LocalDateTime startedAt;
    @Column(name = "completed_at") private LocalDateTime completedAt;
    @Column(name = "last_accessed_at") private LocalDateTime lastAccessedAt;
    @Column(name = "total_items") private Integer totalItems = 0;
    @Column(name = "completed_items") private Integer completedItems = 0;
    @Column(name = "weighted_score", precision = 5, scale = 2) private BigDecimal weightedScore;
    @Column(name = "total_weight_percent", precision = 5, scale = 2) private BigDecimal totalWeightPercent;

    @JdbcTypeCode(SqlTypes.JSON)
    @Column(name = "extra_data", columnDefinition = "jsonb") private Map<String, Object> extraData;

    @Column(name = "created_at", updatable = false) private LocalDateTime createdAt;
    @Column(name = "updated_at") private LocalDateTime updatedAt;

    @Column(name = "has_uncompleted_new_content", nullable = false)
    private Boolean hasUncompletedNewContent = false;

    @PrePersist void onCreate() {
        LocalDateTime now = LocalDateTime.now();
        if (createdAt == null) createdAt = now;
        if (updatedAt == null) updatedAt = now;
        if (progressPercentage == null) progressPercentage = BigDecimal.ZERO;
        if (totalTimeSpent == null) totalTimeSpent = 0;
        if (attempts == null) attempts = 1;
        if (totalItems == null) totalItems = 0;
        if (completedItems == null) completedItems = 0;
        if (status == null) status = ProgressStatus.NOT_STARTED;
    }
    @PreUpdate void onUpdate() { updatedAt = LocalDateTime.now(); }
}

package com.ecms_web_application.OC01.entity;

import com.ecms_web_application.OC01.entity.enums.QuestionStatus;
import com.ecms_web_application.OC01.entity.enums.QuestionType;
import jakarta.persistence.*;
import lombok.Getter;
import lombok.Setter;
import org.hibernate.annotations.JdbcTypeCode;
import org.hibernate.type.SqlTypes;
import java.math.BigDecimal;
import java.time.LocalDateTime;
import java.util.List;

@Setter @Getter
@Entity
@Table(name = "question",
    indexes = {
        @Index(name = "idx_question_course", columnList = "course_id"),
        @Index(name = "idx_question_lesson", columnList = "lesson_id")
    })
public class Question {

    @Id @GeneratedValue(strategy = GenerationType.IDENTITY)
    private Long id;

    @ManyToOne(fetch = FetchType.LAZY, optional = false)
    @JoinColumn(name = "course_id", nullable = false) private Course course;

    @ManyToOne(fetch = FetchType.LAZY)
    @JoinColumn(name = "lesson_id") private CourseResource lesson;

    @Column(length = 255) private String hashtag;
    @Column(columnDefinition = "TEXT", nullable = false) private String content;

    @Enumerated(EnumType.STRING)
    @Column(name = "question_type", nullable = false, length = 30)
    private QuestionType questionType;

    @JdbcTypeCode(SqlTypes.JSON)
    @Column(columnDefinition = "jsonb") private List<String> options;

    @JdbcTypeCode(SqlTypes.JSON)
    @Column(name = "correct_answer", columnDefinition = "jsonb") private List<String> correctAnswer;

    @Column(columnDefinition = "TEXT") private String explanation;
    @Column(nullable = false, precision = 5, scale = 2) private BigDecimal points = BigDecimal.ONE;
    @Column(name = "order_index", nullable = false) private Integer orderIndex = 0;

    @Enumerated(EnumType.STRING)
    @Column(nullable = false, length = 20)
    private QuestionStatus status = QuestionStatus.ACTIVE;

    @Column(name = "created_at", updatable = false) private LocalDateTime createdAt;
    @Column(name = "updated_at") private LocalDateTime updatedAt;

    @PrePersist void onCreate() {
        LocalDateTime now = LocalDateTime.now();
        if (createdAt == null) createdAt = now;
        if (updatedAt == null) updatedAt = now;
        if (points == null) points = BigDecimal.ONE;
        if (orderIndex == null) orderIndex = 0;
        if (status == null) status = QuestionStatus.ACTIVE;
    }
    @PreUpdate void onUpdate() { updatedAt = LocalDateTime.now(); }
}

// FILE: ./src/main/java/com/ecms_web_application/OC01/entity/QuizAttempt.java
// ============================================================
package com.ecms_web_application.OC01.entity;

import com.ecms_web_application.OC01.entity.enums.QuizAttemptStatus;
import jakarta.persistence.*;
import lombok.Getter;
import lombok.Setter;
import org.hibernate.annotations.JdbcTypeCode;
import org.hibernate.type.SqlTypes;
import java.math.BigDecimal;
import java.time.LocalDateTime;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.Map;

@Setter @Getter
@Entity
@Table(name = "quiz_attempt",
    uniqueConstraints = @UniqueConstraint(
        name = "uk_attempt_number",
        columnNames = {"account_id", "resource_id", "attempt_number"}))
public class QuizAttempt {

    @Id @GeneratedValue(strategy = GenerationType.IDENTITY)
    private Long id;

    @ManyToOne(fetch = FetchType.LAZY, optional = false)
    @JoinColumn(name = "account_id", nullable = false) private Account account;

    @ManyToOne(fetch = FetchType.LAZY, optional = false)
    @JoinColumn(name = "course_id", nullable = false) private Course course;

    @ManyToOne(fetch = FetchType.LAZY, optional = false)
    @JoinColumn(name = "resource_id", nullable = false) private CourseResource resource;

    @Column(name = "attempt_number", nullable = false) private Integer attemptNumber = 1;

    @JdbcTypeCode(SqlTypes.JSON)
    @Column(name = "question_ids", columnDefinition = "jsonb") private List<Long> questionIds;

    @JdbcTypeCode(SqlTypes.JSON)
    @Column(columnDefinition = "jsonb") private Map<String, Object> answers;

    @Column(precision = 5, scale = 2) private BigDecimal score;
    @Column(name = "max_score",     precision = 5, scale = 2) private BigDecimal maxScore;
    @Column(name = "passing_score", precision = 5, scale = 2) private BigDecimal passingScore;
    @Column(name = "is_passed") private Boolean isPassed;

    @Enumerated(EnumType.STRING)
    @Column(nullable = false, length = 20)
    private QuizAttemptStatus status = QuizAttemptStatus.IN_PROGRESS;

    @Column(name = "started_at", nullable = false) private LocalDateTime startedAt;
    @Column(name = "submitted_at") private LocalDateTime submittedAt;
    @Column(name = "time_spent")   private Integer timeSpent;
    @Column(name = "created_at", updatable = false) private LocalDateTime createdAt;
    @Column(name = "updated_at") private LocalDateTime updatedAt;

    @PrePersist void onCreate() {
        LocalDateTime now = LocalDateTime.now();
        if (startedAt == null) startedAt = now;
        if (createdAt == null) createdAt = now;
        if (updatedAt == null) updatedAt = now;
        if (status == null) status = QuizAttemptStatus.IN_PROGRESS;
        if (attemptNumber == null) attemptNumber = 1;
    }
    @PreUpdate void onUpdate() { updatedAt = LocalDateTime.now(); }
}

package com.ecms_web_application.OC01.entity;

import jakarta.persistence.*;
import lombok.Getter;
import lombok.Setter;

import java.time.LocalDateTime;
import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.List;

@Setter
@Getter
@Entity
@Table(name = "role")
public class Role {

    @Id
    @GeneratedValue(strategy = GenerationType.IDENTITY)
    @Column(name = "id")
    private Long id;

    @Column(name = "code", nullable = false, unique = true, length = 50)
    private String code;

    @Column(name = "description", length = 255)
    private String description;

    @Column(name = "created_at", updatable = false)
    private LocalDateTime createdAt;

    @Column(name = "updated_at")
    private LocalDateTime updatedAt;

    // ===== Relations =====
    @OneToMany(mappedBy = "role", fetch = FetchType.LAZY)
    private List<Account> accounts = new ArrayList<>();

    // ===== Hooks =====
    @PrePersist
    void onCreate() {
        LocalDateTime now = LocalDateTime.now();
        if (this.createdAt == null) this.createdAt = now;
        if (this.updatedAt == null) this.updatedAt = now;
    }

    @PreUpdate
    void onUpdate() {
        this.updatedAt = LocalDateTime.now();
    }
}

package com.ecms_web_application.OC01.entity;

import jakarta.persistence.*;
import lombok.Getter;
import lombok.Setter;
import java.time.LocalDateTime;

@Setter @Getter
@Entity
@Table(name = "room",
       uniqueConstraints = @UniqueConstraint(name = "uk_room_code", columnNames = "code"))
public class Room {

    @Id @GeneratedValue(strategy = GenerationType.IDENTITY)
    private Long id;

    @Column(unique = true, length = 50) private String code;
    @Column(nullable = false, length = 100) private String name;
    private Integer capacity;
    @Column(length = 255) private String location;
    @Column(name = "is_active", nullable = false) private Boolean isActive = true;
    @Column(name = "created_at", updatable = false) private LocalDateTime createdAt;
    @Column(name = "updated_at") private LocalDateTime updatedAt;

    @PrePersist void onCreate() {
        LocalDateTime now = LocalDateTime.now();
        if (createdAt == null) createdAt = now;
        if (updatedAt == null) updatedAt = now;
        if (isActive == null) isActive = true;
    }
    @PreUpdate void onUpdate() { updatedAt = LocalDateTime.now(); }
}

// FILE: ./src/main/java/com/ecms_web_application/OC01/entity/Session.java
// ============================================================
package com.ecms_web_application.OC01.entity;

import com.ecms_web_application.OC01.entity.enums.SessionStatus;
import jakarta.persistence.*;
import lombok.Getter;
import lombok.Setter;
import java.time.LocalDate;
import java.time.LocalDateTime;

@Setter @Getter
@Entity
@Table(name = "session",
    uniqueConstraints = @UniqueConstraint(
        name = "uk_session_number",
        columnNames = {"course_id", "session_number"}),
    indexes = {
        @Index(name = "idx_session_effective_teacher", columnList = "effective_teacher_id"),
        @Index(name = "idx_session_effective_room",    columnList = "effective_room_id"),
        @Index(name = "idx_session_busy_teacher",
               columnList = "effective_teacher_id, session_date, status"),
        @Index(name = "idx_session_busy_room",
               columnList = "effective_room_id, session_date, status")
    })
public class Session {

    @Id @GeneratedValue(strategy = GenerationType.IDENTITY)
    private Long id;

    @ManyToOne(fetch = FetchType.LAZY, optional = false)
    @JoinColumn(name = "course_id", nullable = false)
    private Course course;

    @Column(name = "session_number", nullable = false)
    private Integer sessionNumber;

    @Column(nullable = false, length = 255)
    private String title;

    @Column(columnDefinition = "TEXT")
    private String description;

    @Column(name = "start_time", nullable = false)
    private LocalDateTime startTime;

    @Column(name = "end_time", nullable = false)
    private LocalDateTime endTime;

    @ManyToOne(fetch = FetchType.LAZY, optional = false)
    @JoinColumn(name = "teacher_id", nullable = false)
    private Account teacher;

    @ManyToOne(fetch = FetchType.LAZY)
    @JoinColumn(name = "substitute_teacher_id")
    private Account substituteTeacher;

    @ManyToOne(fetch = FetchType.LAZY)
    @JoinColumn(name = "assistant_id")
    private Account assistant;

    @ManyToOne(fetch = FetchType.LAZY)
    @JoinColumn(name = "room_id")
    private Room room;

    @Column(name = "session_date", nullable = false)
    private LocalDate sessionDate;

    @Column(name = "effective_room_id")
    private Long effectiveRoomId;

    @Column(name = "effective_teacher_id", insertable = false, updatable = false)
    private Long effectiveTeacherId;

    @Enumerated(EnumType.STRING)
    @Column(nullable = false, length = 20)
    private SessionStatus status = SessionStatus.SCHEDULED;

    @Column(name = "cancel_reason", length = 500)
    private String cancelReason;

    @Column(columnDefinition = "TEXT")
    private String note;

    @Column(name = "rescheduled_from_id")
    private Long rescheduledFromId;

    @Column(name = "created_at", updatable = false)
    private LocalDateTime createdAt;

    @Column(name = "updated_at")
    private LocalDateTime updatedAt;

    @PrePersist
    void onCreate() {
        LocalDateTime now = LocalDateTime.now();
        if (createdAt == null) createdAt = now;
        if (updatedAt == null) updatedAt = now;
        if (status == null) status = SessionStatus.SCHEDULED;
        syncDerivedFields();
    }

    @PreUpdate
    void onUpdate() {
        updatedAt = LocalDateTime.now();
        syncDerivedFields();
    }

    private void syncDerivedFields() {
        if (this.sessionDate == null && this.startTime != null) {
            this.sessionDate = this.startTime.toLocalDate();
        }
        this.effectiveRoomId = (this.room != null)
                ? this.room.getId()
                : (this.course != null && this.course.getRoom() != null
                    ? this.course.getRoom().getId() : null);

        if (this.substituteTeacher != null && this.teacher != null
                && this.substituteTeacher.getId() != null
                && this.substituteTeacher.getId().equals(this.teacher.getId())) {
            this.substituteTeacher = null;
        }
    }
}

package com.ecms_web_application.OC01.entity;

import jakarta.persistence.*;
import lombok.Getter;
import lombok.Setter;

import java.time.LocalDate;
import java.time.LocalDateTime;

/**
 * Entity đại diện cho Student (Học viên).
 * Bảng: students
 */
@Getter
@Setter
@Entity
@Table(name = "students")
public class Student {

    @Id
    @GeneratedValue(strategy = GenerationType.IDENTITY)
    @Column(name = "id")
    private Long id;

    @Column(name = "student_code", nullable = false, unique = true, length = 20)
    private String studentCode;

    @Column(name = "lead_id", unique = true)
    private Long leadId;

    @Column(name = "user_id")
    private Long userId;

    @Column(name = "full_name", nullable = false, length = 255)
    private String fullName;

    @Column(name = "email", length = 255)
    private String email;

    @Column(name = "phone", length = 20)
    private String phone;

    @Column(name = "dob")
    private LocalDate dob;

    @Column(name = "address", columnDefinition = "TEXT")
    private String address;

    @Column(name = "status", nullable = false, length = 20)
    private String status = "ACTIVE";

    @Column(name = "created_at", nullable = false, updatable = false)
    private LocalDateTime createdAt;

    @Column(name = "updated_at", nullable = false)
    private LocalDateTime updatedAt;

    // ===== Hooks =====
    @PrePersist
    void onCreate() {
        LocalDateTime now = LocalDateTime.now();
        if (this.createdAt == null) this.createdAt = now;
        if (this.updatedAt == null) this.updatedAt = now;
        if (this.status == null || this.status.isBlank()) this.status = "ACTIVE";
    }

    @PreUpdate
    void onUpdate() {
        this.updatedAt = LocalDateTime.now();
    }
}

package com.ecms_web_application.OC01.entity;

import jakarta.persistence.*;
import lombok.Getter;
import lombok.Setter;

import java.time.LocalDateTime;

/**
 * Entity đại diện cho nhật ký thay đổi trạng thái học viên (StudentStatusLogs).
 * Bảng: student_status_logs
 */
@Getter
@Setter
@Entity
@Table(name = "student_status_logs")
public class StudentStatusLog {

    @Id
    @GeneratedValue(strategy = GenerationType.IDENTITY)
    @Column(name = "id")
    private Long id;

    @Column(name = "student_id", nullable = false)
    private Long studentId;

    @Column(name = "old_status", length = 30)
    private String oldStatus;

    @Column(name = "new_status", nullable = false, length = 30)
    private String newStatus;

    @Column(name = "reason", columnDefinition = "TEXT")
    private String reason;

    @Column(name = "changed_by")
    private Long changedBy;

    @Column(name = "changed_at", nullable = false, updatable = false)
    private LocalDateTime changedAt;

    @PrePersist
    void onCreate() {
        if (this.changedAt == null) {
            this.changedAt = LocalDateTime.now();
        }
    }
}

package com.ecms_web_application.OC01.entity;

import com.ecms_web_application.OC01.entity.enums.Degree;
import com.ecms_web_application.OC01.entity.enums.Gender;
import jakarta.persistence.*;
import lombok.Getter;
import lombok.Setter;
import org.hibernate.annotations.JdbcTypeCode;
import org.hibernate.type.SqlTypes;

import java.time.LocalDate;
import java.time.LocalDateTime;

@Setter
@Getter
@Entity
@Table(name = "user_profile")
public class UserProfile {

    @Id
    @GeneratedValue(strategy = GenerationType.IDENTITY)
    @Column(name = "id")
    private Long id;

    // ===== Account (OneToOne) =====
    @OneToOne(fetch = FetchType.LAZY)
    @JoinColumn(name = "account_id", nullable = false, unique = true)
    private Account account;

    // ===== Thông tin cá nhân =====
    @Column(name = "full_name", nullable = false, length = 150)
    private String fullName;

    @Column(name = "phone", length = 20)
    private String phone;

    @Column(name = "avatar_url", length = 500)
    private String avatarUrl;

    @Column(name = "address", length = 500)
    private String address;

    @Column(name = "bio", columnDefinition = "TEXT")
    private String bio;

    @Column(name = "date_of_birth")
    private LocalDate dateOfBirth;

    @Enumerated(EnumType.STRING)
    @JdbcTypeCode(SqlTypes.NAMED_ENUM)
    @Column(name = "gender", columnDefinition = "gender_type")
    private Gender gender;

    // ===== Thông tin teacher =====
    @Enumerated(EnumType.STRING)
    @JdbcTypeCode(SqlTypes.NAMED_ENUM)
    @Column(name = "degree", columnDefinition = "degree_type")
    private Degree degree;

    @Column(name = "years_of_experience")
    private Short yearsOfExperience;

    // ===== Verify =====
    @Column(name = "is_verified", nullable = false)
    private Boolean isVerified = false;

    // ===== Audit =====
    @Column(name = "created_at", updatable = false)
    private LocalDateTime createdAt;

    @Column(name = "updated_at")
    private LocalDateTime updatedAt;

    @Column(name = "deleted_at")
    private LocalDateTime deletedAt;

    // ===== Hooks =====
    @PrePersist
    void onCreate() {
        LocalDateTime now = LocalDateTime.now();
        if (this.createdAt == null) this.createdAt = now;
        if (this.updatedAt == null) this.updatedAt = now;
        if (this.isVerified == null) this.isVerified = false;
    }

    @PreUpdate
    void onUpdate() {
        this.updatedAt = LocalDateTime.now();
    }
}
