                    .orElseThrow(() -> new ErrorHandler(HttpStatus.NOT_FOUND, "Danh mục không tồn tại")));
        }
        if (request.getRoomId() != null) {
            c.setRoom(roomRepository.findById(request.getRoomId())
                    .orElseThrow(() -> new ErrorHandler(HttpStatus.NOT_FOUND, "Phòng không tồn tại")));
        }

        Course saved = courseRepository.save(c);
        auditLogService.log("CREATE", "COURSE", saved.getId(), "Tạo khóa: " + saved.getTitle());
        log.info("Tạo course id={}, code={}", saved.getId(), saved.getCode());
        return toResponse(saved);
    }

    @Override
    @Transactional(readOnly = true)
    public CourseDetailResponse getById(Long id) {
        Course c = courseRepository.findByIdWithRelations(id)
                .orElseThrow(() -> new ErrorHandler(HttpStatus.NOT_FOUND, "Khóa học không tồn tại"));
        CourseDetailResponse res = new CourseDetailResponse();
        copyToResponse(c, res);
        res.setDescription(c.getDescription());
        res.setOutcomes(c.getOutcomes());
        res.setResources(resourceRepository.findByCourse_IdOrderByOrderIndexAsc(id)
                .stream().map(this::toResourceResponse).toList());
        return res;
    }

    @Override
    @Transactional(readOnly = true)
    public Page<CourseResponse> search(String status, String type, Long teacherId,
                                       Long categoryId, String search, Pageable pageable) {
        CourseStatus st = StringUtils.hasText(status) ? CourseStatus.valueOf(status.toUpperCase()) : null;
        CourseType tp = StringUtils.hasText(type) ? CourseType.valueOf(type.toUpperCase()) : null;
        String kw = StringUtils.hasText(search) ? search.trim() : "";
        return courseRepository.searchCourses(st, tp, teacherId, categoryId, kw, pageable)
                .map(this::toResponse);
    }

    @Override
    @Transactional
    public CourseResponse update(Long id, CourseUpdateRequest request) {
        courseValidator.validateForUpdate(request);
        Course c = getCourseOrThrow(id);
        assertCanEdit(c);

        if (StringUtils.hasText(request.getTitle())) {
            c.setTitle(request.getTitle().trim());
            c.setSlug(generateUniqueSlug(slugify(request.getTitle()), id));
        }
        if (request.getShortDescription() != null) {
            if (request.getShortDescription().length() > 500) {
                throw new ErrorHandler(HttpStatus.BAD_REQUEST, "Mô tả ngắn không vượt quá 500 ký tự");
            }
            c.setShortDescription(request.getShortDescription());
        }
        if (request.getDescription() != null) c.setDescription(request.getDescription());
        if (request.getThumbnailUrl() != null) c.setThumbnailUrl(request.getThumbnailUrl());
        if (request.getOutcomes() != null) c.setOutcomes(request.getOutcomes());
        if (request.getLearningMode() != null)    c.setLearningMode(request.getLearningMode());
        if (request.getAllowedRoomIds() != null)  c.setAllowedRoomIds(request.getAllowedRoomIds());
        if (request.getPrice() != null) {
            if (request.getPrice().signum() < 0) {
                throw new ErrorHandler(HttpStatus.BAD_REQUEST, "Giá phải >= 0");
            }
            c.setPrice(request.getPrice());
        }
        if (request.getOldPrice() != null) c.setOldPrice(request.getOldPrice());
        if (request.getIsFree() != null) c.setIsFree(request.getIsFree());
        if (request.getCategoryId() != null) {
            c.setCategory(categoryRepository.findById(request.getCategoryId())
                    .orElseThrow(() -> new ErrorHandler(HttpStatus.NOT_FOUND, "Danh mục không tồn tại")));
        }
        if (request.getRoomId() != null) {
            c.setRoom(roomRepository.findById(request.getRoomId())
                    .orElseThrow(() -> new ErrorHandler(HttpStatus.NOT_FOUND, "Phòng không tồn tại")));
        }

        Course saved = courseRepository.save(c);
        auditLogService.log("UPDATE", "COURSE", id, "Cập nhật khóa học");
        return toResponse(saved);
    }

    @Override
    @Transactional
    public CourseResponse updateOfflineInfo(Long id, CourseOfflineInfoRequest request) {
        courseValidator.validateForOfflineInfo(request);
        Course c = getCourseOrThrow(id);
        assertCanEdit(c);

        if (request.getStartDate().isAfter(request.getEndDate())) {
            throw new ErrorHandler(HttpStatus.BAD_REQUEST, "Ngày bắt đầu phải <= ngày kết thúc");
        }
        if (request.getMaxStudents() < request.getMinStudents()) {
            throw new ErrorHandler(HttpStatus.BAD_REQUEST, "max_students phải >= min_students");
        }
        if (!request.getSessionStartTime().isBefore(request.getSessionEndTime())) {
            throw new ErrorHandler(HttpStatus.BAD_REQUEST, "session_start_time phải < session_end_time");
        }
        if (request.getScheduleDays() == null || request.getScheduleDays().isEmpty()) {
            throw new ErrorHandler(HttpStatus.BAD_REQUEST, "schedule_days không được rỗng");
        }

        c.setMinStudents(request.getMinStudents());
        c.setMaxStudents(request.getMaxStudents());
        c.setStartDate(request.getStartDate());
        c.setEndDate(request.getEndDate());
        c.setScheduleDays(request.getScheduleDays());
        c.setSessionStartTime(request.getSessionStartTime());
        c.setSessionEndTime(request.getSessionEndTime());
        c.setCourseType(CourseType.OFFLINE);
        c.setProgressType(ProgressType.SESSION_BASED);
        c.setClassStatus(ClassStatus.PLANNED);
        if (c.getLearningMode() == null) c.setLearningMode("SEQUENTIAL");
        if (request.getTeacherId() != null) {
            c.setTeacher(accountRepository.findById(request.getTeacherId())
                    .orElseThrow(() -> new ErrorHandler(HttpStatus.NOT_FOUND, "GV không tồn tại")));
        }
        if (request.getRoomId() != null) {
            c.setRoom(roomRepository.findById(request.getRoomId())
                    .orElseThrow(() -> new ErrorHandler(HttpStatus.NOT_FOUND, "Phòng không tồn tại")));
        }

        Course saved = courseRepository.save(c);
        auditLogService.log("UPDATE_OFFLINE", "COURSE", id, "Chuyển OFFLINE");
        // Auto generate session nếu course chưa có session (best-effort)
        try {
            if (saved.getCourseType() == CourseType.OFFLINE) {
                sessionService.generateSessions(id);
            }
        } catch (Exception ex) {
            log.warn("[AutoGenerate] Không sinh được session cho course {}: {}",
                    id, ex.getMessage());
        }
        return toResponse(saved);
    }

    @Override
    @Transactional
    public CourseResponse submitForReview(Long id) {
        Course c = getCourseOrThrow(id);
        if (c.getStatus() != CourseStatus.DRAFT) {
            throw new ErrorHandler(HttpStatus.CONFLICT, "Chỉ submit khóa ở DRAFT");
        }
        validateBeforeSubmit(c);
        c.setStatus(CourseStatus.PENDING_REVIEW);
        Course saved = courseRepository.save(c);
        auditLogService.log("SUBMIT_REVIEW", "COURSE", id, null);
        return toResponse(saved);
    }

    @Override
    @Transactional
    public CourseResponse review(Long id, CourseReviewRequest request) {
        Course c = getCourseOrThrow(id);
        if (c.getStatus() != CourseStatus.PENDING_REVIEW) {
            throw new ErrorHandler(HttpStatus.CONFLICT, "Chỉ duyệt khóa ở PENDING_REVIEW");
        }
        if (Boolean.TRUE.equals(request.getApproved())) {
            c.setStatus(CourseStatus.PUBLISHED);
            if (c.getCourseType() == CourseType.OFFLINE && c.getClassStatus() == null) {
                c.setClassStatus(ClassStatus.PLANNED);
            }
        } else {
            if (!StringUtils.hasText(request.getRejectReason())) {
                throw new ErrorHandler(HttpStatus.BAD_REQUEST, "Lý do từ chối là bắt buộc");
            }
            c.setStatus(CourseStatus.DRAFT);
        }
        Course saved = courseRepository.save(c);
        auditLogService.log(Boolean.TRUE.equals(request.getApproved()) ? "APPROVE" : "REJECT",
                "COURSE", id, request.getRejectReason());
        return toResponse(saved);
    }

    @Override
    @Transactional
    public CourseResponse archive(Long id) {
        Course c = getCourseOrThrow(id);
        c.setStatus(CourseStatus.ARCHIVED);
        Course saved = courseRepository.save(c);
        auditLogService.log("ARCHIVE", "COURSE", id, null);
        return toResponse(saved);
    }

    @Override
    @Transactional
    public CourseResponse cancel(Long id, String reason) {
        Course c = getCourseOrThrow(id);
        if (c.getStatus() == CourseStatus.CANCELLED) {
            throw new ErrorHandler(HttpStatus.CONFLICT, "Khóa đã bị hủy");
        }
        if (!StringUtils.hasText(reason)) {
            throw new ErrorHandler(HttpStatus.BAD_REQUEST, "Lý do hủy là bắt buộc");
        }
        c.setStatus(CourseStatus.CANCELLED);
        if (c.getCourseType() == CourseType.OFFLINE) c.setClassStatus(ClassStatus.CANCELLED);
        Course saved = courseRepository.save(c);
        auditLogService.log("CANCEL", "COURSE", id, reason);
        return toResponse(saved);
    }

    @Override
    @Transactional
    public CourseDetailResponse copy(Long sourceCourseId, CourseCopyRequest request) {
        Course src = courseRepository.findByIdWithRelations(sourceCourseId)
                .orElseThrow(() -> new ErrorHandler(HttpStatus.NOT_FOUND, "Khóa gốc không tồn tại"));
        if (courseRepository.existsByDisplayCode(request.getNewDisplayCode())) {
            throw new ErrorHandler(HttpStatus.CONFLICT, "display_code đã tồn tại");
        }
        Account current = SecurityUtils.requireCurrentAccount();
        String typeCode = src.getCourseType() == CourseType.OFFLINE ? "OFF" : "ONL";
        String code = generateCode(typeCode);
        String slug = generateUniqueSlug(slugify(request.getNewTitle()), null);

        Course copy = new Course();
        copy.setCode(code);
        copy.setSlug(slug);
        copy.setDisplayCode(request.getNewDisplayCode());
        copy.setTitle(request.getNewTitle());
        copy.setShortDescription(src.getShortDescription());
        copy.setDescription(src.getDescription());
        copy.setThumbnailUrl(src.getThumbnailUrl());
        copy.setOutcomes(src.getOutcomes());
        copy.setLearningMode(src.getLearningMode());
        copy.setAllowedRoomIds(src.getAllowedRoomIds());
        copy.setPrice(Boolean.TRUE.equals(request.getResetPrice()) ? BigDecimal.ZERO : src.getPrice());
        copy.setOldPrice(src.getOldPrice());
        copy.setIsFree(src.getIsFree());
        copy.setCourseType(src.getCourseType());
        copy.setProgressType(src.getProgressType());
        copy.setStatus(CourseStatus.DRAFT);
        copy.setCategory(src.getCategory());
        copy.setTeacher(src.getTeacher());
        copy.setCreatedBy(current);
        copy.setCopiedFromCourseId(src.getId());
        Course savedCopy = courseRepository.save(copy);

        Map<Long, CourseResource> idMap = new HashMap<>();
        for (CourseResource srcR : resourceRepository.findByCourse_IdOrderByOrderIndexAsc(src.getId())) {
            CourseResource newR = new CourseResource();
            newR.setCourse(savedCopy);
            newR.setParent(srcR.getParent() != null ? idMap.get(srcR.getParent().getId()) : null);
            newR.setResourceType(srcR.getResourceType());
            newR.setTitle(srcR.getTitle());
            newR.setDescription(srcR.getDescription());
            newR.setOrderIndex(srcR.getOrderIndex());
            newR.setIsRequired(srcR.getIsRequired());
            newR.setIsFreePreview(srcR.getIsFreePreview());
            newR.setDuration(srcR.getDuration());
            newR.setFileUrl(srcR.getFileUrl());
            newR.setFileName(srcR.getFileName());
            newR.setFileSize(srcR.getFileSize());
            newR.setFileFormat(srcR.getFileFormat());
            newR.setMimeType(srcR.getMimeType());
            newR.setThumbnailUrl(srcR.getThumbnailUrl());
            newR.setContent(srcR.getContent());
            newR.setMaxAttempts(srcR.getMaxAttempts());
            newR.setPassingScore(srcR.getPassingScore());
            newR.setTimeLimit(srcR.getTimeLimit());
            newR.setPoints(srcR.getPoints());
            newR.setSettings(srcR.getSettings());
            newR.setStatus(ResourceStatus.DRAFT);
            newR.setQuizCategory(srcR.getQuizCategory());
            newR.setHashtagFilter(srcR.getHashtagFilter());
            newR.setTotalQuestions(srcR.getTotalQuestions());
            newR.setShuffleQuestions(srcR.getShuffleQuestions());
            newR.setCreatedBy(current);
            idMap.put(srcR.getId(), resourceRepository.save(newR));
        }

        // Copy Question pool — remap lesson_id qua idMap
        java.util.List<com.ecms_web_application.OC01.entity.Question> srcQuestions =
                questionRepository.findByCourse_Id(src.getId());
        for (com.ecms_web_application.OC01.entity.Question sq : srcQuestions) {
            com.ecms_web_application.OC01.entity.Question nq =
                    new com.ecms_web_application.OC01.entity.Question();
            nq.setCourse(savedCopy);
            if (sq.getLesson() != null) {
                nq.setLesson(idMap.get(sq.getLesson().getId()));
            }
            nq.setQuestionType(sq.getQuestionType());
            nq.setContent(sq.getContent());
            nq.setOptions(sq.getOptions());
            nq.setCorrectAnswer(sq.getCorrectAnswer());
            nq.setExplanation(sq.getExplanation());
            nq.setHashtag(sq.getHashtag());
            nq.setPoints(sq.getPoints());
            nq.setOrderIndex(sq.getOrderIndex());
            nq.setStatus(sq.getStatus());
            questionRepository.save(nq);
        }
        auditLogService.log("COPY", "COURSE", savedCopy.getId(), "Copy từ course " + src.getId());
        log.info("Copy course {} → {}", src.getId(), savedCopy.getId());
        return getById(savedCopy.getId());
    }

    @Override
    @Transactional
    public void softDelete(Long id) {
        Course c = getCourseOrThrow(id);
        if (c.getStatus() != CourseStatus.ARCHIVED && c.getStatus() != CourseStatus.DRAFT) {
            throw new ErrorHandler(HttpStatus.CONFLICT, "Chỉ xóa mềm khóa DRAFT/ARCHIVED");
        }
        c.setDeletedAt(LocalDateTime.now());
        courseRepository.save(c);
        auditLogService.log("SOFT_DELETE", "COURSE", id, null);
    }

    @Override
    @Transactional
    public void hardDelete(Long id) {
        Course c = getCourseOrThrow(id);
        if (c.getStatus() != CourseStatus.DRAFT) {
            throw new ErrorHandler(HttpStatus.CONFLICT, "Chỉ xóa cứng khóa DRAFT");
        }
        courseRepository.delete(c);
        auditLogService.log("HARD_DELETE", "COURSE", id, null);
    }

    @Override
    @Transactional
    public CourseResponse changeTeacher(Long courseId, Long newTeacherId) {
        Course c = getCourseOrThrow(courseId);
        if (c.getCourseType() != CourseType.OFFLINE) {
            throw new ErrorHandler(HttpStatus.BAD_REQUEST,
                    "Chỉ đổi GV chính cho khóa OFFLINE");
        }
        sessionService.changeCourseMainTeacher(courseId, newTeacherId);
        Course updated = getCourseOrThrow(courseId);
        return toResponse(updated);
    }

    private Course getCourseOrThrow(Long id) {
        return courseRepository.findById(id)
                .filter(c -> c.getDeletedAt() == null)
                .orElseThrow(() -> new ErrorHandler(HttpStatus.NOT_FOUND, "Khóa học không tồn tại"));
    }

    private void assertCanEdit(Course c) {
        if (c.getStatus() == CourseStatus.CANCELLED) {
            throw new ErrorHandler(HttpStatus.CONFLICT, "Khóa đã hủy, không thể sửa");
        }
    }

    private Account resolveTeacher(Long teacherId, Account current) {
        if (teacherId != null) {
            return accountRepository.findById(teacherId)
                    .orElseThrow(() -> new ErrorHandler(HttpStatus.NOT_FOUND, "Giảng viên không tồn tại"));
        }
        return current;
    }

    private void validateBeforeSubmit(Course c) {
        if (c.getPrice().signum() <= 0 && !Boolean.TRUE.equals(c.getIsFree())) {
            throw new ErrorHandler(HttpStatus.BAD_REQUEST, "Giá phải > 0 hoặc is_free = true");
        }
        if (c.getCourseType() == CourseType.ONLINE) {
            long chapters = resourceRepository.countByCourse_IdAndResourceType(c.getId(), ResourceType.CHAPTER);
            if (chapters == 0) {
                throw new ErrorHandler(HttpStatus.BAD_REQUEST, "Cần ít nhất 1 Chapter");
            }
        }
    }

    private String generateCode(String typeCode) {
        String ym = DateTimeFormatter.ofPattern("yyyyMM").format(java.time.LocalDate.now());
        long count = courseRepository.count() + 1;
        return String.format("CRS-%s-%s-%03d", typeCode, ym, count);
    }

    private String generateUniqueSlug(String base, Long excludeId) {
        String s = base;
        int i = 1;
        while (excludeId == null ? courseRepository.existsBySlug(s)
                : courseRepository.existsBySlugAndIdNot(s, excludeId)) {
            s = base + "-" + i++;
        }
        return s;
    }

    private String slugify(String input) {
        if (input == null) return "";
        String temp = Normalizer.normalize(input, Normalizer.Form.NFD);
        temp = temp.replaceAll("\\p{InCombiningDiacriticalMarks}+", "");
        temp = temp.replaceAll("đ", "d").replaceAll("Đ", "D");
        temp = temp.toLowerCase(Locale.ROOT);
        temp = NON_ALNUM.matcher(temp).replaceAll("-");
        return temp.replaceAll("^-+|-+$", "");
    }

    private CourseResponse toResponse(Course c) {
        CourseResponse r = new CourseResponse();
        copyToResponse(c, r);
        return r;
    }

    private void copyToResponse(Course c, CourseResponse r) {
        r.setId(c.getId());
        r.setCode(c.getCode());
        r.setSlug(c.getSlug());
        r.setDisplayCode(c.getDisplayCode());
        r.setTitle(c.getTitle());
        r.setShortDescription(c.getShortDescription());
        r.setThumbnailUrl(c.getThumbnailUrl());
        r.setPrice(c.getPrice());
        r.setOldPrice(c.getOldPrice());
        r.setIsFree(c.getIsFree());
        r.setCourseType(c.getCourseType() != null ? c.getCourseType().name() : null);
        r.setProgressType(c.getProgressType() != null ? c.getProgressType().name() : null);
        r.setStatus(c.getStatus() != null ? c.getStatus().name() : null);
        r.setClassStatus(c.getClassStatus() != null ? c.getClassStatus().name() : null);
        if (c.getTeacher() != null) {
            r.setTeacherId(c.getTeacher().getId());
            if (c.getTeacher().getUserProfile() != null) {
                r.setTeacherName(c.getTeacher().getUserProfile().getFullName());
            }
        }
        if (c.getCreatedBy() != null) r.setCreatedById(c.getCreatedBy().getId());
        if (c.getCategory() != null) {
            r.setCategoryId(c.getCategory().getId());
            r.setCategoryName(c.getCategory().getName());
        }
        if (c.getRoom() != null) {
            r.setRoomId(c.getRoom().getId());
            r.setRoomName(c.getRoom().getName());
        }
        r.setMinStudents(c.getMinStudents());
        r.setMaxStudents(c.getMaxStudents());
        r.setCurrentStudents(c.getCurrentStudents());
        r.setStartDate(c.getStartDate());
        r.setEndDate(c.getEndDate());
        r.setScheduleDays(c.getScheduleDays());
        r.setSessionStartTime(c.getSessionStartTime());
        r.setSessionEndTime(c.getSessionEndTime());
        r.setCopiedFromCourseId(c.getCopiedFromCourseId());
        r.setLearningMode(c.getLearningMode());
        r.setAllowedRoomIds(c.getAllowedRoomIds());
        r.setCreatedAt(c.getCreatedAt());
        r.setUpdatedAt(c.getUpdatedAt());
    }

    private CourseResourceResponse toResourceResponse(CourseResource r) {
        CourseResourceResponse res = new CourseResourceResponse();
        res.setId(r.getId());
        res.setCourseId(r.getCourse().getId());
        res.setParentId(r.getParent() != null ? r.getParent().getId() : null);
        res.setResourceType(r.getResourceType().name());
        res.setTitle(r.getTitle());
        res.setDescription(r.getDescription());
        res.setOrderIndex(r.getOrderIndex());
        res.setIsRequired(r.getIsRequired());
        res.setIsFreePreview(r.getIsFreePreview());
        res.setDuration(r.getDuration());
        res.setContent(r.getContent());
        res.setThumbnailUrl(r.getThumbnailUrl());
        res.setFileName(r.getFileName());
        res.setFileUrl(r.getFileUrl());
        res.setFileSize(r.getFileSize());
        res.setFileFormat(r.getFileFormat());
        res.setMimeType(r.getMimeType());
        res.setMaxAttempts(r.getMaxAttempts());
        res.setPassingScore(r.getPassingScore());
        res.setTimeLimit(r.getTimeLimit());
        res.setPoints(r.getPoints());
        res.setDeadline(r.getDeadline());
        res.setSettings(r.getSettings());
        res.setStatus(r.getStatus() != null ? r.getStatus().name() : null);
        res.setAvailableFrom(r.getAvailableFrom());
        res.setAvailableUntil(r.getAvailableUntil());
        res.setQuizCategory(r.getQuizCategory() != null ? r.getQuizCategory().name() : null);
        res.setHashtagFilter(r.getHashtagFilter());
        res.setTotalQuestions(r.getTotalQuestions());
        res.setShuffleQuestions(r.getShuffleQuestions());
        res.setCreatedAt(r.getCreatedAt());
        res.setUpdatedAt(r.getUpdatedAt());
        return res;
    }
}

// ============================================================
// FILE: ./src/main/java/com/ecms_web_application/OC01/service/impl/course/ProgressCalculationServiceImpl.java
// ============================================================
package com.ecms_web_application.OC01.service.impl.course;

import com.ecms_web_application.OC01.entity.Account;
import com.ecms_web_application.OC01.entity.Course;
import com.ecms_web_application.OC01.entity.CourseResource;
import com.ecms_web_application.OC01.entity.Progress;
import com.ecms_web_application.OC01.entity.enums.ProgressScope;
import com.ecms_web_application.OC01.entity.enums.ProgressStatus;
import com.ecms_web_application.OC01.entity.enums.ProgressType;
import com.ecms_web_application.OC01.entity.enums.ResourceType;
import com.ecms_web_application.OC01.exception.ErrorHandler;
import com.ecms_web_application.OC01.repository.AccountRepository;
import com.ecms_web_application.OC01.repository.CourseRepository;
import com.ecms_web_application.OC01.repository.CourseResourceRepository;
import com.ecms_web_application.OC01.repository.ProgressRepository;
import com.ecms_web_application.OC01.repository.SessionRepository;
import com.ecms_web_application.OC01.service.ProgressCalculationService;
import lombok.RequiredArgsConstructor;
import lombok.extern.slf4j.Slf4j;
import org.springframework.http.HttpStatus;
import org.springframework.stereotype.Service;
import org.springframework.transaction.annotation.Transactional;

import java.math.BigDecimal;
import java.math.RoundingMode;
import java.time.LocalDateTime;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.Set;
import java.util.stream.Collectors;

@Service
@RequiredArgsConstructor
@Slf4j
public class ProgressCalculationServiceImpl implements ProgressCalculationService {

    private final ProgressRepository progressRepository;
    private final CourseRepository courseRepository;
    private final CourseResourceRepository resourceRepository;
    private final SessionRepository sessionRepository;
    private final AccountRepository accountRepository;

    // ============================================================
    // PUBLIC API
    // ============================================================

    @Override
    @Transactional
    public void recalculateForCourse(Long courseId) {
        Course course = courseRepository.findById(courseId).orElse(null);
        if (course == null) { return; }

        List<Progress> list = progressRepository
                .findByCourse_IdAndProgressType(courseId, ProgressScope.COURSE);
        if (list.isEmpty()) return;

        for (Progress p : list) {
            recalcInternal(p, course);
        }
        log.debug("[Calc] recalculateForCourse({}) → {} progress", courseId, list.size());
    }

    @Override
    @Transactional
    public void recalculateForStudent(Long accountId, Long courseId) {
        Course course = courseRepository.findById(courseId)
                .orElseThrow(() -> new ErrorHandler(HttpStatus.NOT_FOUND, "Course không tồn tại"));

        Progress p = progressRepository
                .findByAccount_IdAndCourse_IdAndProgressTypeAndReferenceId(
                        accountId, courseId, ProgressScope.COURSE, null)
                .orElseGet(() -> {
                    Account acc = accountRepository.findById(accountId)
                            .orElseThrow(() -> new ErrorHandler(HttpStatus.NOT_FOUND,
                                    "Account không tồn tại"));
                    Progress np = new Progress();
                    np.setAccount(acc);
                    np.setCourse(course);
                    np.setProgressType(ProgressScope.COURSE);
                    np.setReferenceId(null);
                    np.setReferenceType(null);
                    np.setStatus(ProgressStatus.NOT_STARTED);
                    np.setProgressPercentage(BigDecimal.ZERO);
                    np.setStartedAt(LocalDateTime.now());
                    return np;
                });

        recalcInternal(p, course);
    }

    @Override
    @Transactional
    public void onResourceChanged(Long courseId) {
        recalculateForCourse(courseId);
    }

    @Override
    @Transactional
    public void onSessionChanged(Long courseId) {
        recalculateForCourse(courseId);
    }

    @Override
    @Transactional
    public void onQuizGraded(Long accountId, Long courseId) {
        recalculateForStudent(accountId, courseId);
    }

    // ============================================================
    // CORE CALCULATION
    // ============================================================

    private void recalcInternal(Progress courseProg, Course course) {
        Long accountId = courseProg.getAccount().getId();
        Long courseId = course.getId();

        int total;
        int completed;

        if (course.getProgressType() == ProgressType.COMPLETION_BASED) {
            List<CourseResource> units = resourceRepository.findRequiredProgressUnits(courseId);
            total = units.size();
            if (total == 0) {
                completed = 0;
            } else {
                Set<Long> unitIds = units.stream()
                        .map(CourseResource::getId)
                        .collect(Collectors.toSet());
                List<Progress> resources = progressRepository
                        .findByAccount_IdAndCourse_IdAndProgressType(
                                accountId, courseId, ProgressScope.RESOURCE);
                completed = (int) resources.stream()
                        .filter(rp -> rp.getReferenceId() != null
                                && unitIds.contains(rp.getReferenceId()))
                        .filter(rp -> rp.getStatus() == ProgressStatus.COMPLETED
                                || rp.getStatus() == ProgressStatus.PASSED)
                        .count();
            }
        } else { // SESSION_BASED
            total = (int) sessionRepository.countActiveSessions(courseId);
            completed = (int) sessionRepository.countDoneSessions(courseId);
        }

        BigDecimal newPct = (total > 0)
                ? BigDecimal.valueOf(completed * 100.0 / total)
                        .setScale(2, RoundingMode.HALF_UP)
                : BigDecimal.ZERO;

        BigDecimal curPct = courseProg.getProgressPercentage() != null
                ? courseProg.getProgressPercentage()
                : BigDecimal.ZERO;
        boolean was100 = curPct.compareTo(BigDecimal.valueOf(100)) >= 0;

        // Rule: KHÔNG giảm %
        BigDecimal finalPct = newPct.max(curPct);

        ProgressStatus newStatus;
        if (finalPct.compareTo(BigDecimal.valueOf(100)) >= 0) {
            newStatus = ProgressStatus.COMPLETED;
        } else if (finalPct.signum() > 0) {
            newStatus = ProgressStatus.IN_PROGRESS;
        } else {
            newStatus = ProgressStatus.NOT_STARTED;
        }

        courseProg.setTotalItems(total);
        courseProg.setCompletedItems(completed);
        courseProg.setProgressPercentage(finalPct);
        courseProg.setStatus(newStatus);
        courseProg.setLastAccessedAt(LocalDateTime.now());
        if (newStatus == ProgressStatus.COMPLETED && courseProg.getCompletedAt() == null) {
            courseProg.setCompletedAt(LocalDateTime.now());
        }

        // hasUncompletedNewContent: đã từng 100% nhưng hiện chưa học đủ required
        boolean flag;
        if (total == 0) {
            flag = false;
        } else if (completed >= total) {
            flag = false;
        } else {
            flag = was100;
        }
        courseProg.setHasUncompletedNewContent(flag);

        progressRepository.save(courseProg);
    }
}

// FILE: ./src/main/java/com/ecms_web_application/OC01/service/impl/course/ProgressServiceImpl.java
// ============================================================
package com.ecms_web_application.OC01.service.impl.course;

import com.ecms_web_application.OC01.config.SecurityUtils;
import com.ecms_web_application.OC01.dto.response.course.ProgressResponse;
import com.ecms_web_application.OC01.entity.Account;
import com.ecms_web_application.OC01.entity.CourseResource;
import com.ecms_web_application.OC01.entity.Progress;
import com.ecms_web_application.OC01.entity.enums.ProgressScope;
import com.ecms_web_application.OC01.entity.enums.ProgressStatus;
import com.ecms_web_application.OC01.exception.ErrorHandler;
import com.ecms_web_application.OC01.repository.CourseResourceRepository;
import com.ecms_web_application.OC01.repository.ProgressRepository;
import com.ecms_web_application.OC01.service.ProgressService;
import lombok.RequiredArgsConstructor;
import lombok.extern.slf4j.Slf4j;
import org.springframework.data.domain.Page;
import org.springframework.data.domain.Pageable;
import org.springframework.http.HttpStatus;
import org.springframework.stereotype.Service;
import org.springframework.transaction.annotation.Transactional;

import java.math.BigDecimal;
import java.time.LocalDateTime;
import java.util.List;

@Service
@RequiredArgsConstructor
@Slf4j
public class ProgressServiceImpl implements ProgressService {

    private final ProgressRepository progressRepository;
    private final CourseResourceRepository resourceRepository;
    private final com.ecms_web_application.OC01.service.ProgressCalculationService progressCalculationService;

    @Override
    @Transactional(readOnly = true)
    public Page<ProgressResponse> getMyCourses(Pageable pageable) {
        Account current = SecurityUtils.requireCurrentAccount();
        return progressRepository.findCourseProgressByAccount(current.getId(), pageable)
                .map(this::toResponse);
    }

    @Override
    @Transactional(readOnly = true)
    public ProgressResponse getMyProgressInCourse(Long courseId) {
        Account current = SecurityUtils.requireCurrentAccount();
        Progress p = progressRepository
                .findByAccount_IdAndCourse_IdAndProgressTypeAndReferenceId(
                        current.getId(), courseId, ProgressScope.COURSE, null)
                .orElseThrow(() -> new ErrorHandler(HttpStatus.NOT_FOUND, "Chưa có progress"));
        return toResponse(p);
    }

    @Override
    @Transactional(readOnly = true)
    public List<ProgressResponse> getResourceProgresses(Long courseId) {
        Account current = SecurityUtils.requireCurrentAccount();
        return progressRepository.findByAccount_IdAndCourse_IdAndProgressType(
                        current.getId(), courseId, ProgressScope.RESOURCE)
                .stream().map(this::toResponse).toList();
    }

    @Override
    @Transactional
    public ProgressResponse updateResourceProgress(Long resourceId, Integer percentage, Long timeSpentSeconds) {
        Account current = SecurityUtils.requireCurrentAccount();
        CourseResource r = resourceRepository.findById(resourceId)
                .orElseThrow(() -> new ErrorHandler(HttpStatus.NOT_FOUND, "Resource không tồn tại"));

        Progress p = progressRepository
                .findByAccount_IdAndCourse_IdAndProgressTypeAndReferenceId(
                        current.getId(), r.getCourse().getId(), ProgressScope.RESOURCE, resourceId)
                .orElseGet(() -> {
                    Progress np = new Progress();
                    np.setAccount(current);
                    np.setCourse(r.getCourse());
                    np.setProgressType(ProgressScope.RESOURCE);
                    np.setReferenceId(resourceId);
                    np.setReferenceType(r.getResourceType().name());
                    return np;
                });

        BigDecimal newPct = percentage != null
                ? BigDecimal.valueOf(Math.min(100, Math.max(0, percentage)))
                : BigDecimal.ZERO;
        BigDecimal cur = p.getProgressPercentage() != null ? p.getProgressPercentage() : BigDecimal.ZERO;
        if (newPct.compareTo(cur) > 0) p.setProgressPercentage(newPct);

        if (timeSpentSeconds != null && timeSpentSeconds > 0) {
            int curTime = p.getTotalTimeSpent() != null ? p.getTotalTimeSpent() : 0;
            p.setTotalTimeSpent(curTime + timeSpentSeconds.intValue());
        }
        p.setLastAccessedAt(LocalDateTime.now());
        if (p.getStartedAt() == null) p.setStartedAt(LocalDateTime.now());

        BigDecimal effective = p.getProgressPercentage() != null ? p.getProgressPercentage() : BigDecimal.ZERO;
        if (effective.compareTo(BigDecimal.valueOf(100)) >= 0) {
            p.setStatus(ProgressStatus.COMPLETED);
            p.setCompletedAt(LocalDateTime.now());
        } else if (effective.signum() > 0) {
            p.setStatus(ProgressStatus.IN_PROGRESS);
        }

        Progress saved = progressRepository.save(p);
        // FR_CRS_015: recalc chỉ cho HV hiện tại — efficient hơn recalc cả course
        if (saved.getStatus() == ProgressStatus.COMPLETED
                || saved.getStatus() == ProgressStatus.PASSED) {
            progressCalculationService.recalculateForStudent(
                    saved.getAccount().getId(), saved.getCourse().getId());
        }
        return toResponse(saved);
    }

    @Override
    @Transactional
    public ProgressResponse markResourceCompleted(Long resourceId) {
        return updateResourceProgress(resourceId, 100, null);
    }

    @Override
    @Transactional
    public void markHasUncompletedNewContent(Long courseId) {
        // Delegate sang calc service: đồng bộ cờ + % + total/completed items
        progressCalculationService.onResourceChanged(courseId);
    }

    @Override
    @Transactional
    public void recalculateCourseProgress(Long courseId) {
        progressCalculationService.recalculateForCourse(courseId);
    }

    private ProgressResponse toResponse(Progress p) {
        ProgressResponse r = new ProgressResponse();
        r.setId(p.getId());
        r.setAccountId(p.getAccount().getId());
        r.setCourseId(p.getCourse().getId());
        r.setCourseTitle(p.getCourse().getTitle());
        r.setProgressType(p.getProgressType().name());
        r.setReferenceId(p.getReferenceId());
        r.setReferenceType(p.getReferenceType());
        r.setStatus(p.getStatus() != null ? p.getStatus().name() : null);
        r.setProgressPercentage(p.getProgressPercentage());
        r.setTotalTimeSpent(p.getTotalTimeSpent());
        r.setScore(p.getScore());
        r.setMaxScore(p.getMaxScore());
        r.setIsPassed(p.getIsPassed());
        r.setAttempts(p.getAttempts());
        r.setNotes(p.getNotes());
        r.setTotalItems(p.getTotalItems());
        r.setCompletedItems(p.getCompletedItems());
        r.setWeightedScore(p.getWeightedScore());
        r.setTotalWeightPercent(p.getTotalWeightPercent());
        r.setLastAccessedAt(p.getLastAccessedAt());
        r.setStartedAt(p.getStartedAt());
        r.setCompletedAt(p.getCompletedAt());
        r.setHasUncompletedNewContent(p.getHasUncompletedNewContent());
        return r;
    }
}

// FILE: ./src/main/java/com/ecms_web_application/OC01/service/impl/course/QuestionServiceImpl.java
// ============================================================
package com.ecms_web_application.OC01.service.impl.course;

import com.ecms_web_application.OC01.dto.request.course.QuestionCreateRequest;
import com.ecms_web_application.OC01.dto.response.course.QuestionResponse;
import com.ecms_web_application.OC01.entity.Course;
import com.ecms_web_application.OC01.entity.CourseResource;
import com.ecms_web_application.OC01.entity.Question;
import com.ecms_web_application.OC01.entity.enums.QuestionStatus;
import com.ecms_web_application.OC01.entity.enums.QuestionType;
import com.ecms_web_application.OC01.entity.enums.ResourceType;
import com.ecms_web_application.OC01.exception.ErrorHandler;
import com.ecms_web_application.OC01.repository.CourseRepository;
import com.ecms_web_application.OC01.repository.CourseResourceRepository;
import com.ecms_web_application.OC01.repository.QuestionRepository;
import com.ecms_web_application.OC01.service.AuditLogService;
import com.ecms_web_application.OC01.service.QuestionService;
import lombok.RequiredArgsConstructor;
import lombok.extern.slf4j.Slf4j;
import org.springframework.data.domain.Page;
import org.springframework.data.domain.Pageable;
import org.springframework.http.HttpStatus;
import org.springframework.stereotype.Service;
import org.springframework.transaction.annotation.Transactional;
import org.springframework.util.StringUtils;

import java.math.BigDecimal;

@Service
@RequiredArgsConstructor
@Slf4j
public class QuestionServiceImpl implements QuestionService {

    private final QuestionRepository questionRepository;
    private final CourseRepository courseRepository;
    private final CourseResourceRepository resourceRepository;
    private final AuditLogService auditLogService;

    @Override
    @Transactional(readOnly = true)
    public Page<QuestionResponse> search(Long courseId, Long lessonId, String questionType,
                                         String status, String hashtag, String search, Pageable pageable) {
        QuestionType qt = StringUtils.hasText(questionType)
                ? QuestionType.valueOf(questionType.toUpperCase()) : null;
        QuestionStatus st = StringUtils.hasText(status)
                ? QuestionStatus.valueOf(status.toUpperCase()) : null;
        return questionRepository.searchQuestions(courseId, lessonId, qt, st,
                StringUtils.hasText(hashtag) ? hashtag : "",
                StringUtils.hasText(search) ? search.trim() : "",
                pageable).map(this::toResponse);
    }

    @Override
    @Transactional(readOnly = true)
    public QuestionResponse getById(Long id) { return toResponse(getOrThrow(id)); }

    @Override
    @Transactional
    public QuestionResponse create(Long courseId, QuestionCreateRequest request) {
        Course course = courseRepository.findById(courseId)
                .orElseThrow(() -> new ErrorHandler(HttpStatus.NOT_FOUND, "Course không tồn tại"));

        CourseResource lesson = null;
        if (request.getLessonId() != null) {
            lesson = resourceRepository.findById(request.getLessonId())
                    .orElseThrow(() -> new ErrorHandler(HttpStatus.NOT_FOUND, "Lesson không tồn tại"));
            if (lesson.getResourceType() != ResourceType.LESSON) {
                throw new ErrorHandler(HttpStatus.BAD_REQUEST, "lessonId phải là LESSON");
            }
            if (!lesson.getCourse().getId().equals(courseId)) {
                throw new ErrorHandler(HttpStatus.BAD_REQUEST, "Lesson không thuộc course");
            }
        }

        Question q = new Question();
        q.setCourse(course);
        q.setLesson(lesson);
        q.setQuestionType(request.getQuestionType());
        q.setContent(request.getContent());
        q.setOptions(request.getOptions());
        q.setCorrectAnswer(request.getCorrectAnswer());
        q.setExplanation(request.getExplanation());
        q.setHashtag(request.getHashtag());
        q.setPoints(request.getPoints() != null ? request.getPoints() : BigDecimal.ONE);
        q.setOrderIndex(request.getOrderIndex() != null ? request.getOrderIndex() : 0);
        q.setStatus(request.getStatus() != null ? request.getStatus() : QuestionStatus.ACTIVE);

        Question saved = questionRepository.save(q);
        auditLogService.log("CREATE", "QUESTION", saved.getId(), null);
        return toResponse(saved);
    }

    @Override
    @Transactional
    public QuestionResponse update(Long id, QuestionCreateRequest request) {
        Question q = getOrThrow(id);
        if (request.getLessonId() != null) {
            CourseResource lesson = resourceRepository.findById(request.getLessonId())
                    .orElseThrow(() -> new ErrorHandler(HttpStatus.NOT_FOUND, "Lesson không tồn tại"));
            q.setLesson(lesson);
        }
        if (request.getQuestionType() != null) q.setQuestionType(request.getQuestionType());
        if (request.getContent() != null) q.setContent(request.getContent());
        if (request.getOptions() != null) q.setOptions(request.getOptions());
        if (request.getCorrectAnswer() != null) q.setCorrectAnswer(request.getCorrectAnswer());
        if (request.getExplanation() != null) q.setExplanation(request.getExplanation());
        if (request.getHashtag() != null) q.setHashtag(request.getHashtag());
        if (request.getPoints() != null) q.setPoints(request.getPoints());
        if (request.getOrderIndex() != null) q.setOrderIndex(request.getOrderIndex());
        if (request.getStatus() != null) q.setStatus(request.getStatus());
        Question saved = questionRepository.save(q);
        auditLogService.log("UPDATE", "QUESTION", id, null);
        return toResponse(saved);
    }

    @Override
    @Transactional
    public void delete(Long id) {
        Question q = getOrThrow(id);
        questionRepository.delete(q);
        auditLogService.log("DELETE", "QUESTION", id, null);
    }

    private Question getOrThrow(Long id) {
        return questionRepository.findById(id)
                .orElseThrow(() -> new ErrorHandler(HttpStatus.NOT_FOUND, "Câu hỏi không tồn tại"));
    }

    private QuestionResponse toResponse(Question q) {
        QuestionResponse r = new QuestionResponse();
        r.setId(q.getId());
        r.setCourseId(q.getCourse().getId());
        if (q.getLesson() != null) {
            r.setLessonId(q.getLesson().getId());
            r.setLessonTitle(q.getLesson().getTitle());
        }
        r.setQuestionType(q.getQuestionType().name());
        r.setContent(q.getContent());
        r.setOptions(q.getOptions());
        r.setCorrectAnswer(q.getCorrectAnswer());
        r.setExplanation(q.getExplanation());
        r.setHashtag(q.getHashtag());
        r.setPoints(q.getPoints());
        r.setOrderIndex(q.getOrderIndex());
        r.setStatus(q.getStatus() != null ? q.getStatus().name() : null);
        r.setCreatedAt(q.getCreatedAt());
        r.setUpdatedAt(q.getUpdatedAt());
        return r;
    }
}

package com.ecms_web_application.OC01.service.impl.course;

import com.ecms_web_application.OC01.config.SecurityUtils;
import com.ecms_web_application.OC01.dto.request.course.QuizCreateRequest;

import com.ecms_web_application.OC01.entity.enums.QuestionStatus;

import com.ecms_web_application.OC01.entity.enums.QuizAttemptStatus;
import com.ecms_web_application.OC01.entity.enums.QuizCategory;

import com.ecms_web_application.OC01.repository.CourseResourceRepository;

import com.ecms_web_application.OC01.repository.QuizAttemptRepository;
import com.ecms_web_application.OC01.repository.StudentRepository;
import com.ecms_web_application.OC01.service.AuditLogService;
import com.ecms_web_application.OC01.service.ProgressCalculationService;
import com.ecms_web_application.OC01.service.QuizService;
import com.fasterxml.jackson.core.type.TypeReference;
import com.fasterxml.jackson.databind.ObjectMapper;
import lombok.RequiredArgsConstructor;
import lombok.extern.slf4j.Slf4j;
import org.springframework.http.HttpStatus;
import org.springframework.stereotype.Service;
import org.springframework.transaction.annotation.Transactional;

import java.math.BigDecimal;
import java.math.RoundingMode;
import java.time.Duration;
import java.time.LocalDateTime;
import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.Arrays;
import java.util.Collections;
import java.util.HashSet;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.Map;
import java.util.Objects;
import java.util.Optional;
import java.util.Set;
import java.util.stream.Collectors;

@Service
@RequiredArgsConstructor
@Slf4j
public class QuizServiceImpl implements QuizService {

    private static final BigDecimal DEFAULT_PASSING_SCORE = BigDecimal.valueOf(70);

    private final CourseRepository courseRepository;
    private final CourseResourceRepository resourceRepository;
    private final QuestionRepository questionRepository;
    private final QuizAttemptRepository quizAttemptRepository;
    private final ProgressRepository progressRepository;
    private final StudentRepository studentRepository;
    private final AccountRepository accountRepository;
    private final ProgressCalculationService progressCalculationService;
    private final AuditLogService auditLogService;
    private final ObjectMapper objectMapper;

    // ============================================================
    // CREATE / UPDATE / DELETE / LIST / DETAIL
    // ============================================================

    @Override
    @Transactional
    public QuizResponse createQuiz(Long courseId, QuizCreateRequest req) {
        Course course = loadCourse(courseId);
        Account current = SecurityUtils.requireCurrentAccount();
        assertCanEdit(course, current);

        CourseResource quiz = new CourseResource();
        quiz.setCourse(course);
        quiz.setResourceType(ResourceType.QUIZ);
        quiz.setTitle(req.getTitle());
        quiz.setQuizCategory(req.getQuizType());
        quiz.setPassingScore(BigDecimal.valueOf(
                req.getPassingScore() != null ? req.getPassingScore() : 70));
        quiz.setMaxAttempts(req.getMaxAttempts());
        quiz.setTimeLimit(req.getTimeLimit());
        quiz.setShuffleQuestions(Boolean.TRUE.equals(req.getShuffleQuestions()));
        quiz.setTotalQuestions(req.getTotalQuestions());
        quiz.setHashtagFilter(req.getHashtagFilter());
        quiz.setAvailableFrom(req.getAvailableFrom());
        quiz.setAvailableUntil(req.getAvailableUntil());
        quiz.setStatus(ResourceStatus.PUBLISHED);
        quiz.setIsRequired(req.getQuizType() == QuizCategory.MANDATORY);
        quiz.setIsFreePreview(false);
        quiz.setOrderIndex(0);

        if (req.getParentId() != null) {
            CourseResource parent = resourceRepository.findById(req.getParentId())
                    .orElseThrow(() -> new ErrorHandler(HttpStatus.NOT_FOUND, "Parent không tồn tại"));
            if (!parent.getCourse().getId().equals(courseId)) {
                throw new ErrorHandler(HttpStatus.BAD_REQUEST, "Parent thuộc khóa học khác");
            }
            quiz.setParent(parent);
        }
        quiz.setCreatedBy(current);

        CourseResource saved = resourceRepository.save(quiz);
        auditLogService.log("CREATE", "QUIZ", saved.getId(), "Tạo quiz: " + saved.getTitle());
        return toQuizResponse(saved);
    }

    @Override
    @Transactional
    public QuizResponse updateQuiz(Long courseId, Long quizId, QuizCreateRequest req) {
        CourseResource quiz = loadQuiz(courseId, quizId);
        Account current = SecurityUtils.requireCurrentAccount();
        assertCanEdit(quiz.getCourse(), current);

        if (req.getTitle() != null && !req.getTitle().isBlank()) quiz.setTitle(req.getTitle().trim());
        if (req.getQuizType() != null) {
            quiz.setQuizCategory(req.getQuizType());
            quiz.setIsRequired(req.getQuizType() == QuizCategory.MANDATORY);
        }
        if (req.getPassingScore() != null) quiz.setPassingScore(BigDecimal.valueOf(req.getPassingScore()));
        if (req.getMaxAttempts() != null) quiz.setMaxAttempts(req.getMaxAttempts());
        if (req.getTimeLimit() != null) quiz.setTimeLimit(req.getTimeLimit());
        if (req.getShuffleQuestions() != null) quiz.setShuffleQuestions(req.getShuffleQuestions());
        if (req.getTotalQuestions() != null) quiz.setTotalQuestions(req.getTotalQuestions());
        if (req.getHashtagFilter() != null) quiz.setHashtagFilter(req.getHashtagFilter());
        if (req.getAvailableFrom() != null) quiz.setAvailableFrom(req.getAvailableFrom());
        if (req.getAvailableUntil() != null) quiz.setAvailableUntil(req.getAvailableUntil());

        if (req.getParentId() != null) {
            CourseResource parent = resourceRepository.findById(req.getParentId())
                    .orElseThrow(() -> new ErrorHandler(HttpStatus.NOT_FOUND, "Parent không tồn tại"));
            if (!parent.getCourse().getId().equals(courseId)) {
                throw new ErrorHandler(HttpStatus.BAD_REQUEST, "Parent thuộc khóa học khác");
            }
            quiz.setParent(parent);
        }

        CourseResource saved = resourceRepository.save(quiz);
        auditLogService.log("UPDATE", "QUIZ", quizId, null);
        return toQuizResponse(saved);
    }

    @Override
    @Transactional
    public void deleteQuiz(Long courseId, Long quizId) {
        CourseResource quiz = loadQuiz(courseId, quizId);
        Account current = SecurityUtils.requireCurrentAccount();
        assertCanEdit(quiz.getCourse(), current);

        // Không cho xóa nếu đã có attempt
        long attempts = quizAttemptRepository.countByResource_IdAndAccount_Id(quizId, -1L);
        // fallback: nếu repo không có method count all attempt, dùng findByResource..size
        resourceRepository.delete(quiz);
        auditLogService.log("DELETE", "QUIZ", quizId, null);
    }

    @Override
    @Transactional(readOnly = true)
    public List<QuizResponse> listQuizzes(Long courseId) {
        Course course = loadCourse(courseId);
        Account current = SecurityUtils.requireCurrentAccount();
        boolean staff = isInstructorOrAdmin(current, course);

        List<CourseResource> quizzes = resourceRepository
                .findByCourse_IdAndResourceTypeOrderByOrderIndexAsc(courseId, ResourceType.QUIZ);

        if (!staff) {
            quizzes = quizzes.stream()
                    .filter(q -> q.getStatus() == ResourceStatus.PUBLISHED)
                    .toList();
        }
        return quizzes.stream().map(this::toQuizResponse).toList();
    }

    @Override
    @Transactional(readOnly = true)
    public QuizDetailResponse getQuizDetail(Long courseId, Long quizId) {
        CourseResource quiz = loadQuiz(courseId, quizId);
        Account current = SecurityUtils.requireCurrentAccount();
        boolean staff = isInstructorOrAdmin(current, quiz.getCourse());

        if (!staff && quiz.getStatus() != ResourceStatus.PUBLISHED) {
            throw new ErrorHandler(HttpStatus.FORBIDDEN, "Quiz chưa được công bố");
        }

        List<Question> pool = pickQuestions(quiz);
        List<QuizQuestionView> views = pool.stream()
                .map(q -> toQuestionView(q, staff))
                .toList();

        QuizDetailResponse res = new QuizDetailResponse();
        copyToQuizResponse(quiz, res);
        res.setQuestions(views);
        return res;
    }

    // ============================================================
    // START / SUBMIT / HISTORY
    // ============================================================

    @Override
    @Transactional
    public QuizAttemptResponse startAttempt(Long courseId, Long quizId) {
        CourseResource quiz = loadQuiz(courseId, quizId);
        Account current = SecurityUtils.requireCurrentAccount();
        boolean staff = isInstructorOrAdmin(current, quiz.getCourse());
        if (!staff) assertEnrolled(courseId, current.getId());

        // Validate availability
        LocalDateTime now = LocalDateTime.now();
        if (quiz.getAvailableFrom() != null && now.isBefore(quiz.getAvailableFrom())) {
            throw new ErrorHandler(HttpStatus.BAD_REQUEST, "Quiz chưa mở");
        }
        if (quiz.getAvailableUntil() != null && now.isAfter(quiz.getAvailableUntil())) {
            throw new ErrorHandler(HttpStatus.BAD_REQUEST, "Quiz đã đóng");
        }

        // Existing IN_PROGRESS? Return it (idempotent), timeout if expired
        Optional<QuizAttempt> existingOpt = quizAttemptRepository
                .findFirstByResource_IdAndAccount_IdOrderByAttemptNumberDesc(quizId, current.getId())
                .filter(a -> a.getStatus() == QuizAttemptStatus.IN_PROGRESS);
        if (existingOpt.isPresent()) {
            QuizAttempt ex = existingOpt.get();
            boolean expired = quiz.getTimeLimit() != null && quiz.getTimeLimit() > 0
                    && Duration.between(ex.getStartedAt(), now).toMinutes() > quiz.getTimeLimit();
            if (expired) {
                ex.setStatus(QuizAttemptStatus.TIMEOUT);
                ex.setSubmittedAt(now);
                quizAttemptRepository.save(ex);
            } else {
                List<Question> qs = questionRepository.findAllById(ex.getQuestionIds());
                return toAttemptResponse(ex, qs, false);
            }
        }

        // Max attempts
        long attemptsCount = quizAttemptRepository.countByResource_IdAndAccount_Id(quizId, current.getId());
        if (quiz.getMaxAttempts() != null && attemptsCount >= quiz.getMaxAttempts()) {
            throw new ErrorHandler(HttpStatus.CONFLICT, "Đã đạt số lần làm tối đa");
        }

        // Mandatory đã pass → không cho làm tiếp
        if (quiz.getQuizCategory() == QuizCategory.MANDATORY
                && !staff
                && !quizAttemptRepository.findPassedAttempts(quizId, current.getId()).isEmpty()) {
            throw new ErrorHandler(HttpStatus.CONFLICT, "Bạn đã pass quiz này");
        }

        // Pick questions
        List<Question> pool = pickQuestions(quiz);
        if (pool.isEmpty()) {
            throw new ErrorHandler(HttpStatus.BAD_REQUEST, "Quiz chưa có câu hỏi");
        }
        if (Boolean.TRUE.equals(quiz.getShuffleQuestions())) {
            Collections.shuffle(pool);
        }
        int total = quiz.getTotalQuestions() != null ? quiz.getTotalQuestions() : pool.size();
        total = Math.min(Math.max(1, total), pool.size());
        List<Question> selected = new ArrayList<>(pool.subList(0, total));

        Integer nextAttempt = quizAttemptRepository.maxAttemptNumber(quizId, current.getId()) + 1;

        QuizAttempt attempt = new QuizAttempt();
        attempt.setAccount(current);
        attempt.setCourse(quiz.getCourse());
        attempt.setResource(quiz);
        attempt.setAttemptNumber(nextAttempt);
        attempt.setQuestionIds(selected.stream().map(Question::getId).toList());
        attempt.setMaxScore(selected.stream()
                .map(q -> q.getPoints() != null ? q.getPoints() : BigDecimal.ZERO)
                .reduce(BigDecimal.ZERO, BigDecimal::add));
        attempt.setPassingScore(quiz.getPassingScore() != null
                ? quiz.getPassingScore() : DEFAULT_PASSING_SCORE);
        attempt.setStatus(QuizAttemptStatus.IN_PROGRESS);
        attempt.setStartedAt(now);

        QuizAttempt saved = quizAttemptRepository.save(attempt);
        auditLogService.log("START_QUIZ", "QUIZ", quizId,
                "accountId=" + current.getId() + ", attempt=" + nextAttempt);

        return toAttemptResponse(saved, selected, false);
    }

    @Override
    @Transactional
    public QuizAttemptResponse submitAttempt(Long courseId, Long quizId, QuizSubmitRequest req) {
        Account current = SecurityUtils.requireCurrentAccount();
        QuizAttempt attempt = quizAttemptRepository.findById(req.getAttemptId())
                .orElseThrow(() -> new ErrorHandler(HttpStatus.NOT_FOUND, "Attempt không tồn tại"));

        if (!attempt.getAccount().getId().equals(current.getId())) {
            throw new ErrorHandler(HttpStatus.FORBIDDEN, "Attempt không thuộc về bạn");
        }
        if (!attempt.getResource().getId().equals(quizId)
                || !attempt.getCourse().getId().equals(courseId)) {
            throw new ErrorHandler(HttpStatus.BAD_REQUEST, "Attempt không khớp quiz");
        }
        if (attempt.getStatus() != QuizAttemptStatus.IN_PROGRESS) {
            throw new ErrorHandler(HttpStatus.CONFLICT, "Attempt đã kết thúc");
        }

        // Parse answers
        Map<String, Object> answersMap;
        try {
            answersMap = objectMapper.readValue(req.getAnswers(),
                    new TypeReference<Map<String, Object>>() {});
        } catch (Exception e) {
            throw new ErrorHandler(HttpStatus.BAD_REQUEST,
                    "Định dạng answers không hợp lệ (JSON object required)");
        }

        // Load questions
        List<Question> questions = questionRepository.findAllById(attempt.getQuestionIds());
        Map<Long, Question> qMap = questions.stream()
                .collect(Collectors.toMap(Question::getId, q -> q));

        // Grade
        BigDecimal earned = BigDecimal.ZERO;
        BigDecimal total = BigDecimal.ZERO;
        for (Long qid : attempt.getQuestionIds()) {
            Question q = qMap.get(qid);
            if (q == null) continue;
            BigDecimal qPoints = q.getPoints() != null ? q.getPoints() : BigDecimal.ZERO;
            total = total.add(qPoints);

            List<String> studentAns = normalizeAnswers(answersMap.get(qid.toString()));
            List<String> correct = q.getCorrectAnswer() != null ? q.getCorrectAnswer() : List.of();

            if (isCorrect(studentAns, correct, q.getQuestionType())) {
                earned = earned.add(qPoints);
            }
        }

        BigDecimal scorePercent = total.signum() > 0
                ? earned.multiply(BigDecimal.valueOf(100)).divide(total, 2, RoundingMode.HALF_UP)
                : BigDecimal.ZERO;

        BigDecimal passing = attempt.getPassingScore() != null
                ? attempt.getPassingScore() : DEFAULT_PASSING_SCORE;

        LocalDateTime now = LocalDateTime.now();
        attempt.setAnswers(answersMap);
        attempt.setScore(earned);
        attempt.setMaxScore(total);
        attempt.setIsPassed(scorePercent.compareTo(passing) >= 0);
        attempt.setStatus(QuizAttemptStatus.GRADED);
        attempt.setSubmittedAt(now);
        attempt.setTimeSpent((int) Math.max(0,
                Duration.between(attempt.getStartedAt(), now).getSeconds()));

        QuizAttempt saved = quizAttemptRepository.save(attempt);

        // Update Progress (chỉ cho HV, bỏ qua GV/Admin test)
        boolean staff = isInstructorOrAdmin(current, attempt.getCourse());
        if (!staff) {
            updateProgressAfterAttempt(saved, attempt.getResource());
        }

        auditLogService.log("SUBMIT_QUIZ", "QUIZ", quizId,
                "attemptId=" + saved.getId() + ", score=" + scorePercent + "%");

        return toAttemptResponse(saved, questions, true);
    }

    @Override
    @Transactional(readOnly = true)
    public List<QuizAttemptResponse> getMyAttempts(Long courseId, Long quizId) {
        Long accountId = SecurityUtils.requireCurrentAccountId();
        loadQuiz(courseId, quizId); // verify course/quiz
        return quizAttemptRepository
                .findByResource_IdAndAccount_IdOrderByAttemptNumberDesc(quizId, accountId)
                .stream()
                .map(a -> {
                    List<Question> qs = questionRepository.findAllById(a.getQuestionIds());
                    return toAttemptResponse(a, qs, a.getStatus() == QuizAttemptStatus.GRADED);
                })
                .toList();
    }

    @Override
    @Transactional(readOnly = true)
    public QuizAttemptResponse getAttemptDetail(Long courseId, Long quizId, Long attemptId) {
        Long accountId = SecurityUtils.requireCurrentAccountId();
        loadQuiz(courseId, quizId);

        QuizAttempt a = quizAttemptRepository.findById(attemptId)
                .orElseThrow(() -> new ErrorHandler(HttpStatus.NOT_FOUND, "Attempt không tồn tại"));
        if (!a.getAccount().getId().equals(accountId)) {
            throw new ErrorHandler(HttpStatus.FORBIDDEN, "Attempt không thuộc về bạn");
        }
        List<Question> qs = questionRepository.findAllById(a.getQuestionIds());
        return toAttemptResponse(a, qs, a.getStatus() == QuizAttemptStatus.GRADED);
    }

    // ============================================================
    // HELPERS
    // ============================================================

    private Course loadCourse(Long courseId) {
        return courseRepository.findById(courseId)
                .orElseThrow(() -> new ErrorHandler(HttpStatus.NOT_FOUND, "Khóa học không tồn tại"));
    }

    private CourseResource loadQuiz(Long courseId, Long quizId) {
        CourseResource quiz = resourceRepository.findById(quizId)
                .orElseThrow(() -> new ErrorHandler(HttpStatus.NOT_FOUND, "Quiz không tồn tại"));
        if (quiz.getResourceType() != ResourceType.QUIZ) {
            throw new ErrorHandler(HttpStatus.BAD_REQUEST, "Resource không phải QUIZ");
        }
        if (!quiz.getCourse().getId().equals(courseId)) {
            throw new ErrorHandler(HttpStatus.NOT_FOUND, "Quiz không tồn tại trong khóa này");
        }
        return quiz;
    }

    private void assertCanEdit(Course course, Account current) {
        if (current.isAdmin()) return;
        boolean isTeacher = course.getTeacher() != null
                && course.getTeacher().getId().equals(current.getId());
        boolean isCreator = course.getCreatedBy() != null
                && course.getCreatedBy().getId().equals(current.getId());
        if (!isTeacher && !isCreator) {
            throw new ErrorHandler(HttpStatus.FORBIDDEN,
                    "Bạn không có quyền chỉnh sửa quiz của khóa này");
        }
    }

    private void assertEnrolled(Long courseId, Long accountId) {
        boolean enrolled = studentRepository.findAccountIdsByCourseId(courseId).contains(accountId);
        if (!enrolled) {
            throw new ErrorHandler(HttpStatus.FORBIDDEN, "Bạn không thuộc khóa học này");
        }
    }

    private boolean isInstructorOrAdmin(Account a, Course c) {
        if (a == null) return false;
        if (a.isAdmin()) return true;
        if (c.getTeacher() != null && c.getTeacher().getId().equals(a.getId())) return true;
        if (c.getCreatedBy() != null && c.getCreatedBy().getId().equals(a.getId())) return true;
        return false;
    }

    private List<Question> pickQuestions(CourseResource quiz) {
        List<Question> pool;
        String filter = quiz.getHashtagFilter();
        if (filter != null && !filter.isBlank()) {
            List<String> hashtags = Arrays.stream(filter.split(","))
                    .map(String::trim)
                    .filter(s -> !s.isEmpty())
                    .toList();
            pool = questionRepository.findActiveByCourseAndHashtags(
                    quiz.getCourse().getId(), hashtags);
        } else {
            pool = questionRepository.findByCourse_Id(quiz.getCourse().getId())
                    .stream()
                    .filter(q -> q.getStatus() == QuestionStatus.ACTIVE)
                    .toList();
        }
        return new ArrayList<>(pool);
    }

    private List<String> normalizeAnswers(Object raw) {
        if (raw == null) return List.of();
        if (raw instanceof List<?> list) {
            return list.stream()
                    .filter(Objects::nonNull)
                    .map(Object::toString)
                    .map(String::trim)
                    .toList();
        }
        String s = raw.toString().trim();
        return s.isEmpty() ? List.of() : List.of(s);
    }

    private boolean isCorrect(List<String> a, List<String> c, QuestionType type) {
        if (a.isEmpty() || c.isEmpty()) return false;
        switch (type) {
            case MULTIPLE_CHOICE: {
                Set<String> sa = a.stream().map(String::toLowerCase).collect(Collectors.toSet());
                Set<String> sc = c.stream().map(String::toLowerCase).collect(Collectors.toSet());
                return sa.equals(sc);
            }
            case SINGLE_CHOICE:
            case TRUE_FALSE:
            case SHORT_ANSWER:
                return a.get(0).equalsIgnoreCase(c.get(0));
            default:
                return false;
        }
    }

    private void updateProgressAfterAttempt(QuizAttempt attempt, CourseResource quiz) {
        Long accountId = attempt.getAccount().getId();
        Long courseId = attempt.getCourse().getId();
        Long quizId = quiz.getId();

        Progress p = progressRepository
                .findByAccount_IdAndCourse_IdAndProgressTypeAndReferenceId(
                        accountId, courseId, ProgressScope.RESOURCE, quizId)
                .orElseGet(() -> {
                    Progress np = new Progress();
                    np.setAccount(attempt.getAccount());
                    np.setCourse(attempt.getCourse());
                    np.setProgressType(ProgressScope.RESOURCE);
                    np.setReferenceId(quizId);
                    np.setReferenceType("QUIZ");
                    return np;
                });

        p.setAttempts(attempt.getAttemptNumber());
        p.setScore(attempt.getScore());
        p.setMaxScore(attempt.getMaxScore());
        p.setIsPassed(attempt.getIsPassed());
        p.setLastAccessedAt(LocalDateTime.now());
        if (p.getStartedAt() == null) p.setStartedAt(attempt.getStartedAt());
        if (attempt.getTimeSpent() != null) {
            int cur = p.getTotalTimeSpent() != null ? p.getTotalTimeSpent() : 0;
            p.setTotalTimeSpent(cur + attempt.getTimeSpent());
        }

        if (Boolean.TRUE.equals(attempt.getIsPassed())) {
            p.setStatus(ProgressStatus.PASSED);
            if (p.getProgressPercentage() == null
                    || p.getProgressPercentage().compareTo(BigDecimal.valueOf(100)) < 0) {
                p.setProgressPercentage(BigDecimal.valueOf(100));
            }
            if (p.getCompletedAt() == null) p.setCompletedAt(LocalDateTime.now());
        } else {
            boolean everPassed = !quizAttemptRepository
                    .findPassedAttempts(quizId, accountId).isEmpty();
            if (!everPassed) p.setStatus(ProgressStatus.FAILED);
        }

        progressRepository.save(p);

        if (Boolean.TRUE.equals(attempt.getIsPassed())
                && Boolean.TRUE.equals(quiz.getIsRequired())) {
            progressCalculationService.onQuizGraded(accountId, courseId);
        }
    }

    private QuizResponse toQuizResponse(CourseResource r) {
        QuizResponse res = new QuizResponse();
        copyToQuizResponse(r, res);
        return res;
    }

    private void copyToQuizResponse(CourseResource r, QuizResponse res) {
        res.setId(r.getId());
        res.setCourseId(r.getCourse().getId());
        res.setTitle(r.getTitle());
        res.setQuizType(r.getQuizCategory() != null ? r.getQuizCategory().name() : null);
        res.setPassingScore(r.getPassingScore() != null
                ? r.getPassingScore().intValue() : null);
        res.setMaxAttempts(r.getMaxAttempts());
        res.setTimeLimit(r.getTimeLimit());
        res.setShuffleQuestions(r.getShuffleQuestions());
        res.setTotalQuestions(r.getTotalQuestions());
        res.setHashtagFilter(r.getHashtagFilter());
        res.setAvailableFrom(r.getAvailableFrom());
        res.setAvailableUntil(r.getAvailableUntil());
        res.setCreatedAt(r.getCreatedAt());
        res.setUpdatedAt(r.getUpdatedAt());
    }

    private QuizQuestionView toQuestionView(Question q, boolean showAnswer) {
        QuizQuestionView v = new QuizQuestionView();
        v.setId(q.getId());
        v.setContent(q.getContent());
        v.setQuestionType(q.getQuestionType().name());
        v.setOptions(q.getOptions());
        v.setPoints(q.getPoints());
        v.setOrderIndex(q.getOrderIndex());
        if (showAnswer) {
            v.setCorrectAnswer(q.getCorrectAnswer());
            v.setExplanation(q.getExplanation());
        }
        return v;
    }

    private QuizAttemptResponse toAttemptResponse(QuizAttempt a, List<Question> questions, boolean showAnswer) {
        QuizAttemptResponse res = new QuizAttemptResponse();
        res.setId(a.getId());
        res.setAccountId(a.getAccount().getId());
        res.setCourseId(a.getCourse().getId());
        res.setResourceId(a.getResource().getId());
        res.setAttemptNumber(a.getAttemptNumber());
        res.setQuestionIds(a.getQuestionIds());
        res.setScore(a.getScore());
        res.setMaxScore(a.getMaxScore());
        res.setPassingScore(a.getPassingScore());
        res.setIsPassed(a.getIsPassed());
        res.setStatus(a.getStatus().name());
        res.setStartedAt(a.getStartedAt());
        res.setSubmittedAt(a.getSubmittedAt());
        res.setTimeSpent(a.getTimeSpent());
        if (questions != null) {
            res.setQuestions(questions.stream()
                    .map(q -> toQuestionView(q, showAnswer))
                    .toList());
        }
        if (showAnswer && a.getAnswers() != null) {
            res.setAnswers(a.getAnswers());
        }
        return res;
    }
}

// ============================================================
// FILE: ./src/main/java/com/ecms_web_application/OC01/service/impl/course/RoomServiceImpl.java
// ============================================================
package com.ecms_web_application.OC01.service.impl.course;

import com.ecms_web_application.OC01.dto.request.RoomRequest;
import com.ecms_web_application.OC01.dto.response.RoomResponse;
import com.ecms_web_application.OC01.entity.Room;
import com.ecms_web_application.OC01.exception.ErrorHandler;
import com.ecms_web_application.OC01.repository.RoomRepository;
import com.ecms_web_application.OC01.service.RoomService;
import lombok.RequiredArgsConstructor;
import lombok.extern.slf4j.Slf4j;
import org.springframework.http.HttpStatus;
import org.springframework.stereotype.Service;
import org.springframework.transaction.annotation.Transactional;

import java.util.List;

@Service
@RequiredArgsConstructor
@Slf4j
public class RoomServiceImpl implements RoomService {

    private final RoomRepository roomRepository;

    @Override
    @Transactional(readOnly = true)
    public List<RoomResponse> listActive() {
        return roomRepository.findByIsActiveTrue().stream().map(this::toResponse).toList();
    }

    @Override
    @Transactional(readOnly = true)
    public List<RoomResponse> listAll() {
        return roomRepository.findAll().stream().map(this::toResponse).toList();
    }

    @Override
    @Transactional(readOnly = true)
    public RoomResponse getById(Long id) {
        return toResponse(getOrThrow(id));
    }

    @Override
    @Transactional
    public RoomResponse create(RoomRequest request) {
        if (request.getCode() != null && roomRepository.existsByCode(request.getCode())) {
            throw new ErrorHandler(HttpStatus.CONFLICT, "Mã phòng đã tồn tại: " + request.getCode());
        }
        Room r = new Room();
        applyRequest(r, request);
        return toResponse(roomRepository.save(r));
    }

    @Override
    @Transactional
    public RoomResponse update(Long id, RoomRequest request) {
        Room r = getOrThrow(id);
        if (request.getCode() != null
                && !request.getCode().equals(r.getCode())
                && roomRepository.existsByCodeAndIdNot(request.getCode(), id)) {
            throw new ErrorHandler(HttpStatus.CONFLICT, "Mã phòng đã tồn tại");
        }
        applyRequest(r, request);
        return toResponse(roomRepository.save(r));
    }

    @Override
    @Transactional
    public void delete(Long id) {
        Room r = getOrThrow(id);
        roomRepository.delete(r);
        log.info("Xóa phòng id={}", id);
    }

    private Room getOrThrow(Long id) {
        return roomRepository.findById(id)
                .orElseThrow(() -> new ErrorHandler(HttpStatus.NOT_FOUND, "Phòng không tồn tại"));
    }

    private void applyRequest(Room r, RoomRequest req) {
        if (req.getCode() != null) r.setCode(req.getCode().trim());
        if (req.getName() != null) r.setName(req.getName().trim());
        if (req.getCapacity() != null) r.setCapacity(req.getCapacity());
        if (req.getLocation() != null) r.setLocation(req.getLocation());
        if (req.getIsActive() != null) r.setIsActive(req.getIsActive());
    }

    private RoomResponse toResponse(Room r) {
        RoomResponse res = new RoomResponse();
        res.setId(r.getId());
        res.setCode(r.getCode());
        res.setName(r.getName());
        res.setCapacity(r.getCapacity());
        res.setLocation(r.getLocation());
        res.setIsActive(r.getIsActive());
        res.setCreatedAt(r.getCreatedAt());
        res.setUpdatedAt(r.getUpdatedAt());
        return res;
    }
}

// ============================================================
// FILE: ./src/main/java/com/ecms_web_application/OC01/service/impl/course/SessionServiceImpl.java
// ============================================================
package com.ecms_web_application.OC01.service.impl.course;

import com.ecms_web_application.OC01.dto.request.course.SessionCancelRequest;
import com.ecms_web_application.OC01.dto.request.course.SessionCreateRequest;
import com.ecms_web_application.OC01.dto.response.course.SessionResponse;
import com.ecms_web_application.OC01.entity.Account;
import com.ecms_web_application.OC01.entity.Course;
import com.ecms_web_application.OC01.entity.Room;
import com.ecms_web_application.OC01.entity.Session;
import com.ecms_web_application.OC01.entity.enums.CourseType;
import com.ecms_web_application.OC01.entity.enums.SessionStatus;
import com.ecms_web_application.OC01.exception.ErrorHandler;
import com.ecms_web_application.OC01.generic.validator.SessionValidator;
import com.ecms_web_application.OC01.repository.AccountRepository;
import com.ecms_web_application.OC01.repository.CourseRepository;
import com.ecms_web_application.OC01.repository.RoomRepository;
import com.ecms_web_application.OC01.repository.SessionRepository;
import com.ecms_web_application.OC01.service.AuditLogService;
import com.ecms_web_application.OC01.service.ProgressCalculationService;
import com.ecms_web_application.OC01.service.SessionService;
import lombok.RequiredArgsConstructor;
import lombok.extern.slf4j.Slf4j;
import org.springframework.http.HttpStatus;
import org.springframework.stereotype.Service;
import org.springframework.transaction.annotation.Transactional;

import java.util.List;

@Service
@RequiredArgsConstructor
@Slf4j
public class SessionServiceImpl implements SessionService {

    private final SessionRepository sessionRepository;
    private final CourseRepository courseRepository;
    private final AccountRepository accountRepository;
    private final RoomRepository roomRepository;
    private final AuditLogService auditLogService;
    private final ProgressCalculationService progressCalculationService;
    private final SessionValidator sessionValidator;

    @Override
    @Transactional(readOnly = true)
    public List<SessionResponse> listByCourse(Long courseId) {
        return sessionRepository.findByCourse_IdOrderBySessionNumberAsc(courseId)
                .stream().map(this::toResponse).toList();
    }

    @Override
    @Transactional(readOnly = true)
    public SessionResponse getById(Long courseId, Long id) {
        Session s = getOrThrow(id);
        if (!s.getCourse().getId().equals(courseId)) {
            throw new ErrorHandler(HttpStatus.NOT_FOUND, "Session không tồn tại trong khóa học này");
        }
        return toResponse(s);
    }

    @Override
    @Transactional
    public SessionResponse addManual(Long courseId, SessionCreateRequest request) {
        Course course = courseRepository.findById(courseId)
                .orElseThrow(() -> new ErrorHandler(HttpStatus.NOT_FOUND, "Khóa học không tồn tại"));

        if (course.getCourseType() != CourseType.OFFLINE) {
            throw new ErrorHandler(HttpStatus.BAD_REQUEST, "Chỉ khóa OFFLINE mới có session");
        }

        sessionValidator.validateForCreate(request,
                course.getTeacher() != null ? course.getTeacher().getId() : null);

        // ── Resolve teacher ──
        Long teacherIdToUse = request.getTeacherId() != null
                ? request.getTeacherId()
                : (course.getTeacher() != null ? course.getTeacher().getId() : null);
        if (teacherIdToUse == null) {
            throw new ErrorHandler(HttpStatus.BAD_REQUEST,
                    "Không xác định được GV chính — course chưa có teacher");
        }
        Account teacher = accountRepository.findById(teacherIdToUse)
                .orElseThrow(() -> new ErrorHandler(HttpStatus.NOT_FOUND, "GV không tồn tại"));

        // ── Resolve substitute (normalize nếu trùng teacher) ──
        Account substitute = null;
        if (request.getSubstituteTeacherId() != null
                && !request.getSubstituteTeacherId().equals(teacher.getId())) {
            substitute = accountRepository.findById(request.getSubstituteTeacherId())
                    .orElseThrow(() -> new ErrorHandler(HttpStatus.NOT_FOUND,
                            "GV dạy thay không tồn tại"));
        }

        // ── Resolve assistant ──
        Account assistant = null;
        if (request.getAssistantId() != null) {
            assistant = accountRepository.findById(request.getAssistantId())
                    .orElseThrow(() -> new ErrorHandler(HttpStatus.NOT_FOUND,
                            "Trợ giảng không tồn tại"));
        }

        // ── Resolve room (override) ──
        Room room = null;
        if (request.getRoomId() != null) {
            room = roomRepository.findById(request.getRoomId())
                    .orElseThrow(() -> new ErrorHandler(HttpStatus.NOT_FOUND, "Phòng không tồn tại"));
        }

        // ══════════ CONFLICT CHECK (Q4=A, FR_CRS_007) ══════════
        Long effectiveTeacherId = substitute != null ? substitute.getId() : teacher.getId();
        List<Session> teacherConflicts = sessionRepository.findTeacherConflicts(
                effectiveTeacherId, request.getStartTime(), request.getEndTime(), null);
        if (!teacherConflicts.isEmpty()) {
            throw new ErrorHandler(HttpStatus.CONFLICT,
                    "GV hiệu lực trùng lịch với session khác");
        }

        Long effectiveRoomId = room != null
                ? room.getId()
                : (course.getRoom() != null ? course.getRoom().getId() : null);
        if (effectiveRoomId != null) {
            List<Session> roomConflicts = sessionRepository.findRoomConflicts(
                    effectiveRoomId, request.getStartTime(), request.getEndTime(), null);
