            if (!roomConflicts.isEmpty()) {
                throw new ErrorHandler(HttpStatus.CONFLICT,
                        "Phòng hiệu lực trùng lịch với session khác");
            }
        }

        Integer nextNum = sessionRepository.maxSessionNumber(courseId) + 1;

        Session s = new Session();
        s.setCourse(course);
        s.setSessionNumber(nextNum);
        s.setTitle(request.getTitle());
        s.setDescription(request.getDescription());
        s.setStartTime(request.getStartTime());
        s.setEndTime(request.getEndTime());
        s.setSessionDate(request.getStartTime().toLocalDate());
        s.setTeacher(teacher);
        s.setSubstituteTeacher(substitute);
        s.setAssistant(assistant);
        s.setRoom(room);
        s.setNote(request.getNote());
        s.setStatus(SessionStatus.SCHEDULED);

        Session saved = sessionRepository.save(s);
        auditLogService.log("CREATE", "SESSION", saved.getId(),
                "Thêm session #" + nextNum + " cho course " + courseId);
        return toResponse(saved);
    }

    @Override
    @Transactional
    public SessionResponse cancel(Long id, SessionCancelRequest request) {
        Session s = getOrThrow(id);
        if (s.getStatus() == SessionStatus.CANCELLED) {
            throw new ErrorHandler(HttpStatus.CONFLICT, "Session đã hủy");
        }
        if (s.getStatus() == SessionStatus.DONE) {
            throw new ErrorHandler(HttpStatus.CONFLICT, "Không thể hủy session đã DONE");
        }
        s.setStatus(SessionStatus.CANCELLED);
        s.setCancelReason(request.getReason());
        Session saved = sessionRepository.save(s);
        auditLogService.log("CANCEL", "SESSION", id, request.getReason());
        progressCalculationService.onSessionChanged(s.getCourse().getId());
        return toResponse(saved);
    }

    @Override
    @Transactional
    public SessionResponse markDone(Long sessionId) {
        Session s = getOrThrow(sessionId);
        if (s.getStatus() == SessionStatus.CANCELLED) {
            throw new ErrorHandler(HttpStatus.CONFLICT, "Session đã hủy");
        }
        if (s.getStatus() == SessionStatus.RESCHEDULED) {
            throw new ErrorHandler(HttpStatus.CONFLICT, "Session đã được thay thế");
        }
        if (s.getStatus() == SessionStatus.DONE) {
            return toResponse(s); // idempotent
        }
        s.setStatus(SessionStatus.DONE);
        Session saved = sessionRepository.save(s);
        auditLogService.log("MARK_DONE", "SESSION", sessionId, null);
        progressCalculationService.onSessionChanged(s.getCourse().getId());
        return toResponse(saved);
    }

    @Override
    @Transactional
    public SessionResponse assignSubstituteTeacher(Long sessionId, Long substituteTeacherId) {
        Session s = getOrThrow(sessionId);
        if (s.getStatus() == SessionStatus.CANCELLED || s.getStatus() == SessionStatus.DONE) {
            throw new ErrorHandler(HttpStatus.CONFLICT,
                    "Không thể gán GV dạy thay cho session đã " + s.getStatus());
        }

        if (substituteTeacherId == null) {
            s.setSubstituteTeacher(null);
        } else if (s.getTeacher() != null && substituteTeacherId.equals(s.getTeacher().getId())) {
            s.setSubstituteTeacher(null); // normalize về null (BA §5.10)
        } else {
            Account sub = accountRepository.findById(substituteTeacherId)
                    .orElseThrow(() -> new ErrorHandler(HttpStatus.NOT_FOUND,
                            "GV dạy thay không tồn tại"));
            List<Session> conflicts = sessionRepository.findTeacherConflicts(
                    substituteTeacherId, s.getStartTime(), s.getEndTime(), s.getId());
            if (!conflicts.isEmpty()) {
                throw new ErrorHandler(HttpStatus.CONFLICT,
                        "GV dạy thay trùng lịch với session khác");
            }
            s.setSubstituteTeacher(sub);
        }

        Session saved = sessionRepository.save(s);
        auditLogService.log("ASSIGN_SUBSTITUTE", "SESSION", sessionId,
                "substituteTeacherId=" + substituteTeacherId);
        return toResponse(saved);
    }

    @Override
    @Transactional
    public SessionResponse assignAssistant(Long sessionId, Long assistantId) {
        Session s = getOrThrow(sessionId);
        if (s.getStatus() == SessionStatus.CANCELLED) {
            throw new ErrorHandler(HttpStatus.CONFLICT,
                    "Không thể gán trợ giảng cho session đã hủy");
        }
        if (assistantId == null) {
            s.setAssistant(null);
        } else {
            Account a = accountRepository.findById(assistantId)
                    .orElseThrow(() -> new ErrorHandler(HttpStatus.NOT_FOUND,
                            "Trợ giảng không tồn tại"));
            s.setAssistant(a);
        }
        Session saved = sessionRepository.save(s);
        auditLogService.log("ASSIGN_ASSISTANT", "SESSION", sessionId,
                "assistantId=" + assistantId);
        return toResponse(saved);
    }

    @Override
    @Transactional
    public void changeCourseMainTeacher(Long courseId, Long newTeacherId) {
        Course course = courseRepository.findById(courseId)
                .orElseThrow(() -> new ErrorHandler(HttpStatus.NOT_FOUND, "Khóa học không tồn tại"));
        Account newTeacher = accountRepository.findById(newTeacherId)
                .orElseThrow(() -> new ErrorHandler(HttpStatus.NOT_FOUND, "GV không tồn tại"));

        Account oldTeacher = course.getTeacher();
        if (oldTeacher != null && oldTeacher.getId().equals(newTeacherId)) {
            log.info("Đổi GV chính course {}: GV mới trùng GV cũ — bỏ qua", courseId);
            return;
        }

        // 1. Update course.teacher_id
        course.setTeacher(newTeacher);
        courseRepository.save(course);

        // 2. Sync session SCHEDULED chưa có dạy thay (BA §5.10)
        List<Session> scheduled = sessionRepository.findScheduledSessionsWithoutSubstitute(courseId);
        for (Session s : scheduled) {
            s.setTeacher(newTeacher);
        }
        if (!scheduled.isEmpty()) {
            sessionRepository.saveAll(scheduled);
        }

        // 3. Audit log
        auditLogService.log("CHANGE_MAIN_TEACHER", "COURSE", courseId,
                String.format("oldTeacher=%s, newTeacher=%s, sessionsSynced=%d",
                        oldTeacher != null ? oldTeacher.getId() : null,
                        newTeacherId, scheduled.size()));
        log.info("Đổi GV chính course {}: {} → {}, sync {} session",
                courseId,
                oldTeacher != null ? oldTeacher.getId() : null,
                newTeacherId, scheduled.size());
    }

    @Override
    @Transactional
    public void delete(Long id) {
        Session s = getOrThrow(id);
        if (s.getStatus() == SessionStatus.DONE) {
            throw new ErrorHandler(HttpStatus.CONFLICT, "Không thể xóa session đã DONE");
        }
        sessionRepository.delete(s);
        auditLogService.log("DELETE", "SESSION", id, null);
    }

    @Override
    @Transactional
    public com.ecms_web_application.OC01.dto.response.course.SessionGenerationResult generateSessions(Long courseId) {
        Course course = courseRepository.findById(courseId)
                .orElseThrow(() -> new ErrorHandler(HttpStatus.NOT_FOUND, "Khóa học không tồn tại"));

        if (course.getCourseType() != CourseType.OFFLINE) {
            throw new ErrorHandler(HttpStatus.BAD_REQUEST,
                    "Chỉ sinh session cho khóa OFFLINE");
        }
        if (course.getStartDate() == null || course.getEndDate() == null) {
            throw new ErrorHandler(HttpStatus.BAD_REQUEST, "Course thiếu start_date/end_date");
        }
        if (course.getScheduleDays() == null || course.getScheduleDays().isEmpty()) {
            throw new ErrorHandler(HttpStatus.BAD_REQUEST, "Course thiếu schedule_days");
        }
        if (course.getSessionStartTime() == null || course.getSessionEndTime() == null) {
            throw new ErrorHandler(HttpStatus.BAD_REQUEST, "Course thiếu session_start/end_time");
        }
        if (course.getTeacher() == null) {
            throw new ErrorHandler(HttpStatus.BAD_REQUEST,
                    "Course chưa có teacher_id — không thể sinh session");
        }
        if (course.getRoom() == null) {
            throw new ErrorHandler(HttpStatus.BAD_REQUEST,
                    "Course chưa có room_id — không thể sinh session");
        }

        // Idempotent: đã có session → skip
        long existingCount = sessionRepository.countByCourse_Id(courseId);
        if (existingCount > 0) {
            return com.ecms_web_application.OC01.dto.response.course.SessionGenerationResult.builder()
                    .courseId(courseId)
                    .totalDates(0)
                    .generated(0)
                    .message("Course đã có " + existingCount + " session — không sinh lại")
                    .build();
        }

        // Danh sách ngày khớp schedule_days
        java.util.List<java.time.LocalDate> dates = new java.util.ArrayList<>();
        java.time.LocalDate cur = course.getStartDate();
        while (!cur.isAfter(course.getEndDate())) {
            int dow = (cur.getDayOfWeek().getValue() % 7) + 1; // 1=CN..7=T7
            if (course.getScheduleDays().contains(dow)) {
                dates.add(cur);
            }
            cur = cur.plusDays(1);
        }
        if (dates.isEmpty()) {
            throw new ErrorHandler(HttpStatus.BAD_REQUEST,
                    "Không có ngày nào khớp schedule_days trong khoảng start..end");
        }
        if (dates.size() > 500) {
            throw new ErrorHandler(HttpStatus.BAD_REQUEST,
                    "Số session cần sinh (" + dates.size() + ") vượt quá giới hạn 500");
        }

        // Sinh + conflict check
        java.util.List<Session> toSave = new java.util.ArrayList<>();
        java.util.List<com.ecms_web_application.OC01.dto.response.course.SessionGenerationResult.SkippedDate> skipped
                = new java.util.ArrayList<>();
        int counter = 0;

        Long teacherId = course.getTeacher().getId();
        Long roomId = course.getRoom().getId();

        for (java.time.LocalDate date : dates) {
            java.time.LocalDateTime start = java.time.LocalDateTime.of(date, course.getSessionStartTime());
            java.time.LocalDateTime end = java.time.LocalDateTime.of(date, course.getSessionEndTime());

            boolean teacherConflict = !sessionRepository.findTeacherConflicts(
                    teacherId, start, end, null).isEmpty();
            if (teacherConflict) {
                skipped.add(new com.ecms_web_application.OC01.dto.response.course.SessionGenerationResult.SkippedDate(
                        date, "GV bận"));
                continue;
            }
            boolean roomConflict = !sessionRepository.findRoomConflicts(
                    roomId, start, end, null).isEmpty();
            if (roomConflict) {
                skipped.add(new com.ecms_web_application.OC01.dto.response.course.SessionGenerationResult.SkippedDate(
                        date, "Phòng bận"));
                continue;
            }

            Session s = new Session();
            s.setCourse(course);
            s.setSessionNumber(++counter);
            s.setTitle("Buổi " + counter);
            s.setStartTime(start);
            s.setEndTime(end);
            s.setSessionDate(date);
            s.setTeacher(course.getTeacher());
            s.setRoom(null); // kế thừa course.room_id
            s.setStatus(SessionStatus.SCHEDULED);
            toSave.add(s);
        }

        if (toSave.isEmpty()) {
            throw new ErrorHandler(HttpStatus.CONFLICT,
                    "Tất cả " + dates.size() + " ngày đều conflict — không sinh được session nào");
        }

        sessionRepository.saveAll(toSave);
        auditLogService.log("GENERATE_SESSIONS", "COURSE", courseId,
                "generated=" + toSave.size() + ", skipped=" + skipped.size());

        return com.ecms_web_application.OC01.dto.response.course.SessionGenerationResult.builder()
                .courseId(courseId)
                .totalDates(dates.size())
                .generated(toSave.size())
                .skipped(skipped)
                .message("Sinh " + toSave.size() + "/" + dates.size() + " session")
                .build();
    }

    private Session getOrThrow(Long id) {
        return sessionRepository.findById(id)
                .orElseThrow(() -> new ErrorHandler(HttpStatus.NOT_FOUND, "Session không tồn tại"));
    }

    private SessionResponse toResponse(Session s) {
        SessionResponse r = new SessionResponse();
        r.setId(s.getId());
        r.setCourseId(s.getCourse().getId());
        r.setSessionNumber(s.getSessionNumber());
        r.setTitle(s.getTitle());
        r.setDescription(s.getDescription());
        r.setStartTime(s.getStartTime());
        r.setEndTime(s.getEndTime());
        r.setSessionDate(s.getSessionDate());

        if (s.getTeacher() != null) {
            r.setTeacherId(s.getTeacher().getId());
            r.setTeacherName(fullNameOf(s.getTeacher()));
        }
        if (s.getSubstituteTeacher() != null) {
            r.setSubstituteTeacherId(s.getSubstituteTeacher().getId());
            r.setSubstituteTeacherName(fullNameOf(s.getSubstituteTeacher()));
        }
        if (s.getAssistant() != null) {
            r.setAssistantId(s.getAssistant().getId());
            r.setAssistantName(fullNameOf(s.getAssistant()));
        }
        if (s.getRoom() != null) {
            r.setRoomId(s.getRoom().getId());
            r.setRoomName(s.getRoom().getName());
        }

        r.setEffectiveTeacherId(s.getEffectiveTeacherId());
        Account effTeacher = s.getSubstituteTeacher() != null
                ? s.getSubstituteTeacher() : s.getTeacher();
        if (effTeacher != null) r.setEffectiveTeacherName(fullNameOf(effTeacher));

        r.setEffectiveRoomId(s.getEffectiveRoomId());
        Room effRoom = s.getRoom() != null
                ? s.getRoom()
                : (s.getCourse() != null ? s.getCourse().getRoom() : null);
        if (effRoom != null) r.setEffectiveRoomName(effRoom.getName());

        r.setStatus(s.getStatus() != null ? s.getStatus().name() : null);
        r.setCancelReason(s.getCancelReason());
        r.setNote(s.getNote());
        r.setRescheduledFromId(s.getRescheduledFromId());
        r.setCreatedAt(s.getCreatedAt());
        r.setUpdatedAt(s.getUpdatedAt());
        return r;
    }

    private static String fullNameOf(Account a) {
        if (a == null) return null;
        return a.getUserProfile() != null ? a.getUserProfile().getFullName() : a.getEmail();
    }
}

// ============================================================
// FILE: ./src/main/java/com/ecms_web_application/OC01/service/impl/CustomOAuth2UserService.java
// ============================================================
package com.ecms_web_application.OC01.service.impl;

import com.ecms_web_application.OC01.entity.Account;
import com.ecms_web_application.OC01.entity.Role;
import com.ecms_web_application.OC01.entity.UserProfile;
import com.ecms_web_application.OC01.entity.enums.AuthProvider;
import com.ecms_web_application.OC01.exception.ErrorHandler;
import com.ecms_web_application.OC01.repository.AccountRepository;
import com.ecms_web_application.OC01.repository.RoleRepository;
import com.ecms_web_application.OC01.repository.UserProfileRepository;
import lombok.RequiredArgsConstructor;
import lombok.extern.slf4j.Slf4j;
import org.springframework.http.HttpStatus;
import org.springframework.security.oauth2.client.userinfo.DefaultOAuth2UserService;
import org.springframework.security.oauth2.client.userinfo.OAuth2UserRequest;
import org.springframework.security.oauth2.core.OAuth2AuthenticationException;
import org.springframework.security.oauth2.core.user.DefaultOAuth2User;
import org.springframework.security.oauth2.core.user.OAuth2User;
import org.springframework.stereotype.Service;
import org.springframework.transaction.annotation.Transactional;

import java.time.LocalDateTime;
import java.util.Map;

/**
 * OAuth2 login:
 * - Account chưa tồn tại → tạo mới với role STUDENT (KHÔNG gán permission)
 * - Account đã tồn tại → cập nhật provider info + lastLoginAt
 *
 * Admin sẽ gán permission thủ công sau khi cần.
 */
@Service
@RequiredArgsConstructor
@Slf4j
public class CustomOAuth2UserService extends DefaultOAuth2UserService {

    private final AccountRepository accountRepository;
    private final RoleRepository roleRepository;
    private final UserProfileRepository userProfileRepository;

    private static final String DEFAULT_ROLE = "STUDENT";

    @Override
    @Transactional
    public OAuth2User loadUser(OAuth2UserRequest userRequest) throws OAuth2AuthenticationException {
        OAuth2User oAuth2User = super.loadUser(userRequest);

        String registrationId = userRequest.getClientRegistration().getRegistrationId();
        AuthProvider provider = mapToProvider(registrationId);
        Map<String, Object> attributes = oAuth2User.getAttributes();

        String providerId = extractProviderId(attributes);
        String email = (String) attributes.getOrDefault(
                "email",
                provider.name().toLowerCase() + "_" + providerId + "@generated.com"
        );
        String name = (String) attributes.getOrDefault("name", email);
        String picture = (String) attributes.get("picture");

        accountRepository.findByEmail(email.toLowerCase())
                .map(existing -> updateExistingAccount(existing, provider, providerId, name, picture))
                .orElseGet(() -> createNewAccount(email.toLowerCase(), name, picture, provider, providerId));

        return new DefaultOAuth2User(
                oAuth2User.getAuthorities(),
                attributes,
                "email"
        );
    }

    // ============================================================
    // HELPERS
    // ============================================================

    private AuthProvider mapToProvider(String registrationId) {
        return switch (registrationId.toLowerCase()) {
            case "google"   -> AuthProvider.GOOGLE;
            case "facebook" -> AuthProvider.FACEBOOK;
            case "apple"    -> AuthProvider.APPLE;
            default -> throw new IllegalArgumentException("OAuth2 provider không hỗ trợ: " + registrationId);
        };
    }

    private String extractProviderId(Map<String, Object> attributes) {
        Object id = attributes.get("sub");
        if (id == null) id = attributes.get("id");
        return id != null ? id.toString() : null;
    }

    private Account updateExistingAccount(Account account, AuthProvider provider,
                                          String providerId, String name, String picture) {
        boolean changed = false;

        if (providerId != null && !providerId.equals(account.getProviderId())) {
            account.setProviderId(providerId);
            changed = true;
        }
        if (account.getProvider() == AuthProvider.LOCAL || account.getProvider() != provider) {
            account.setProvider(provider);
            changed = true;
        }
        account.setLastLoginAt(LocalDateTime.now());
        account.setUpdatedAt(LocalDateTime.now());

        if (changed) accountRepository.save(account);

        UserProfile profile = account.getUserProfile();
        if (profile != null) {
            boolean profileChanged = false;
            if (name != null && !name.equals(profile.getFullName())) {
                profile.setFullName(name);
                profileChanged = true;
            }
            if (picture != null && !picture.equals(profile.getAvatarUrl())) {
                profile.setAvatarUrl(picture);
                profileChanged = true;
            }
            if (profileChanged) {
                profile.setUpdatedAt(LocalDateTime.now());
                userProfileRepository.save(profile);
            }
        }

        log.info("OAuth2 login — account hiện có: {}", account.getEmail());
        return account;
    }

    private Account createNewAccount(String email, String name, String picture,
                                     AuthProvider provider, String providerId) {
        Role role = roleRepository.findByCode(DEFAULT_ROLE)
                .orElseThrow(() -> new ErrorHandler(HttpStatus.INTERNAL_SERVER_ERROR,
                        "Role " + DEFAULT_ROLE + " không tồn tại"));

        LocalDateTime now = LocalDateTime.now();

        Account account = new Account();
        account.setEmail(email);
        account.setProvider(provider);
        account.setProviderId(providerId);
        account.setRole(role);
        account.setEmailVerifiedAt(now);
        account.setIsActive(true);
        account.setIsLocked(false);
        account.setFailedLoginCount(0);
        account.setCreatedAt(now);
        account.setUpdatedAt(now);
        account.setLastLoginAt(now);
        accountRepository.save(account);

        UserProfile profile = new UserProfile();
        profile.setAccount(account);
        profile.setFullName(name != null ? name : email);
        profile.setAvatarUrl(picture);
        profile.setCreatedAt(now);
        profile.setUpdatedAt(now);
        userProfileRepository.save(profile);

        account.setUserProfile(profile);

        log.info("OAuth2 login — tạo account mới: {} (role={})", email, DEFAULT_ROLE);
        return account;
    }
}

// ============================================================
// FILE: ./src/main/java/com/ecms_web_application/OC01/service/impl/EmailServiceImpl.java
// ============================================================
package com.ecms_web_application.OC01.service.impl;

import jakarta.mail.MessagingException;
import jakarta.mail.internet.MimeMessage;
import lombok.RequiredArgsConstructor;
import lombok.extern.slf4j.Slf4j;
import org.springframework.beans.factory.annotation.Autowired;
import org.springframework.beans.factory.annotation.Value;
import org.springframework.mail.javamail.JavaMailSender;
import org.springframework.mail.javamail.MimeMessageHelper;
import org.springframework.stereotype.Service;
import com.ecms_web_application.OC01.service.EmailService;

import java.time.LocalDateTime;

@Slf4j
@Service
@RequiredArgsConstructor
public class EmailServiceImpl implements EmailService {

    private final JavaMailSender mailSender;

    @Value("${spring.mail.username}")
    private String fromEmail;

    // ===== CRITICAL → throw khi lỗi =====
    @Override
    public void sendOtpEmail(String to, String otp) {
        String html = buildHtml("Đặt lại mật khẩu",
                "<p style='text-align:center'>Mã OTP của bạn là:</p>"
                        + "<div class='code'>" + otp + "</div>"
                        + "<p class='muted'>Mã hết hạn sau <strong>5 phút</strong>.</p>");
        doSend(to, "Mã OTP đặt lại mật khẩu", html, true);
    }

    // ===== Informational → swallow khi lỗi =====
    @Override
    public void sendPasswordChangedEmail(String to) {
        String html = buildHtml("Mật khẩu đã được thay đổi",
                "<p>Mật khẩu của bạn vừa được thay đổi thành công.</p>"
                        + "<p class='muted'>Nếu không phải bạn, hãy liên hệ hỗ trợ ngay.</p>");
        doSend(to, "Mật khẩu đã được thay đổi", html, false);
    }

    // ===== CORE =====
    private void doSend(String to, String subject, String html, boolean throwOnError) {
        try {
            MimeMessage msg = mailSender.createMimeMessage();
            MimeMessageHelper helper = new MimeMessageHelper(msg, true, "UTF-8");
            helper.setFrom(fromEmail);
            helper.setTo(to);
            helper.setSubject(subject);
            helper.setText(html, true);
            mailSender.send(msg);
            log.info("[Email] Sent '{}' to {}", subject, to);
        } catch (Exception e) {
            log.warn("[Email] Failed '{}' to {}: {}", subject, to, e.getMessage());
            if (throwOnError) throw new RuntimeException("Failed to send email", e);
        }
    }

    private String buildHtml(String title, String bodyHtml) {
        return "<div style='font-family:Arial,sans-serif;max-width:600px;margin:0 auto;padding:24px;"
                + "border:1px solid #eee;border-radius:8px'>"
                + "<h2 style='color:#333;text-align:center;margin-bottom:16px'>" + esc(title) + "</h2>"
                + bodyHtml
                + "<hr style='border:none;border-top:1px solid #eee;margin:24px 0'/>"
                + "<p style='color:#999;font-size:12px;text-align:center'>ECMS</p>"
                + "<style>"
                + ".code{background:#f5f5f5;padding:20px;border-radius:8px;text-align:center;"
                + "margin:20px 0;font-size:36px;letter-spacing:8px;color:#2196F3;font-weight:bold}"
                + ".muted{color:#999;font-size:14px}"
                + ".reason{background:#fff3e0;border-left:4px solid #ff9800;padding:12px;margin:16px 0}"
                + "</style></div>";
    }

    private String esc(String s) {
        if (s == null) return "";
        return s.replace("&", "&amp;").replace("<", "&lt;").replace(">", "&gt;")
                .replace("\"", "&quot;");
    }

    @Override
    public void sendAccountLockedEmail(String to, String fullName,
                                       String reason, LocalDateTime lockedUntil) {
        String body = "<p>Xin chào <strong>" + esc(fullName) + "</strong>,</p>"
                + "<p>Tài khoản ECMS của bạn đã bị <strong style='color:#d32f2f'>khóa</strong>.</p>"
                + (reason != null && !reason.isBlank()
                ? "<div class='reason'><b>Lý do:</b> " + esc(reason) + "</div>" : "")
                + (lockedUntil != null
                ? "<p><b>Tự động mở khóa lúc:</b> " + lockedUntil + "</p>"
                : "<p>Tài khoản sẽ bị khóa cho đến khi quản trị viên mở lại.</p>")
                + "<p class='muted'>Nếu bạn không thực hiện hành động này, hãy liên hệ hỗ trợ ngay.</p>";

        doSend(to, "Tài khoản của bạn đã bị khóa",
                buildHtml("Tài khoản đã bị khóa", body), false);
    }


}

// ============================================================
// FILE: ./src/main/java/com/ecms_web_application/OC01/service/impl/InstructorSpecializationServiceImpl.java
// ============================================================
package com.ecms_web_application.OC01.service.impl;

import lombok.RequiredArgsConstructor;
import lombok.extern.slf4j.Slf4j;
import org.springframework.http.HttpStatus;
import org.springframework.stereotype.Service;
import org.springframework.transaction.annotation.Transactional;
import com.ecms_web_application.OC01.dto.request.SpecializationAddRequest;
import com.ecms_web_application.OC01.dto.response.InstructorSpecializationResponse;
import com.ecms_web_application.OC01.entity.Account;
import com.ecms_web_application.OC01.entity.Category;
import com.ecms_web_application.OC01.entity.InstructorSpecialization;
import com.ecms_web_application.OC01.exception.ErrorHandler;
import com.ecms_web_application.OC01.repository.AccountRepository;
import com.ecms_web_application.OC01.repository.CategoryRepository;
import com.ecms_web_application.OC01.repository.InstructorSpecializationRepository;
import com.ecms_web_application.OC01.service.InstructorSpecializationService;

import java.util.List;

@Service
@RequiredArgsConstructor
@Slf4j
public class InstructorSpecializationServiceImpl implements InstructorSpecializationService {

    private final InstructorSpecializationRepository specializationRepository;
    private final AccountRepository accountRepository;
    private final CategoryRepository categoryRepository;

    private static final String INSTRUCTOR_ROLE = "INSTRUCTOR";

    // ============================================================
    // QUERY
    // ============================================================

    @Override
    @Transactional(readOnly = true)
    public List<InstructorSpecializationResponse> getByAccount(Long accountId) {
        ensureAccountExists(accountId);
        return specializationRepository.findWithCategoryByAccountId(accountId)
                .stream()
                .map(this::toResponse)
                .toList();
    }

    @Override
    @Transactional(readOnly = true)
    public List<InstructorSpecializationResponse> getByCategory(Long categoryId) {
        if (!categoryRepository.existsById(categoryId)) {
            throw new ErrorHandler(HttpStatus.NOT_FOUND, "Category không tồn tại");
        }
        return specializationRepository.findWithAccountByCategoryId(categoryId)
                .stream()
                .map(this::toResponse)
                .toList();
    }

    @Override
    @Transactional(readOnly = true)
    public List<InstructorSpecializationResponse> getPrimaryOfAccount(Long accountId) {
        ensureAccountExists(accountId);
        return specializationRepository.findByAccount_IdAndIsPrimaryTrue(accountId)
                .map(s -> List.of(toResponse(s)))
                .orElse(List.of());
    }

    // ============================================================
    // ADD
    // ============================================================

    @Override
    @Transactional
    public InstructorSpecializationResponse add(Long accountId, SpecializationAddRequest request) {
        // 1. Validate account
        Account account = ensureInstructor(accountId);

        // 2. Validate category
        Category category = categoryRepository.findById(request.getCategoryId())
                .orElseThrow(() -> new ErrorHandler(HttpStatus.NOT_FOUND, "Category không tồn tại"));

        if (Boolean.FALSE.equals(category.getIsActive())) {
            throw new ErrorHandler(HttpStatus.BAD_REQUEST, "Category đã bị vô hiệu hóa");
        }

        // 3. Check đã gán chưa
        if (specializationRepository.existsByAccount_IdAndCategory_Id(accountId, category.getId())) {
            throw new ErrorHandler(HttpStatus.CONFLICT, "Chuyên môn này đã được gán cho giảng viên");
        }

        // 4. Nếu isPrimary = true → unset primary cũ
        boolean makePrimary = Boolean.TRUE.equals(request.getIsPrimary());
        if (makePrimary) {
            specializationRepository.resetPrimaryFlag(accountId);
        }

        // 5. Tạo bản ghi mới
        InstructorSpecialization spec = new InstructorSpecialization();
        spec.setAccount(account);
        spec.setCategory(category);
        spec.setIsPrimary(makePrimary);
        spec.setYearsOfExp(request.getYearsOfExp());

        InstructorSpecialization saved = specializationRepository.save(spec);
        log.info("Gán chuyên môn {} cho giảng viên {} (primary={})",
                category.getName(), account.getEmail(), makePrimary);

        return toResponse(saved);
    }

    // ============================================================
    // REMOVE
    // ============================================================

    @Override
    @Transactional
    public void remove(Long accountId, Long categoryId) {
        InstructorSpecialization spec = specializationRepository
                .findByAccount_IdAndCategory_Id(accountId, categoryId)
                .orElseThrow(() -> new ErrorHandler(HttpStatus.NOT_FOUND,
                        "Giảng viên chưa có chuyên môn này"));

        specializationRepository.delete(spec);
        log.info("Xóa chuyên môn categoryId={} khỏi giảng viên accountId={}", categoryId, accountId);
    }

    @Override
    @Transactional
    public void removeAll(Long accountId) {
        ensureAccountExists(accountId);
        specializationRepository.deleteAllByAccountId(accountId);
        log.info("Xóa tất cả chuyên môn của accountId={}", accountId);
    }

    // ============================================================
    // SET PRIMARY
    // ============================================================

    @Override
    @Transactional
    public void setPrimary(Long accountId, Long categoryId) {
        InstructorSpecialization spec = specializationRepository
                .findByAccount_IdAndCategory_Id(accountId, categoryId)
                .orElseThrow(() -> new ErrorHandler(HttpStatus.NOT_FOUND,
                        "Giảng viên chưa có chuyên môn này"));

        if (Boolean.TRUE.equals(spec.getIsPrimary())) {
            return;  // đã là primary rồi
        }

        // 1. Reset cờ primary cũ
        specializationRepository.resetPrimaryFlag(accountId);

        // 2. Set cờ primary mới
        spec.setIsPrimary(true);
        specializationRepository.save(spec);
        log.info("Đặt chuyên môn chính: accountId={}, categoryId={}", accountId, categoryId);
    }

    @Override
    @Transactional
    public void clearPrimary(Long accountId) {
        ensureAccountExists(accountId);
        specializationRepository.resetPrimaryFlag(accountId);
        log.info("Bỏ cờ chuyên môn chính cho accountId={}", accountId);
    }

    // ============================================================
    // HELPERS
    // ============================================================

    private Account ensureAccountExists(Long accountId) {
        return accountRepository.findById(accountId)
                .orElseThrow(() -> new ErrorHandler(HttpStatus.NOT_FOUND, "Account không tồn tại"));
    }

    private Account ensureInstructor(Long accountId) {
        Account a = ensureAccountExists(accountId);
        if (a.getRole() == null || !INSTRUCTOR_ROLE.equals(a.getRole().getCode())) {
            throw new ErrorHandler(HttpStatus.BAD_REQUEST,
                    "Chỉ giảng viên mới có chuyên môn");
        }
        return a;
    }

    private InstructorSpecializationResponse toResponse(InstructorSpecialization spec) {
        InstructorSpecializationResponse res = new InstructorSpecializationResponse();

        if (spec.getAccount() != null) {
            res.setAccountId(spec.getAccount().getId());
            res.setInstructorEmail(spec.getAccount().getEmail());
            if (spec.getAccount().getUserProfile() != null) {
                res.setInstructorName(spec.getAccount().getUserProfile().getFullName());
            }
        }

        if (spec.getCategory() != null) {
            res.setCategoryId(spec.getCategory().getId());
            res.setCategoryName(spec.getCategory().getName());
            res.setCategorySlug(spec.getCategory().getSlug());
        }

        res.setIsPrimary(spec.getIsPrimary());
        res.setYearsOfExp(spec.getYearsOfExp());
        res.setCreatedAt(spec.getCreatedAt());

        return res;
    }
}

// ============================================================
// FILE: ./src/main/java/com/ecms_web_application/OC01/service/impl/LeadServiceImpl.java
// ============================================================
package com.ecms_web_application.OC01.service.impl;

import com.ecms_web_application.OC01.config.SecurityUtils;
import com.ecms_web_application.OC01.dto.request.LeadAssignRequest;
import com.ecms_web_application.OC01.dto.request.LeadCreateRequest;
import com.ecms_web_application.OC01.dto.request.LeadInteractionCreateRequest;
import com.ecms_web_application.OC01.dto.request.LeadPlacementTestCreateRequest;
import com.ecms_web_application.OC01.dto.request.LeadUpdateRequest;
import com.ecms_web_application.OC01.dto.response.LeadDetailResponse;
import com.ecms_web_application.OC01.dto.response.LeadInteractionResponse;
import com.ecms_web_application.OC01.dto.response.LeadPlacementTestResponse;
import com.ecms_web_application.OC01.dto.response.LeadResponse;
import com.ecms_web_application.OC01.entity.Lead;
import com.ecms_web_application.OC01.entity.LeadAssignmentLog;
import com.ecms_web_application.OC01.entity.LeadInteraction;
import com.ecms_web_application.OC01.entity.LeadPlacementTest;
import com.ecms_web_application.OC01.entity.LeadStatusLog;
import com.ecms_web_application.OC01.exception.ErrorHandler;
import com.ecms_web_application.OC01.repository.LeadAssignmentLogRepository;
import com.ecms_web_application.OC01.repository.LeadInteractionRepository;
import com.ecms_web_application.OC01.repository.LeadPlacementTestRepository;
import com.ecms_web_application.OC01.repository.LeadRepository;
import com.ecms_web_application.OC01.repository.LeadStatusLogRepository;
import com.ecms_web_application.OC01.service.LeadService;
import lombok.RequiredArgsConstructor;
import lombok.extern.slf4j.Slf4j;
import org.springframework.data.domain.Page;
import org.springframework.data.domain.Pageable;
import org.springframework.http.HttpStatus;
import org.springframework.stereotype.Service;
import org.springframework.transaction.annotation.Transactional;

import java.time.LocalDateTime;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.Objects;

@Service
@RequiredArgsConstructor
@Slf4j
public class LeadServiceImpl implements LeadService {

    private final LeadRepository leadRepository;
    private final LeadInteractionRepository interactionRepository;
    private final LeadPlacementTestRepository placementTestRepository;
    private final LeadStatusLogRepository statusLogRepository;
    private final LeadAssignmentLogRepository assignmentLogRepository;

    @Override
    @Transactional(readOnly = true)
    public Page<LeadResponse> search(String search, String status, Long courseId, Long sourceId, Long consultantId, Boolean inQueue, Pageable pageable) {
        return leadRepository
                .searchLeads(emptyToNull(status), courseId, sourceId, consultantId, inQueue, emptyToNull(search), pageable)
                .map(this::toResponse);
    }

    @Override
    @Transactional(readOnly = true)
    public LeadResponse getById(Long id) {
        Lead lead = findLeadById(id);
        return toResponse(lead);
    }

    @Override
    @Transactional(readOnly = true)
    public LeadDetailResponse getDetailById(Long id) {
        Lead lead = findLeadById(id);
        LeadDetailResponse detail = new LeadDetailResponse();
        copyPropertiesToResponse(lead, detail);

        List<LeadInteractionResponse> interactions = interactionRepository.findByLeadIdOrderByCreatedAtDesc(id)
                .stream().map(this::toInteractionResponse).toList();
        List<LeadPlacementTestResponse> placementTests = placementTestRepository.findByLeadIdOrderByCreatedAtDesc(id)
                .stream().map(this::toPlacementTestResponse).toList();
        List<LeadStatusLog> statusLogs = statusLogRepository.findByLeadIdOrderByChangedAtDesc(id);
        List<LeadAssignmentLog> assignmentLogs = assignmentLogRepository.findByLeadIdOrderByChangedAtDesc(id);

        detail.setInteractions(interactions);
        detail.setPlacementTests(placementTests);
        detail.setStatusLogs(statusLogs);
        detail.setAssignmentLogs(assignmentLogs);
        return detail;
    }

    @Override
    @Transactional
    public LeadResponse create(LeadCreateRequest request) {
        Lead lead = new Lead();
        lead.setFullName(request.getFullName().trim());
        lead.setEmail(request.getEmail() != null ? request.getEmail().trim().toLowerCase() : null);
        lead.setPhone(emptyToNull(request.getPhone()));
        lead.setCourseId(request.getCourseId());
        lead.setSourceId(request.getSourceId());
        lead.setAssignedConsultantId(request.getAssignedConsultantId());
        lead.setNote(emptyToNull(request.getNote()));

        String status = (request.getStatus() != null && !request.getStatus().isBlank())
                ? request.getStatus().trim().toUpperCase()
                : "NEW";
        lead.setStatus(status);

        Lead saved = leadRepository.save(lead);
        Long currentUserId = SecurityUtils.getCurrentAccountId();

        // Ghi log trạng thái ban đầu
        recordStatusLog(saved.getId(), null, saved.getStatus(), "Khởi tạo Lead mới", currentUserId);

        // Ghi log phân công nếu đã chọn người phụ trách ban đầu
        if (saved.getAssignedConsultantId() != null) {
            recordAssignmentLog(saved.getId(), null, saved.getAssignedConsultantId(), "Phân công tư vấn viên ban đầu", currentUserId);
        }

        log.info("Tạo mới Lead id={}, fullName={}, status={}", saved.getId(), saved.getFullName(), saved.getStatus());
        return toResponse(saved);
    }

    @Override
    @Transactional
    public LeadResponse update(Long id, LeadUpdateRequest request) {
        Lead lead = findLeadById(id);
        Long currentUserId = SecurityUtils.getCurrentAccountId();

        if (request.getFullName() != null && !request.getFullName().isBlank()) {
            lead.setFullName(request.getFullName().trim());
        }
        if (request.getEmail() != null) {
            lead.setEmail(emptyToNull(request.getEmail().toLowerCase()));
        }
        if (request.getPhone() != null) {
            lead.setPhone(emptyToNull(request.getPhone()));
        }
        if (request.getCourseId() != null) {
            lead.setCourseId(request.getCourseId());
        }
        if (request.getSourceId() != null) {
            lead.setSourceId(request.getSourceId());
        }
        if (request.getStudentId() != null) {
            lead.setStudentId(request.getStudentId());
        }
        if (request.getNote() != null) {
            lead.setNote(emptyToNull(request.getNote()));
        }

        // Xử lý thay đổi phân công
        if (request.getAssignedConsultantId() != null && !Objects.equals(request.getAssignedConsultantId(), lead.getAssignedConsultantId())) {
            Long oldConsultantId = lead.getAssignedConsultantId();
            lead.setAssignedConsultantId(request.getAssignedConsultantId());
            recordAssignmentLog(lead.getId(), oldConsultantId, request.getAssignedConsultantId(), "Cập nhật người phụ trách", currentUserId);
        }

        // Xử lý thay đổi trạng thái (Bất biến: KHÔNG lùi từ CONSULTED về NEW)
        if (request.getStatus() != null && !request.getStatus().isBlank()) {
            String newStatus = request.getStatus().trim().toUpperCase();
            String oldStatus = lead.getStatus();
            if ("CONSULTED".equalsIgnoreCase(oldStatus) && "NEW".equalsIgnoreCase(newStatus)) {
                throw new ErrorHandler(HttpStatus.BAD_REQUEST, "Quy tắc nghiệp vụ: Không được phép chuyển lùi trạng thái Lead từ CONSULTED về NEW");
            }
            if (!newStatus.equalsIgnoreCase(oldStatus)) {
                lead.setStatus(newStatus);
                recordStatusLog(lead.getId(), oldStatus, newStatus, "Cập nhật trạng thái qua API", currentUserId);
            }
        }

        Lead saved = leadRepository.save(lead);
        log.info("Cập nhật Lead id={}, status={}", saved.getId(), saved.getStatus());
        return toResponse(saved);
    }

    @Override
    @Transactional
    public LeadResponse assignConsultant(Long id, LeadAssignRequest request) {
        Lead lead = findLeadById(id);
        Long oldConsultantId = lead.getAssignedConsultantId();
        Long newConsultantId = request.getConsultantId();

        lead.setAssignedConsultantId(newConsultantId);
        Lead saved = leadRepository.save(lead);

        Long currentUserId = SecurityUtils.getCurrentAccountId();
        String reason = (request.getReason() != null && !request.getReason().isBlank())
                ? request.getReason()
                : "Chuyển phân công tư vấn viên";
        recordAssignmentLog(saved.getId(), oldConsultantId, newConsultantId, reason, currentUserId);

        log.info("Phân công Lead id={} từ consultant={} sang consultant={}", id, oldConsultantId, newConsultantId);
        return toResponse(saved);
    }

    @Override
    @Transactional
    public LeadInteractionResponse logInteraction(Long id, LeadInteractionCreateRequest request) {
        Lead lead = findLeadById(id);

        String result = request.getResult().trim().toUpperCase();
        // Ràng buộc: reject_reason bắt buộc khi result = REJECTED (hoặc TỪ CHỐI)
        if ("REJECTED".equalsIgnoreCase(result) || "TỪ CHỐI".equalsIgnoreCase(result)) {
            if (request.getRejectReason() == null || request.getRejectReason().isBlank()) {
                throw new ErrorHandler(HttpStatus.BAD_REQUEST, "Quy tắc nghiệp vụ: Bắt buộc cung cấp lý do từ chối (rejectReason) khi kết quả tư vấn là Từ chối");
            }
        }

        Long currentUserId = SecurityUtils.getCurrentAccountId();

        LeadInteraction interaction = new LeadInteraction();
        interaction.setLeadId(id);
        interaction.setInteractionType(request.getInteractionType().trim().toUpperCase());
        interaction.setNotes(emptyToNull(request.getNotes()));
        interaction.setResult(result);
        interaction.setRejectReason(emptyToNull(request.getRejectReason()));
        interaction.setPerformedBy(currentUserId);
        LeadInteraction savedInteraction = interactionRepository.save(interaction);

        // Bất biến & Tác dụng phụ: Nếu Lead đang ở trạng thái NEW -> tự động chuyển sang CONSULTED
        if ("NEW".equalsIgnoreCase(lead.getStatus())) {
            lead.setStatus("CONSULTED");
            leadRepository.save(lead);
            recordStatusLog(lead.getId(), "NEW", "CONSULTED", "Tự động chuyển CONSULTED khi ghi nhận tương tác tư vấn đầu tiên", currentUserId);
            log.info("Tự động chuyển trạng thái Lead id={} từ NEW sang CONSULTED", id);
        }

        return toInteractionResponse(savedInteraction);
    }

    @Override
    @Transactional(readOnly = true)
    public List<LeadInteractionResponse> getInteractions(Long id) {
        findLeadById(id);
        return interactionRepository.findByLeadIdOrderByCreatedAtDesc(id)
                .stream().map(this::toInteractionResponse).toList();
    }

    @Override
    @Transactional
    public LeadPlacementTestResponse recordPlacementTest(Long id, LeadPlacementTestCreateRequest request) {
        findLeadById(id);
        String testStatus = request.getTestStatus().trim().toUpperCase();

        // Ràng buộc: test_score chỉ có khi test_status = COMPLETED
        if (!"COMPLETED".equalsIgnoreCase(testStatus) && request.getTestScore() != null) {
            throw new ErrorHandler(HttpStatus.BAD_REQUEST, "Quy tắc nghiệp vụ: Điểm đánh giá (testScore) chỉ được phép ghi nhận khi trạng thái bài test là COMPLETED");
        }

        LeadPlacementTest test = new LeadPlacementTest();
        test.setLeadId(id);
        test.setTestStatus(testStatus);
        test.setScheduledAt(request.getScheduledAt());
        test.setTestScore(request.getTestScore());
        test.setRecommendedLevel(emptyToNull(request.getRecommendedLevel()));
        test.setNotes(emptyToNull(request.getNotes()));
        test.setEvaluatedAt(request.getEvaluatedAt() != null ? request.getEvaluatedAt() : ("COMPLETED".equalsIgnoreCase(testStatus) ? LocalDateTime.now() : null));

        LeadPlacementTest saved = placementTestRepository.save(test);
        log.info("Ghi nhận đánh giá đầu vào cho Lead id={}, status={}", id, saved.getTestStatus());
        return toPlacementTestResponse(saved);
    }

    @Override
    @Transactional(readOnly = true)
    public List<LeadPlacementTestResponse> getPlacementTests(Long id) {
        findLeadById(id);
        return placementTestRepository.findByLeadIdOrderByCreatedAtDesc(id)
                .stream().map(this::toPlacementTestResponse).toList();
    }

    @Override
    @Transactional
    public void delete(Long id) {
        Lead lead = findLeadById(id);
        // Chuyển trạng thái kết thúc tư vấn / ghi log
        recordStatusLog(lead.getId(), lead.getStatus(), "CONSULTED", "Đóng tư vấn Lead", SecurityUtils.getCurrentAccountId());
        lead.setStatus("CONSULTED");
        leadRepository.save(lead);
        log.info("Đã cập nhật đóng tư vấn Lead id={}", id);
    }

    // ===== Private Helper Methods =====

    private Lead findLeadById(Long id) {
        return leadRepository.findById(id)
                .orElseThrow(() -> new ErrorHandler(HttpStatus.NOT_FOUND, "Không tìm thấy Lead với ID: " + id));
    }

    private void recordStatusLog(Long leadId, String oldStatus, String newStatus, String reason, Long changedBy) {
        LeadStatusLog logEntity = new LeadStatusLog();
        logEntity.setLeadId(leadId);
        logEntity.setOldStatus(oldStatus);
        logEntity.setNewStatus(newStatus);
        logEntity.setReason(reason);
        logEntity.setChangedBy(changedBy);
        statusLogRepository.save(logEntity);
    }

    private void recordAssignmentLog(Long leadId, Long oldConsultantId, Long newConsultantId, String reason, Long changedBy) {
        LeadAssignmentLog logEntity = new LeadAssignmentLog();
        logEntity.setLeadId(leadId);
        logEntity.setOldConsultantId(oldConsultantId);
        logEntity.setNewConsultantId(newConsultantId);
        logEntity.setReason(reason);
        logEntity.setChangedBy(changedBy);
        assignmentLogRepository.save(logEntity);
    }

    private void copyPropertiesToResponse(Lead lead, LeadResponse response) {
        response.setId(lead.getId());
        response.setFullName(lead.getFullName());
        response.setEmail(lead.getEmail());
        response.setPhone(lead.getPhone());
        response.setCourseId(lead.getCourseId());
        response.setStudentId(lead.getStudentId());
        response.setStatus(lead.getStatus());
        response.setConvertedAt(lead.getConvertedAt());
        response.setSourceId(lead.getSourceId());
        response.setAssignedConsultantId(lead.getAssignedConsultantId());
        response.setNote(lead.getNote());
        response.setCreatedAt(lead.getCreatedAt());
        response.setUpdatedAt(lead.getUpdatedAt());
    }

    private LeadResponse toResponse(Lead lead) {
        LeadResponse response = new LeadResponse();
        copyPropertiesToResponse(lead, response);
        return response;
    }

    private LeadInteractionResponse toInteractionResponse(LeadInteraction interaction) {
        LeadInteractionResponse response = new LeadInteractionResponse();
        response.setId(interaction.getId());
        response.setLeadId(interaction.getLeadId());
        response.setInteractionType(interaction.getInteractionType());
        response.setNotes(interaction.getNotes());
        response.setResult(interaction.getResult());
        response.setRejectReason(interaction.getRejectReason());
        response.setPerformedBy(interaction.getPerformedBy());
        response.setCreatedAt(interaction.getCreatedAt());
        return response;
    }

    private LeadPlacementTestResponse toPlacementTestResponse(LeadPlacementTest test) {
        LeadPlacementTestResponse response = new LeadPlacementTestResponse();
        response.setId(test.getId());
        response.setLeadId(test.getLeadId());
        response.setTestStatus(test.getTestStatus());
        response.setScheduledAt(test.getScheduledAt());
        response.setTestScore(test.getTestScore());
        response.setRecommendedLevel(test.getRecommendedLevel());
        response.setNotes(test.getNotes());
        response.setEvaluatedAt(test.getEvaluatedAt());
        response.setCreatedAt(test.getCreatedAt());
        return response;
    }

    private String emptyToNull(String str) {
        return (str == null || str.trim().isEmpty()) ? null : str.trim();
    }
}

// ============================================================
// FILE: ./src/main/java/com/ecms_web_application/OC01/service/impl/notification/NotificationCleanupJob.java
// ============================================================
package com.ecms_web_application.OC01.service.impl.notification;

import com.ecms_web_application.OC01.service.NotificationService;
import lombok.RequiredArgsConstructor;
import lombok.extern.slf4j.Slf4j;
import org.springframework.beans.factory.annotation.Value;
import org.springframework.boot.autoconfigure.condition.ConditionalOnProperty;
import org.springframework.scheduling.annotation.Scheduled;
import org.springframework.stereotype.Component;

/**
 * Cron dọn dẹp notification định kỳ.
 *
 * Bật/tắt qua application.yml:
 *   notification.cleanup.enabled=true|false
 *
 * Đổi retention (số ngày giữ lại sau khi hết hạn):
 *   notification.cleanup.expired-retention-days=7
 *
 * Đổi giờ chạy: sửa cron bên dưới.
 */
@Slf4j
@Component
@ConditionalOnProperty(
        name = "notification.cleanup.enabled",
        havingValue = "true",
        matchIfMissing = true          // Không có key trong YAML → mặc định BẬT
)
@RequiredArgsConstructor
public class NotificationCleanupJob {

    private final NotificationService notificationService;

    @Value("${notification.cleanup.expired-retention-days:7}")
    private int retentionDays;

    /** Chạy 3h sáng mỗi ngày. */
    @Scheduled(cron = "0 0 3 * * *")
    public void cleanupDaily() {
        try {
            log.info("[Notification Cleanup] Bắt đầu dọn dẹp (retention={} ngày)...", retentionDays);
            int total = notificationService.cleanupExpired(retentionDays);
            log.info("[Notification Cleanup] Hoàn tất. Đã xoá {} bản ghi", total);
        } catch (Exception e) {
            log.error("[Notification Cleanup] Lỗi: {}", e.getMessage(), e);
        }
    }
}

// ============================================================
// FILE: ./src/main/java/com/ecms_web_application/OC01/service/impl/NotificationServiceImpl.java
// ============================================================
package com.ecms_web_application.OC01.service.impl;

import com.ecms_web_application.OC01.config.SecurityUtils;
import com.ecms_web_application.OC01.dto.request.NotificationRequest;
import com.ecms_web_application.OC01.dto.request.NotificationSearchRequest;
import com.ecms_web_application.OC01.dto.response.NotificationResponse;
import com.ecms_web_application.OC01.entity.Account;
import com.ecms_web_application.OC01.entity.Notification;
import com.ecms_web_application.OC01.entity.NotificationRecipient;
import com.ecms_web_application.OC01.entity.enums.EmailStatusEnum;
import com.ecms_web_application.OC01.entity.enums.NotificationPriorityEnum;
import com.ecms_web_application.OC01.entity.enums.PushStatusEnum;
import com.ecms_web_application.OC01.exception.ErrorHandler;
import com.ecms_web_application.OC01.repository.AccountRepository;
import com.ecms_web_application.OC01.repository.NotificationRecipientRepository;
import com.ecms_web_application.OC01.repository.NotificationRepository;
import com.ecms_web_application.OC01.repository.StudentRepository;
import com.ecms_web_application.OC01.service.NotificationService;
import lombok.RequiredArgsConstructor;
import lombok.extern.slf4j.Slf4j;
import org.springframework.data.domain.Page;
import org.springframework.data.domain.PageRequest;
import org.springframework.data.domain.Pageable;
import org.springframework.data.domain.Sort;
import org.springframework.http.HttpStatus;
import org.springframework.messaging.simp.SimpMessagingTemplate;
import org.springframework.stereotype.Service;
import org.springframework.transaction.annotation.Transactional;

import java.time.LocalDateTime;
import java.util.*;
import java.util.stream.Collectors;

@Slf4j
@Service
@RequiredArgsConstructor
@Transactional(readOnly = true)
public class NotificationServiceImpl implements NotificationService {

    private final NotificationRepository notificationRepo;
    private final NotificationRecipientRepository recipientRepo;
    private final AccountRepository accountRepo;
    private final StudentRepository studentRepo;
    private final SimpMessagingTemplate messagingTemplate;

    // ==================== SEND ====================

    @Override
    @Transactional
    public void sendNotification(NotificationRequest request) {
        List<Long> receiverIds = resolveReceivers(request);
        if (receiverIds.isEmpty()) {
            throw new ErrorHandler(HttpStatus.BAD_REQUEST,
                    "Không xác định được người nhận — cần ít nhất 1 trong: receiverAccountIds, receiverRole, courseId");
        }

        // 1. Tạo Notification
        Notification notification = new Notification();
        notification.setSenderAccountId(request.getSenderAccountId());
        notification.setNotificationType(request.getNotificationType());
        notification.setTitle(request.getTitle());
        notification.setContent(request.getContent());
        notification.setReferenceType(request.getReferenceType());
        notification.setReferenceId(request.getReferenceId());
        notification.setExtraData(request.getExtraData());
        notification.setPriority(parsePriority(request.getPriority()));
        notification.setExpiresAt(request.getExpiresAt());
        notification = notificationRepo.save(notification);

        // 2. Tạo recipients (batch)
        EmailStatusEnum emailStatus = Boolean.TRUE.equals(request.getSendEmail())
                ? EmailStatusEnum.PENDING : null;
        PushStatusEnum pushStatus = Boolean.TRUE.equals(request.getSendPush())
                ? PushStatusEnum.PENDING : null;

        List<NotificationRecipient> recipients = new ArrayList<>();
        for (Long accId : new LinkedHashSet<>(receiverIds)) {
            NotificationRecipient r = new NotificationRecipient();
            r.setNotificationId(notification.getId());
            r.setAccountId(accId);
            r.setEmailStatus(emailStatus);
            r.setPushStatus(pushStatus);
            recipients.add(r);
        }
        recipients = recipientRepo.saveAll(recipients);

        // 3. Broadcast WS in-app cho từng receiver
        for (NotificationRecipient r : recipients) {
            broadcastWS(notification.getId(), r.getAccountId(),
                    notification.getTitle(), notification.getContent(),
                    notification.getNotificationType(),
                    notification.getReferenceType(), notification.getReferenceId(),
                    notification.getExtraData(), notification.getPriority().name());
        }

        log.info("[Notification] Sent #{} '{}' to {} receivers (email={}, push={})",
                notification.getId(), notification.getTitle(),
                recipients.size(), emailStatus, pushStatus);
    }

    private void broadcastWS(Long notificationId, Long accountId,
                             String title, String content, String type,
                             String refType, Long refId,
                             Map<String, Object> extraData, String priority) {
        try {
            Map<String, Object> payload = new HashMap<>();
            payload.put("notificationId", notificationId);
            payload.put("title", title);
            payload.put("content", content);
            payload.put("notificationType", type);
            payload.put("referenceType", refType);
            payload.put("referenceId", refId);
            payload.put("extraData", extraData);
            payload.put("priority", priority);
            payload.put("isRead", false);
            payload.put("createdAt", LocalDateTime.now().toString());

            messagingTemplate.convertAndSend(
                    "/topic/user/" + accountId + "/notifications", payload);
        } catch (Exception e) {
            log.warn("[Notification] WS broadcast failed for account {}: {}",
                    accountId, e.getMessage());
        }
    }

    private List<Long> resolveReceivers(NotificationRequest request) {
        // Ưu tiên 1: danh sách accountId cụ thể
        if (request.getReceiverAccountIds() != null && !request.getReceiverAccountIds().isEmpty()) {
            return request.getReceiverAccountIds().stream()
                    .filter(accountRepo::existsById)
                    .distinct().collect(Collectors.toList());
        }
        // Ưu tiên 2: theo role
        if (request.getReceiverRole() != null && !request.getReceiverRole().isBlank()) {
            String role = request.getReceiverRole().trim().toUpperCase();
            switch (role) {
                case "ALL_STUDENTS":
                    return toIds(accountRepo.findByRoleCode("STUDENT"));
                case "ALL_TEACHERS", "ALL_INSTRUCTORS":
                    return toIds(accountRepo.findByRoleCode("INSTRUCTOR"));
                case "ALL_USERS":
                    return accountRepo.findAll().stream()
                            .filter(a -> Boolean.TRUE.equals(a.getIsActive()) && a.getDeletedAt() == null)
                            .map(Account::getId).collect(Collectors.toList());
                default:
                    throw new ErrorHandler(HttpStatus.BAD_REQUEST,
                            "receiverRole không hợp lệ: " + request.getReceiverRole());
            }
        }
        // Ưu tiên 3: theo course
        if (request.getCourseId() != null) {
            return studentRepo.findAccountIdsByCourseId(request.getCourseId());
        }
        return List.of();
    }

    private List<Long> toIds(List<Account> accounts) {
        return accounts.stream()
                .filter(a -> Boolean.TRUE.equals(a.getIsActive()) && a.getDeletedAt() == null)
                .map(Account::getId).collect(Collectors.toList());
    }

    private NotificationPriorityEnum parsePriority(String priority) {
        if (priority == null || priority.isBlank()) return NotificationPriorityEnum.NORMAL;
        try {
            return NotificationPriorityEnum.valueOf(priority.trim().toUpperCase());
        } catch (IllegalArgumentException e) {
            throw new ErrorHandler(HttpStatus.BAD_REQUEST, "Priority không hợp lệ: " + priority);
        }
    }

    // ==================== INBOX ====================

    @Override
    public Page<NotificationResponse> getMyInbox(NotificationSearchRequest request) {
        Long me = SecurityUtils.requireCurrentAccountId();

        java.util.Set<String> allowedSort = java.util.Set.of("createdAt", "priority", "notificationType", "readAt");
        String sortBy = allowedSort.contains(request.getSortBy()) ? request.getSortBy() : "createdAt";
        Sort sort = "ASC".equalsIgnoreCase(request.getSortDirection())
                ? Sort.by(sortBy).ascending()
                : Sort.by(sortBy).descending();
        int safeSize = Math.max(1, Math.min(request.getSize(), 100));
        Pageable pageable = PageRequest.of(request.getPage(), safeSize, sort);
        LocalDateTime fromDate = request.getFromDate() != null
                ? request.getFromDate()
                : LocalDateTime.of(1970, 1, 1, 0, 0, 0);

        LocalDateTime toDate = request.getToDate() != null
                ? request.getToDate()
                : LocalDateTime.of(9999, 12, 31, 23, 59, 59);

        Page<NotificationRecipient> page = recipientRepo.searchInbox(
                me,
                blankIfNull(request.getNotificationType()),
                request.getIsRead(),
                fromDate,
                toDate,
                blankIfNull(request.getKeyword()),
                pageable);

        return page.map(this::toResponse);
    }

    @Override
    public NotificationResponse getNotificationDetail(Long notificationId) {
        Long me = SecurityUtils.requireCurrentAccountId();
        NotificationRecipient r = recipientRepo.findDetail(notificationId, me);
        if (r == null) {
            throw new ErrorHandler(HttpStatus.NOT_FOUND,
                    "Thông báo không tồn tại hoặc không thuộc về bạn");
        }
        return toResponse(r);
    }

    @Override
    public long getUnreadCount() {
        Long me = SecurityUtils.requireCurrentAccountId();
        return recipientRepo.countUnread(me);
    }

    // ==================== MARK AS READ ====================

    @Override
    @Transactional
    public void markAsRead(Long notificationId) {
        Long me = SecurityUtils.requireCurrentAccountId();
        NotificationRecipient r = recipientRepo.findByNotificationIdAndAccountId(notificationId, me);
        if (r == null) {
            throw new ErrorHandler(HttpStatus.NOT_FOUND,
                    "Thông báo không tồn tại hoặc không thuộc về bạn");
        }
        recipientRepo.markAsRead(notificationId, me);
        // Multi-tab sync
        try {
            messagingTemplate.convertAndSend(
                    "/topic/user/" + me + "/notifications",
                    Map.of("action", "MARKED_READ", "notificationId", notificationId));
        } catch (Exception e) {
            log.warn("[Notification] WS markAsRead failed: {}", e.getMessage());
        }
    }

    @Override
    @Transactional
    public void markAllAsRead() {
        Long me = SecurityUtils.requireCurrentAccountId();
        int count = recipientRepo.markAllAsRead(me);
        log.info("[Notification] Marked {} as read for account {}", count, me);
        try {
            messagingTemplate.convertAndSend(
                    "/topic/user/" + me + "/notifications",
                    Map.of("action", "MARKED_ALL_READ"));
        } catch (Exception e) {
            log.warn("[Notification] WS markAllAsRead failed: {}", e.getMessage());
        }
    }

    // ==================== DELETE ====================

    @Override
    @Transactional
    public void deleteNotification(Long notificationId) {
        Long me = SecurityUtils.requireCurrentAccountId();
        int deleted = recipientRepo.softDelete(notificationId, me);
        if (deleted == 0) {
            throw new ErrorHandler(HttpStatus.NOT_FOUND,
                    "Thông báo không tồn tại hoặc không thuộc về bạn");
        }
    }

    @Override
    @Transactional
    public void deleteAllNotifications() {
        Long me = SecurityUtils.requireCurrentAccountId();
        int count = recipientRepo.softDeleteAll(me);
        log.info("[Notification] Soft-deleted {} notifications for account {}", count, me);
    }

    // ==================== CLEANUP ====================

    @Override
    @Transactional
    public int cleanupExpired(int retentionDays) {
        if (retentionDays < 0) retentionDays = 7;
        LocalDateTime expiredThreshold = LocalDateTime.now().minusDays(retentionDays);
        LocalDateTime softDeletedThreshold = LocalDateTime.now().minusDays(30);
        LocalDateTime orphanThreshold = LocalDateTime.now().minusDays(30);

        int rExpired = recipientRepo.deleteRecipientsOfExpiredNotifications(expiredThreshold);
        int expired = notificationRepo.deleteExpiredBefore(expiredThreshold);
        int rOrphan = recipientRepo.deleteOrphanRecipientsOfOldNotifications(orphanThreshold);
        int orphans = notificationRepo.deleteOrphanBefore(orphanThreshold);

        int total = rExpired + expired + rOrphan + orphans;
        log.info("[Notification Cleanup] rExpired={}, expired={}, rOrphan={}, orphans={}, total={}",
                rExpired, expired, rOrphan, orphans, total);
        return total;
    }

    // ==================== MAPPER ====================

    private NotificationResponse toResponse(NotificationRecipient r) {
        Notification n = r.getNotification();
        if (n == null) {
            // Fallback: load manually nếu chưa fetch
            n = notificationRepo.findById(r.getNotificationId()).orElse(null);
        }
        if (n == null) {
            return null;
        }

        Account sender = n.getSenderAccountId() != null
                ? accountRepo.findById(n.getSenderAccountId()).orElse(null) : null;

        return NotificationResponse.builder()
                .id(r.getId())
                .notificationId(n.getId())
                .senderAccountId(n.getSenderAccountId())
                .senderName(sender != null ? sender.getEmail() : "Hệ thống")
                .notificationType(n.getNotificationType())
                .title(n.getTitle())
                .content(n.getContent())
                .referenceType(n.getReferenceType())
                .referenceId(n.getReferenceId())
                .extraData(n.getExtraData())
                .priority(n.getPriority() != null ? n.getPriority().name() : null)
                .expiresAt(n.getExpiresAt())
                .createdAt(n.getCreatedAt())
                .isRead(r.getIsRead())
                .readAt(r.getReadAt())
                .receivedAt(r.getCreatedAt())
                .emailStatus(r.getEmailStatus() != null ? r.getEmailStatus().name() : null)
                .pushStatus(r.getPushStatus() != null ? r.getPushStatus().name() : null)
                .build();
    }

    private String blankIfNull(String s) {
        return s == null ? "" : s.trim();
    }
}

// ============================================================
// FILE: ./src/main/java/com/ecms_web_application/OC01/service/impl/OtpServiceImpl.java
// ============================================================
package com.ecms_web_application.OC01.service.impl;

import lombok.RequiredArgsConstructor;
import lombok.extern.slf4j.Slf4j;
import org.springframework.scheduling.annotation.Scheduled;
import org.springframework.stereotype.Service;
import com.ecms_web_application.OC01.entity.enums.OtpPurpose;
import com.ecms_web_application.OC01.service.EmailService;
import com.ecms_web_application.OC01.service.OtpService;

import java.security.SecureRandom;
import java.util.Map;
import java.util.concurrent.ConcurrentHashMap;
import java.util.concurrent.atomic.AtomicInteger;

@Slf4j
@Service
@RequiredArgsConstructor
public class OtpServiceImpl implements OtpService {

    private static final int OTP_LENGTH = 6;
    private static final int OTP_EXPIRY_MINUTES = 5;
    private static final int VERIFIED_EXPIRY_MINUTES = 5;
    private static final int MAX_ATTEMPTS = 5;
    private static final int LOCKOUT_MINUTES = 15;
    private static final int RESEND_COOLDOWN_SECONDS = 60;

    private static final SecureRandom SECURE_RANDOM = new SecureRandom();

    private final EmailService emailService;

    private final Map<String, OtpData> otpStore = new ConcurrentHashMap<>();
    private final Map<String, VerifiedData> verifiedEmails = new ConcurrentHashMap<>();
    private final Map<String, AttemptData> attemptStore = new ConcurrentHashMap<>();

    @Override
    public void generateAndSend(String email, OtpPurpose purpose) {
        String otp = generateOtp();
        saveOtp(email, otp);

        if (purpose == OtpPurpose.FORGOT_PASSWORD) {
            emailService.sendOtpEmail(email, otp);
        } else {
            log.warn("[OTP] Chưa hỗ trợ purpose: {}", purpose);
            throw new UnsupportedOperationException("Chưa hỗ trợ purpose: " + purpose);
        }
    }

    @Override
    public void saveOtp(String email, String otp) {
        if (email == null || otp == null) {
            throw new IllegalArgumentException("Email và OTP không được null");
        }

        OtpData existing = otpStore.get(email);
        if (existing != null) {
            long elapsedSeconds = (System.currentTimeMillis() - existing.createdTime()) / 1000;
            if (elapsedSeconds < RESEND_COOLDOWN_SECONDS) {
                log.warn("[OTP] Rate limit — email {} vừa yêu cầu OTP {}s trước",
                        maskEmail(email), elapsedSeconds);
                throw new IllegalStateException(
                        "Vui lòng đợi " + (RESEND_COOLDOWN_SECONDS - elapsedSeconds)
                                + " giây trước khi yêu cầu OTP mới");
            }
        }

        otpStore.put(email, new OtpData(otp, System.currentTimeMillis()));
        attemptStore.put(email, new AttemptData());

        log.info("[OTP] Đã lưu OTP cho {}", maskEmail(email));
    }

    @Override
    public String getOtp(String email) {
        OtpData data = otpStore.get(email);
        if (data == null) return null;
        if (data.isExpired()) {
            otpStore.remove(email);
            return null;
        }
        return data.otp();
    }

    @Override
    public void deleteOtp(String email) {
        otpStore.remove(email);
    }

    @Override
    public boolean verify(String email, String otp, OtpPurpose purpose) {
        return isOtpValid(email, otp);
    }

    public boolean isOtpValid(String email, String otp) {
        if (email == null || otp == null) return false;

        AttemptData attempts = attemptStore.get(email);
        if (attempts != null && attempts.isLocked()) {
            log.warn("[OTP] Email {} đang bị lockout do sai quá nhiều lần", maskEmail(email));
            return false;
        }

        String storedOtp = getOtp(email);
        if (storedOtp == null) {
            log.info("[OTP] Không có OTP hoặc đã hết hạn cho {}", maskEmail(email));
            return false;
        }

        boolean matches = constantTimeEquals(storedOtp, otp);

        if (!matches) {
            int current = attemptStore
                    .computeIfAbsent(email, k -> new AttemptData())
                    .incrementAndCheck();

            log.warn("[OTP] Verify thất bại cho {} — lần thử {}/{}",
                    maskEmail(email), current, MAX_ATTEMPTS);

            if (current >= MAX_ATTEMPTS) {
                log.error("[OTP] Email {} bị lock {} phút sau {} lần sai",
                        maskEmail(email), LOCKOUT_MINUTES, current);
            }
            return false;
        }

        deleteOtp(email);
        attemptStore.remove(email);
        log.info("[OTP] Verify thành công cho {}", maskEmail(email));
        return true;
    }

    @Override
    public void markEmailAsVerified(String email) {
        verifiedEmails.put(email, new VerifiedData(System.currentTimeMillis()));
        log.info("[OTP] Đánh dấu verified cho {}", maskEmail(email));
    }

    @Override
    public boolean isEmailVerified(String email) {
        VerifiedData data = verifiedEmails.get(email);
        if (data == null) return false;
        if (data.isExpired()) {
            verifiedEmails.remove(email);
            return false;
        }
        return true;
    }

    @Override
    public void clearEmailVerified(String email) {
        verifiedEmails.remove(email);
        log.info("[OTP] Xóa verified cho {}", maskEmail(email));
    }

    @Scheduled(fixedRate = 300_000)
    public void cleanExpiredData() {
        long now = System.currentTimeMillis();

        otpStore.entrySet().removeIf(e ->
                (now - e.getValue().createdTime()) > OTP_EXPIRY_MINUTES * 60_000L);

        verifiedEmails.entrySet().removeIf(e ->
                (now - e.getValue().createdTime()) > VERIFIED_EXPIRY_MINUTES * 60_000L);

        attemptStore.entrySet().removeIf(e -> e.getValue().isLockoutExpired());

        log.debug("[OTP] Cleanup xong. otp={}, verified={}, attempts={}",
                otpStore.size(), verifiedEmails.size(), attemptStore.size());
    }

    private String generateOtp() {
        StringBuilder sb = new StringBuilder(OTP_LENGTH);
        for (int i = 0; i < OTP_LENGTH; i++) {
            sb.append(SECURE_RANDOM.nextInt(10));
        }
        return sb.toString();
    }

    private String maskEmail(String email) {
        if (email == null || !email.contains("@")) return "***";
        String[] parts = email.split("@");
        String local = parts[0];
        String domain = parts[1];
        String masked = local.length() <= 2
                ? local.charAt(0) + "***"
                : local.substring(0, 2) + "***";
        return masked + "@" + domain;
    }

    private boolean constantTimeEquals(String a, String b) {
        if (a == null || b == null) return false;
        if (a.length() != b.length()) return false;
        int diff = 0;
        for (int i = 0; i < a.length(); i++) {
            diff |= a.charAt(i) ^ b.charAt(i);
        }
        return diff == 0;
    }

    private static class OtpData {
        private final String otp;
        private final long createdTime;
        OtpData(String otp, long createdTime) {
            this.otp = otp;
            this.createdTime = createdTime;
        }
        String otp() { return otp; }
        long createdTime() { return createdTime; }
        boolean isExpired() {
            return (System.currentTimeMillis() - createdTime) > OTP_EXPIRY_MINUTES * 60_000L;
        }
    }

    private static class VerifiedData {
        private final long createdTime;
        VerifiedData(long createdTime) { this.createdTime = createdTime; }
        long createdTime() { return createdTime; }
        boolean isExpired() {
            return (System.currentTimeMillis() - createdTime) > VERIFIED_EXPIRY_MINUTES * 60_000L;
        }
    }

    private static class AttemptData {
        private final AtomicInteger count = new AtomicInteger(0);
        private volatile long lockedUntil = 0;

        int incrementAndCheck() {
            int current = count.incrementAndGet();
            if (current >= MAX_ATTEMPTS) {
                lockedUntil = System.currentTimeMillis() + LOCKOUT_MINUTES * 60_000L;
            }
            return current;
        }

        boolean isLocked() {
            return System.currentTimeMillis() < lockedUntil;
        }

        boolean isLockoutExpired() {
            return lockedUntil > 0 && System.currentTimeMillis() >= lockedUntil;
        }
    }
}

// ============================================================
// FILE: ./src/main/java/com/ecms_web_application/OC01/service/impl/rbac/AccountPermissionServiceImpl.java
// ============================================================
package com.ecms_web_application.OC01.service.impl.rbac;

import lombok.RequiredArgsConstructor;
import lombok.extern.slf4j.Slf4j;
import org.springframework.http.HttpStatus;
import org.springframework.stereotype.Service;
import org.springframework.transaction.annotation.Transactional;
import org.springframework.util.AntPathMatcher;
import com.ecms_web_application.OC01.config.security.PermissionManager;
import com.ecms_web_application.OC01.dto.request.rbac.AssignPermissionRequest;
import com.ecms_web_application.OC01.dto.request.rbac.AssignPermissionsBatchRequest;
import com.ecms_web_application.OC01.dto.response.rbac.AccountPermissionResponse;
import com.ecms_web_application.OC01.dto.response.rbac.PermissionCheckResponse;
import com.ecms_web_application.OC01.entity.Account;
import com.ecms_web_application.OC01.entity.AccountPermission;
import com.ecms_web_application.OC01.entity.Permission;
import com.ecms_web_application.OC01.exception.ErrorHandler;
import com.ecms_web_application.OC01.repository.AccountPermissionRepository;
import com.ecms_web_application.OC01.repository.AccountRepository;
import com.ecms_web_application.OC01.repository.PermissionRepository;
import com.ecms_web_application.OC01.service.AccountPermissionService;

import java.util.*;
import java.util.stream.Collectors;

@Service
@RequiredArgsConstructor
@Slf4j
public class AccountPermissionServiceImpl implements AccountPermissionService {

    private final AccountRepository accountRepository;
    private final PermissionRepository permissionRepository;
    private final AccountPermissionRepository accountPermissionRepository;
    private final PermissionManager permissionManager;

    private final AntPathMatcher pathMatcher = new AntPathMatcher();

    // ============================================================
    // QUERY
    // ============================================================
    @Override
    @Transactional(readOnly = true)
    public List<AccountPermissionResponse> getAllAccountPermissions(Long accountId) {
        ensureAccountExists(accountId);
        return accountPermissionRepository
                .findWithPermissionDetailByAccountId(accountId)
                .stream()
                .map(this::toResponse)
                .toList();
    }

    @Override
    @Transactional(readOnly = true)
    public List<AccountPermissionResponse> getCustomAccountPermissions(Long accountId) {
        ensureAccountExists(accountId);
