        return accountPermissionRepository
                .findByAccount_Id(accountId)
                .stream()
                .map(ap -> toResponse(ap, ap.getPermission()))
                .toList();
    }

    // ============================================================
    // ASSIGN
    // ============================================================
    @Override
    @Transactional
    public void assignPermission(Long accountId, AssignPermissionRequest request) {
        Account account = getAccountOrThrow(accountId);
        Permission permission = getPermissionOrThrow(request.getPermissionId());

        Optional<AccountPermission> existing =
                accountPermissionRepository.findByAccount_IdAndPermission_Id(accountId, permission.getId());

        if (existing.isPresent()) {
            AccountPermission ap = existing.get();
            if (Boolean.TRUE.equals(ap.getIsEnabled())) {
                throw new ErrorHandler(HttpStatus.CONFLICT, "Quyền đã được gán và đang hoạt động");
            }
            ap.setIsEnabled(true);
            accountPermissionRepository.save(ap);
        } else {
            AccountPermission ap = new AccountPermission();
            ap.setAccount(account);
            ap.setPermission(permission);
            ap.setIsEnabled(true);
            accountPermissionRepository.save(ap);
        }

        permissionManager.clearCache(accountId);
        log.info("Gán permission {} cho account {}", permission.getId(), accountId);
    }

    @Override
    @Transactional
    public void assignPermissionsBatch(Long accountId, AssignPermissionsBatchRequest request) {
        Account account = getAccountOrThrow(accountId);
        List<Long> ids = request.getPermissionIds();

        List<Permission> permissions = permissionRepository.findAllById(ids);
        if (permissions.size() != ids.size()) {
            Set<Long> found = permissions.stream().map(Permission::getId).collect(Collectors.toSet());
            List<Long> missing = ids.stream().filter(id -> !found.contains(id)).toList();
            throw new ErrorHandler(HttpStatus.NOT_FOUND, "Permission không tồn tại: " + missing);
        }

        Map<Long, AccountPermission> existingMap = accountPermissionRepository
                .findByAccount_Id(accountId)
                .stream()
                .collect(Collectors.toMap(ap -> ap.getPermission().getId(), ap -> ap));

        List<AccountPermission> toSave = new ArrayList<>();
        for (Permission p : permissions) {
            AccountPermission existing = existingMap.get(p.getId());
            if (existing != null) {
                if (!Boolean.TRUE.equals(existing.getIsEnabled())) {
                    existing.setIsEnabled(true);
                    toSave.add(existing);
                }
            } else {
                AccountPermission ap = new AccountPermission();
                ap.setAccount(account);
                ap.setPermission(p);
                ap.setIsEnabled(true);
                toSave.add(ap);
            }
        }

        if (!toSave.isEmpty()) {
            accountPermissionRepository.saveAll(toSave);
        }
        permissionManager.clearCache(accountId);
        log.info("Gán batch {} permission cho account {}", toSave.size(), accountId);
    }

    // ============================================================
    // REMOVE / ACTIVATE / DEACTIVATE
    // ============================================================
    @Override
    @Transactional
    public void removePermission(Long accountId, Long permissionId) {
        AccountPermission ap = accountPermissionRepository
                .findByAccount_IdAndPermission_Id(accountId, permissionId)
                .orElseThrow(() -> new ErrorHandler(HttpStatus.NOT_FOUND, "Account chưa có quyền này"));

        accountPermissionRepository.delete(ap);
        permissionManager.clearCache(accountId);
    }

    @Override
    @Transactional
    public void deactivatePermission(Long accountId, Long permissionId) {
        AccountPermission ap = accountPermissionRepository
                .findByAccount_IdAndPermission_Id(accountId, permissionId)
                .orElseThrow(() -> new ErrorHandler(HttpStatus.NOT_FOUND, "Account chưa có quyền này"));

        if (!Boolean.TRUE.equals(ap.getIsEnabled())) {
            throw new ErrorHandler(HttpStatus.CONFLICT, "Quyền đã bị vô hiệu hóa");
        }
        ap.setIsEnabled(false);
        accountPermissionRepository.save(ap);
        permissionManager.clearCache(accountId);
    }

    @Override
    @Transactional
    public void activatePermission(Long accountId, Long permissionId) {
        AccountPermission ap = accountPermissionRepository
                .findByAccount_IdAndPermission_Id(accountId, permissionId)
                .orElseThrow(() -> new ErrorHandler(HttpStatus.NOT_FOUND, "Account chưa có quyền này"));

        if (Boolean.TRUE.equals(ap.getIsEnabled())) {
            throw new ErrorHandler(HttpStatus.CONFLICT, "Quyền đã đang hoạt động");
        }
        ap.setIsEnabled(true);
        accountPermissionRepository.save(ap);
        permissionManager.clearCache(accountId);
    }

    @Override
    @Transactional
    public void removeAllPermissions(Long accountId) {
        ensureAccountExists(accountId);
        accountPermissionRepository.deleteAllByAccountId(accountId);
        permissionManager.clearCache(accountId);
    }

    // ============================================================
    // CHECK
    // ============================================================
    @Override
    @Transactional(readOnly = true)
    public PermissionCheckResponse checkPermission(Long accountId, String permissionName) {
        ensureAccountExists(accountId);
        String[] parts = permissionName.split(":", 2);
        if (parts.length != 2) {
            throw new ErrorHandler(HttpStatus.BAD_REQUEST,
                    "permissionName phải có format '{METHOD}:{endpoint}'");
        }
        boolean has = hasPermission(accountId, parts[0], parts[1]);

        PermissionCheckResponse res = new PermissionCheckResponse();
        res.setAccountId(accountId);
        res.setPermissionName(permissionName);
        res.setHasPermission(has);
        res.setMessage(has ? "Account có quyền" : "Account không có quyền");
        return res;
    }

    @Override
    @Transactional(readOnly = true)
    public PermissionCheckResponse checkPermissionByResource(Long accountId, String resource, String action) {
        ensureAccountExists(accountId);
        boolean has = permissionRepository.findByModule(resource).stream()
                .anyMatch(p -> p.getMethod().equalsIgnoreCase(action)
                        && accountPermissionRepository
                        .existsByAccount_IdAndPermission_IdAndIsEnabledTrue(accountId, p.getId()));

        PermissionCheckResponse res = new PermissionCheckResponse();
        res.setAccountId(accountId);
        res.setResource(resource);
        res.setAction(action);
        res.setHasPermission(has);
        res.setMessage(has ? "Account có quyền" : "Account không có quyền");
        return res;
    }

    @Override
    @Transactional(readOnly = true)
    public boolean hasPermission(Long accountId, String method, String endpoint) {
        if (accountId == null || method == null || endpoint == null) return false;

        String upperMethod = method.toUpperCase();
        String normalizedEndpoint = normalizePath(endpoint);

        Map<String, List<String>> cached = permissionManager.getPermissions(accountId);
        if (cached == null) {
            cached = loadAndCachePermissions(accountId);
        }

        List<String> patterns = cached.get(upperMethod);
        if (patterns == null || patterns.isEmpty()) return false;

        for (String pattern : patterns) {
            if (pathMatcher.match(pattern, normalizedEndpoint)) {
                return true;
            }
        }
        return false;
    }

    // ============================================================
    // HELPERS
    // ============================================================
    private Map<String, List<String>> loadAndCachePermissions(Long accountId) {
        List<Permission> permissions =
                accountPermissionRepository.findEnabledPermissionsByAccountId(accountId);

        Map<String, List<String>> grouped = permissions.stream()
                .collect(Collectors.groupingBy(
                        p -> p.getMethod().toUpperCase(),
                        Collectors.mapping(Permission::getEndpointPattern, Collectors.toList())
                ));

        permissionManager.putPermissions(accountId, grouped);
        return grouped;
    }

    private String normalizePath(String path) {
        if (path == null) return "/";
        if (path.length() > 1 && path.endsWith("/")) {
            return path.substring(0, path.length() - 1);
        }
        return path;
    }

    private Account getAccountOrThrow(Long accountId) {
        return accountRepository.findById(accountId)
                .orElseThrow(() -> new ErrorHandler(HttpStatus.NOT_FOUND, "Account không tồn tại"));
    }

    private Permission getPermissionOrThrow(Long permissionId) {
        return permissionRepository.findById(permissionId)
                .orElseThrow(() -> new ErrorHandler(HttpStatus.NOT_FOUND, "Permission không tồn tại"));
    }

    private void ensureAccountExists(Long accountId) {
        if (!accountRepository.existsById(accountId)) {
            throw new ErrorHandler(HttpStatus.NOT_FOUND, "Account không tồn tại");
        }
    }

    private AccountPermissionResponse toResponse(AccountPermission ap) {
        return toResponse(ap, ap.getPermission());
    }

    private AccountPermissionResponse toResponse(AccountPermission ap, Permission p) {
        AccountPermissionResponse res = new AccountPermissionResponse();
        res.setPermissionId(p.getId());
        res.setMethod(p.getMethod());
        res.setEndpointPattern(p.getEndpointPattern());
        res.setModule(p.getModule());
        res.setDescription(p.getDescription());
        res.setIsEnabled(ap.getIsEnabled());
        res.setAssignedAt(ap.getAssignedAt());
        res.setUpdatedAt(ap.getUpdatedAt());
        return res;
    }
}

// ============================================================
// FILE: ./src/main/java/com/ecms_web_application/OC01/service/impl/rbac/AccountRoleServiceImpl.java
// ============================================================
package com.ecms_web_application.OC01.service.impl.rbac;

import lombok.RequiredArgsConstructor;
import lombok.extern.slf4j.Slf4j;
import org.springframework.data.domain.Page;
import org.springframework.data.domain.Pageable;
import org.springframework.http.HttpStatus;
import org.springframework.stereotype.Service;
import org.springframework.transaction.annotation.Transactional;
import com.ecms_web_application.OC01.config.security.PermissionManager;
import com.ecms_web_application.OC01.dto.request.AccountLockRequest;
import com.ecms_web_application.OC01.dto.request.rbac.AssignRoleRequest;
import com.ecms_web_application.OC01.dto.response.rbac.AccountResponse;
import com.ecms_web_application.OC01.dto.response.rbac.AccountRoleResponse;
import com.ecms_web_application.OC01.entity.Account;
import com.ecms_web_application.OC01.entity.Role;
import com.ecms_web_application.OC01.entity.enums.RevokedReason;                    // ✅ thêm
import com.ecms_web_application.OC01.exception.ErrorHandler;
import com.ecms_web_application.OC01.repository.AccountRepository;
import com.ecms_web_application.OC01.repository.AuthSessionRepository;
import com.ecms_web_application.OC01.repository.RoleRepository;
import com.ecms_web_application.OC01.service.AccountRoleService;
import com.ecms_web_application.OC01.service.AuditLogService;                     // ⚠️ đổi theo project
import com.ecms_web_application.OC01.service.EmailService;                       // ✅ thêm

import java.time.LocalDateTime;
import java.util.List;

@Service
@RequiredArgsConstructor
@Slf4j
public class AccountRoleServiceImpl implements AccountRoleService {

    private final AccountRepository accountRepository;
    private final RoleRepository roleRepository;
    private final PermissionManager permissionManager;
    private final AuthSessionRepository authSessionRepository;
    private final EmailService emailService;          // ✅ inject
    private final AuditLogService auditLogService;    // ⚠️ bỏ nếu project chưa có

    private static final String INSTRUCTOR_ROLE_CODE = "INSTRUCTOR";

    // ==================== ROLE ====================

    @Override
    @Transactional(readOnly = true)
    public List<AccountRoleResponse> getAccountRoles(Long accountId) {
        Account account = getAccountOrThrow(accountId);
        if (account.getRole() == null) {
            return List.of();
        }
        Role r = account.getRole();
        AccountRoleResponse res = new AccountRoleResponse();
        res.setRoleId(r.getId());
        res.setCode(r.getCode());
        res.setDescription(r.getDescription());
        return List.of(res);
    }

    @Override
    @Transactional
    public void assignRoleToAccount(Long accountId, AssignRoleRequest request) {
        Account account = getAccountOrThrow(accountId);
        Role role = roleRepository.findById(request.getRoleId())
                .orElseThrow(() -> new ErrorHandler(HttpStatus.NOT_FOUND, "Role không tồn tại"));

        account.setRole(role);
        accountRepository.save(account);
        permissionManager.clearCache(accountId);
        log.info("Gán role {} cho account {}", role.getCode(), accountId);
    }

    @Override
    @Transactional
    public void removeRoleFromAccount(Long accountId) {
        Account account = getAccountOrThrow(accountId);
        account.setRole(null);
        accountRepository.save(account);
        permissionManager.clearCache(accountId);
        log.info("Xóa role khỏi account {}", accountId);
    }

    // ==================== SEARCH ====================

    @Override
    @Transactional(readOnly = true)
    public Page<AccountResponse> getAccountsByRole(Long roleId, Pageable pageable) {
        if (!roleRepository.existsById(roleId)) {
            throw new ErrorHandler(HttpStatus.NOT_FOUND, "Role không tồn tại");
        }
        return accountRepository.findByRoleIdPageable(roleId, pageable)
                .map(this::toResponse);
    }

    @Override
    @Transactional(readOnly = true)
    public Page<AccountResponse> searchAccounts(
            String roleCode, Boolean isActive, String search, Pageable pageable) {
        // ⚠️ FIX: String params PHẢI dùng "" thay vì null (PostgreSQL lower(bytea))
        return accountRepository
                .searchAccounts(emptyToBlank(roleCode), isActive, emptyToBlank(search), pageable)
                .map(this::toResponse);
    }

    @Override
    @Transactional(readOnly = true)
    public AccountResponse getAccountById(Long accountId) {
        Account account = accountRepository.findByIdWithRoleAndProfile(accountId)
                .orElseThrow(() -> new ErrorHandler(HttpStatus.NOT_FOUND, "Account không tồn tại"));
        return toResponse(account);
    }

    @Override
    @Transactional(readOnly = true)
    public List<AccountResponse> getTeacherAccounts() {
        return accountRepository.findByRoleCode(INSTRUCTOR_ROLE_CODE)
                .stream()
                .map(this::toResponse)
                .toList();
    }

    // ==================== LOCK / UNLOCK ====================

    @Override
    @Transactional
    public void lockAccount(Long accountId, AccountLockRequest request) {
        Account account = getAccountOrThrow(accountId);
        boolean isLocked = Boolean.TRUE.equals(request.getIsLocked());
        account.setIsLocked(isLocked);

        if (isLocked) {
            account.setLockedUntil(null);

            // ✅ Truyền String mô tả đầy đủ — lưu nguyên văn vào DB
            String reasonText = "Admin khóa tài khoản: "
                    + (request.getReason() != null && !request.getReason().isBlank()
                    ? request.getReason()
                    : "Không có lý do");
            authSessionRepository.revokeAllByAccountId(
                    account.getId(),
                    LocalDateTime.now(),
                    reasonText
            );

            try {
                auditLogService.log(
                        "LOCK_ACCOUNT", "Account", accountId,
                        "Admin khóa tài khoản: " + request.getReason());
            } catch (Exception e) {
                log.warn("[LockAccount] Audit log thất bại: {}", e.getMessage());
            }

            sendLockEmail(account, request.getReason());

            log.info("Khóa tài khoản accountId={}, email={}, reason={}",
                    accountId, account.getEmail(), request.getReason());
        } else {
            account.setLockedUntil(null);
            account.setFailedLoginCount(0);
            log.info("Mở khóa tài khoản accountId={}, email={}", accountId, account.getEmail());
        }

        accountRepository.save(account);
    }
    // ==================== HELPERS ====================

    private Account getAccountOrThrow(Long accountId) {
        return accountRepository.findById(accountId)
                .orElseThrow(() -> new ErrorHandler(HttpStatus.NOT_FOUND, "Account không tồn tại"));
    }

    /** Gửi mail thông báo khóa tài khoản. Lỗi không làm rollback transaction. */
    private void sendLockEmail(Account acc, String reason) {
        try {
            String fullName = acc.getUserProfile() != null
                    ? acc.getUserProfile().getFullName()
                    : acc.getEmail();
            emailService.sendAccountLockedEmail(acc.getEmail(), fullName, reason, null);
        } catch (Exception e) {
            log.warn("[LockAccount] Gửi mail thất bại cho {}: {}", acc.getEmail(), e.getMessage());
        }
    }

    /** FIX: trả "" thay vì null cho String params */
    private String emptyToBlank(String s) {
        return (s == null || s.isBlank()) ? "" : s.trim();
    }

    private AccountResponse toResponse(Account a) {
        AccountResponse res = new AccountResponse();
        res.setAccountId(a.getId());
        res.setEmail(a.getEmail());
        if (a.getRole() != null) {
            res.setRoleId(a.getRole().getId());
            res.setRoleCode(a.getRole().getCode());
        }
        if (a.getUserProfile() != null) {
            res.setFullName(a.getUserProfile().getFullName());
            res.setPhone(a.getUserProfile().getPhone());
            res.setAvatarUrl(a.getUserProfile().getAvatarUrl());
        }
        res.setIsActive(a.getIsActive());
        res.setIsLocked(a.getIsLocked());
        res.setEmailVerifiedAt(a.getEmailVerifiedAt());
        res.setLastLoginAt(a.getLastLoginAt());
        res.setLockedUntil(a.getLockedUntil());
        res.setCreatedAt(a.getCreatedAt());
        res.setUpdatedAt(a.getUpdatedAt());
        return res;
    }
}

// ============================================================
// FILE: ./src/main/java/com/ecms_web_application/OC01/service/impl/rbac/PermissionServiceImpl.java
// ============================================================
package com.ecms_web_application.OC01.service.impl.rbac;

import lombok.RequiredArgsConstructor;
import lombok.extern.slf4j.Slf4j;
import org.springframework.data.domain.Page;
import org.springframework.data.domain.Pageable;
import org.springframework.http.HttpStatus;
import org.springframework.stereotype.Service;
import org.springframework.transaction.annotation.Transactional;
import org.springframework.util.AntPathMatcher;
import com.ecms_web_application.OC01.config.security.PermissionManager;
import com.ecms_web_application.OC01.dto.request.rbac.PermissionRequest;
import com.ecms_web_application.OC01.dto.response.rbac.PermissionResponse;
import com.ecms_web_application.OC01.entity.Permission;
import com.ecms_web_application.OC01.exception.ErrorHandler;
import com.ecms_web_application.OC01.repository.AccountPermissionRepository;
import com.ecms_web_application.OC01.repository.PermissionRepository;
import com.ecms_web_application.OC01.service.PermissionService;

import java.util.List;

@Service
@RequiredArgsConstructor
@Slf4j
public class PermissionServiceImpl implements PermissionService {

    private final PermissionRepository permissionRepository;
    private final AccountPermissionRepository accountPermissionRepository;
    private final PermissionManager permissionManager;

    /** ⚠️ ĐỔI: module mặc định — "public" cho mọi user authenticated */
    private static final String DEFAULT_MODULE = "public";

    @Override
    @Transactional(readOnly = true)
    public Page<PermissionResponse> searchPermissions(String module, String search, Pageable pageable) {
        return permissionRepository
                .searchPermissions(emptyToBlank(module), emptyToBlank(search), pageable)
                .map(this::toResponse);
    }

    @Override
    @Transactional(readOnly = true)
    public List<PermissionResponse> getAllPermissions() {
        return permissionRepository.findAll()
                .stream()
                .map(this::toResponse)
                .toList();
    }

    @Override
    @Transactional(readOnly = true)
    public List<PermissionResponse> getDefaultPermissions() {
        return permissionRepository.findByModule(DEFAULT_MODULE)
                .stream()
                .map(this::toResponse)
                .toList();
    }

    @Override
    @Transactional(readOnly = true)
    public PermissionResponse getPermissionById(Long permissionId) {
        Permission p = permissionRepository.findById(permissionId)
                .orElseThrow(() -> new ErrorHandler(HttpStatus.NOT_FOUND, "Permission không tồn tại"));
        return toResponse(p);
    }

    @Override
    @Transactional
    public PermissionResponse createPermission(PermissionRequest request) {
        if (permissionRepository.existsByMethodAndEndpointPattern(
                request.getMethod(), request.getEndpointPattern())) {
            throw new ErrorHandler(HttpStatus.CONFLICT,
                    "Permission đã tồn tại: " + request.getMethod() + " " + request.getEndpointPattern());
        }

        Permission p = new Permission();
        p.setMethod(request.getMethod());
        p.setEndpointPattern(request.getEndpointPattern());
        p.setModule(request.getModule());
        p.setDescription(request.getDescription());

        Permission saved = permissionRepository.save(p);
        log.info("Tạo permission: {} {}", saved.getMethod(), saved.getEndpointPattern());
        return toResponse(saved);
    }

    @Override
    @Transactional
    public PermissionResponse updatePermission(Long permissionId, PermissionRequest request) {
        Permission p = permissionRepository.findById(permissionId)
                .orElseThrow(() -> new ErrorHandler(HttpStatus.NOT_FOUND, "Permission không tồn tại"));

        boolean changingKey = !p.getMethod().equals(request.getMethod())
                || !p.getEndpointPattern().equals(request.getEndpointPattern());

        if (changingKey && permissionRepository.existsByMethodAndEndpointPattern(
                request.getMethod(), request.getEndpointPattern())) {
            throw new ErrorHandler(HttpStatus.CONFLICT,
                    "Permission đã tồn tại: " + request.getMethod() + " " + request.getEndpointPattern());
        }

        p.setMethod(request.getMethod());
        p.setEndpointPattern(request.getEndpointPattern());
        p.setModule(request.getModule());
        p.setDescription(request.getDescription());

        Permission saved = permissionRepository.save(p);
        permissionManager.clearAllCache();
        log.info("Cập nhật permission id={}", permissionId);
        return toResponse(saved);
    }

    @Override
    @Transactional
    public void deletePermission(Long permissionId) {
        Permission p = permissionRepository.findById(permissionId)
                .orElseThrow(() -> new ErrorHandler(HttpStatus.NOT_FOUND, "Permission không tồn tại"));

        permissionRepository.delete(p);
        permissionManager.clearAllCache();
        log.info("Xóa permission: {} {}", p.getMethod(), p.getEndpointPattern());
    }

    @Override
    @Transactional(readOnly = true)
    public List<String> getDistinctModules() {
        return permissionRepository.findAllDistinctModules();
    }

    @Override
    @Transactional(readOnly = true)
    public boolean existsByMethodAndEndpoint(String method, String endpoint) {
        List<Permission> candidates = permissionRepository.findByMethod(method.toUpperCase());

        AntPathMatcher matcher = new AntPathMatcher();
        for (Permission p : candidates) {
            if (matcher.match(p.getEndpointPattern(), endpoint)) {
                return true;
            }
        }
        return false;
    }

    private PermissionResponse toResponse(Permission p) {
        PermissionResponse res = new PermissionResponse();
        res.setId(p.getId());
        res.setMethod(p.getMethod());
        res.setEndpointPattern(p.getEndpointPattern());
        res.setModule(p.getModule());
        res.setDescription(p.getDescription());
        res.setCreatedAt(p.getCreatedAt());
        res.setUpdatedAt(p.getUpdatedAt());
        return res;
    }

    private String emptyToNull(String s) {
        return (s == null || s.isBlank()) ? null : s.trim();
    }

    private String emptyToBlank(String s) {
        return (s == null || s.isBlank()) ? "" : s.trim();
    }
}

// ============================================================
// FILE: ./src/main/java/com/ecms_web_application/OC01/service/impl/RoleServiceImpl.java
// ============================================================
package com.ecms_web_application.OC01.service.impl.rbac;

import lombok.RequiredArgsConstructor;
import lombok.extern.slf4j.Slf4j;
import org.springframework.http.HttpStatus;
import org.springframework.stereotype.Service;
import org.springframework.transaction.annotation.Transactional;
import com.ecms_web_application.OC01.config.security.PermissionManager;
import com.ecms_web_application.OC01.dto.request.rbac.RoleRequest;
import com.ecms_web_application.OC01.dto.response.rbac.RoleResponse;
import com.ecms_web_application.OC01.entity.Role;
import com.ecms_web_application.OC01.exception.ErrorHandler;
import com.ecms_web_application.OC01.repository.AccountRepository;
import com.ecms_web_application.OC01.repository.RoleRepository;
import com.ecms_web_application.OC01.service.RoleService;

import java.util.List;

@Service
@RequiredArgsConstructor
@Slf4j
public class RoleServiceImpl implements RoleService {

    private final RoleRepository roleRepository;
    private final AccountRepository accountRepository;
    private final PermissionManager permissionManager;

    @Override
    @Transactional(readOnly = true)
    public List<RoleResponse> getAllRoles() {
        return roleRepository.findAllByOrderByCodeAsc()
                .stream()
                .map(this::toResponse)
                .toList();
    }

    @Override
    @Transactional(readOnly = true)
    public RoleResponse getRoleById(Long roleId) {
        Role role = roleRepository.findById(roleId)
                .orElseThrow(() -> new ErrorHandler(HttpStatus.NOT_FOUND, "Role không tồn tại"));
        return toResponse(role);
    }

    @Override
    @Transactional
    public RoleResponse createRole(RoleRequest request) {
        if (roleRepository.existsByCode(request.getCode())) {
            throw new ErrorHandler(HttpStatus.CONFLICT, "Mã role đã tồn tại: " + request.getCode());
        }

        Role role = new Role();
        role.setCode(request.getCode());
        role.setDescription(request.getDescription());

        Role saved = roleRepository.save(role);
        log.info("Tạo role mới: {}", saved.getCode());
        return toResponse(saved);
    }

    @Override
    @Transactional
    public RoleResponse updateRole(Long roleId, RoleRequest request) {
        Role role = roleRepository.findById(roleId)
                .orElseThrow(() -> new ErrorHandler(HttpStatus.NOT_FOUND, "Role không tồn tại"));

        if (!role.getCode().equals(request.getCode())
                && roleRepository.existsByCode(request.getCode())) {
            throw new ErrorHandler(HttpStatus.CONFLICT, "Mã role đã tồn tại: " + request.getCode());
        }

        role.setCode(request.getCode());
        role.setDescription(request.getDescription());

        Role saved = roleRepository.save(role);
        permissionManager.clearAllCache();
        log.info("Cập nhật role id={}", roleId);
        return toResponse(saved);
    }

    @Override
    @Transactional
    public void deleteRole(Long roleId) {
        Role role = roleRepository.findById(roleId)
                .orElseThrow(() -> new ErrorHandler(HttpStatus.NOT_FOUND, "Role không tồn tại"));

        long count = accountRepository.findByRoleId(roleId).size();
        if (count > 0) {
            throw new ErrorHandler(HttpStatus.CONFLICT,
                    "Không thể xóa role — còn " + count + " account đang sử dụng");
        }

        roleRepository.delete(role);
        permissionManager.clearAllCache();
        log.info("Xóa role: {}", role.getCode());
    }

    private RoleResponse toResponse(Role role) {
        RoleResponse res = new RoleResponse();
        res.setId(role.getId());
        res.setCode(role.getCode());
        res.setDescription(role.getDescription());
        res.setCreatedAt(role.getCreatedAt());
        res.setUpdatedAt(role.getUpdatedAt());
        return res;
    }
}

// ============================================================
// FILE: ./src/main/java/com/ecms_web_application/OC01/service/impl/StudentServiceImpl.java
// ============================================================
package com.ecms_web_application.OC01.service.impl;

import com.ecms_web_application.OC01.config.SecurityUtils;
import com.ecms_web_application.OC01.dto.request.GuardianRequest;
import com.ecms_web_application.OC01.dto.request.StudentAccountLockRequest;
import com.ecms_web_application.OC01.dto.request.StudentConvertFromLeadRequest;
import com.ecms_web_application.OC01.dto.request.StudentCreateRequest;
import com.ecms_web_application.OC01.dto.request.StudentStatusChangeRequest;
import com.ecms_web_application.OC01.dto.request.StudentUpdateRequest;
import com.ecms_web_application.OC01.dto.response.AcademicHistoryResponse;
import com.ecms_web_application.OC01.dto.response.GuardianResponse;
import com.ecms_web_application.OC01.dto.response.StudentDetailResponse;
import com.ecms_web_application.OC01.dto.response.StudentResponse;
import com.ecms_web_application.OC01.entity.AcademicHistory;
import com.ecms_web_application.OC01.entity.Account;
import com.ecms_web_application.OC01.entity.Guardian;
import com.ecms_web_application.OC01.entity.Lead;
import com.ecms_web_application.OC01.entity.Student;
import com.ecms_web_application.OC01.entity.StudentStatusLog;
import com.ecms_web_application.OC01.exception.ErrorHandler;
import com.ecms_web_application.OC01.repository.AcademicHistoryRepository;
import com.ecms_web_application.OC01.repository.AccountRepository;
import com.ecms_web_application.OC01.repository.AuthSessionRepository;
import com.ecms_web_application.OC01.repository.GuardianRepository;
import com.ecms_web_application.OC01.repository.LeadRepository;
import com.ecms_web_application.OC01.repository.StudentRepository;
import com.ecms_web_application.OC01.repository.StudentStatusLogRepository;
import com.ecms_web_application.OC01.service.StudentService;
import lombok.RequiredArgsConstructor;
import lombok.extern.slf4j.Slf4j;
import org.springframework.data.domain.Page;
import org.springframework.data.domain.Pageable;
import org.springframework.http.HttpStatus;
import org.springframework.stereotype.Service;
import org.springframework.transaction.annotation.Transactional;

import java.time.LocalDateTime;
import java.time.format.DateTimeFormatter;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.Optional;
import java.util.concurrent.ThreadLocalRandom;

@Service
@RequiredArgsConstructor
@Slf4j
public class StudentServiceImpl implements StudentService {

    private final StudentRepository studentRepository;
    private final LeadRepository leadRepository;
    private final GuardianRepository guardianRepository;
    private final AcademicHistoryRepository academicHistoryRepository;
    private final StudentStatusLogRepository statusLogRepository;
    private final AccountRepository accountRepository;
    private final AuthSessionRepository authSessionRepository;

    @Override
    @Transactional(readOnly = true)
    public Page<StudentResponse> search(String search, String status, Pageable pageable) {
        return studentRepository
                .searchStudents(emptyToNull(status), emptyToNull(search), pageable)
                .map(this::toResponse);
    }

    @Override
    @Transactional(readOnly = true)
    public StudentResponse getById(Long id) {
        Student student = findStudentById(id);
        return toResponse(student);
    }

    @Override
    @Transactional(readOnly = true)
    public StudentDetailResponse getDetailById(Long id) {
        Student student = findStudentById(id);
        StudentDetailResponse detail = new StudentDetailResponse();
        copyPropertiesToResponse(student, detail);

        List<GuardianResponse> guardians = guardianRepository.findByStudentId(id)
                .stream().map(this::toGuardianResponse).toList();
        List<AcademicHistoryResponse> academicHistories = academicHistoryRepository.findByStudentIdOrderByCreatedAtDesc(id)
                .stream().map(this::toAcademicHistoryResponse).toList();
        List<StudentStatusLog> statusLogs = statusLogRepository.findByStudentIdOrderByChangedAtDesc(id);

        detail.setGuardians(guardians);
        detail.setAcademicHistories(academicHistories);
        detail.setStatusLogs(statusLogs);
        return detail;
    }

    @Override
    @Transactional(readOnly = true)
    public StudentResponse getByStudentCode(String studentCode) {
        Student student = studentRepository.findByStudentCode(studentCode)
                .orElseThrow(() -> new ErrorHandler(HttpStatus.NOT_FOUND, "Không tìm thấy học viên với mã: " + studentCode));
        return toResponse(student);
    }

    @Override
    @Transactional
    public StudentResponse create(StudentCreateRequest request) {
        // 1. Mã học viên: nếu để trống thì sinh mã tự động STD + yyyyMM + 4 số ngẫu nhiên
        String code = emptyToNull(request.getStudentCode());
        if (code == null) {
            code = generateUniqueStudentCode();
        } else {
            code = code.trim().toUpperCase();
            if (studentRepository.existsByStudentCode(code)) {
                throw new ErrorHandler(HttpStatus.CONFLICT, "Mã học viên đã tồn tại: " + code);
            }
        }

        // 2. Kiểm tra leadId duy nhất (uk_students_lead)
        if (request.getLeadId() != null && studentRepository.existsByLeadId(request.getLeadId())) {
            throw new ErrorHandler(HttpStatus.CONFLICT, "Lead này đã được liên kết với một học viên khác");
        }

        Student student = new Student();
        student.setStudentCode(code);
        student.setFullName(request.getFullName().trim());
        student.setEmail(request.getEmail() != null ? request.getEmail().trim().toLowerCase() : null);
        student.setPhone(emptyToNull(request.getPhone()));
        student.setDob(request.getDateOfBirth());
        student.setAddress(emptyToNull(request.getAddress()));
        student.setLeadId(request.getLeadId());
        student.setUserId(request.getUserId());

        String status = (request.getStatus() != null && !request.getStatus().isBlank())
                ? request.getStatus().trim().toUpperCase()
                : "ACTIVE";
        student.setStatus(status);

        Student saved = studentRepository.save(student);
        Long currentUserId = SecurityUtils.getCurrentAccountId();

        // Ghi nhật ký trạng thái
        recordStatusLog(saved.getId(), null, saved.getStatus(), "Khởi tạo học viên mới", currentUserId);

        log.info("Tạo mới học viên id={}, code={}, fullName={}", saved.getId(), saved.getStudentCode(), saved.getFullName());
        return toResponse(saved);
    }

    /**
     * Chuyển đổi Lead thành Student sau khi đóng cọc thành công (Hỗ trợ Idempotent)
     */
    @Override
    @Transactional
    public StudentResponse convertFromLead(StudentConvertFromLeadRequest request) {
        Long leadId = request.getLeadId();

        // 1. Kiểm tra tính Idempotent: Nếu leadId đã được chuyển đổi sang Student thì trả về Student hiện tại
        Optional<Student> existingByLead = studentRepository.findByLeadId(leadId);
        if (existingByLead.isPresent()) {
            log.info("Idempotent check: Lead id={} đã được chuyển đổi thành Student id={}", leadId, existingByLead.get().getId());
            return toResponse(existingByLead.get());
        }

        // 2. Tìm Lead
        Lead lead = leadRepository.findById(leadId)
                .orElseThrow(() -> new ErrorHandler(HttpStatus.NOT_FOUND, "Không tìm thấy Lead với ID: " + leadId));

        if (lead.getStudentId() != null) {
            Optional<Student> existingStudent = studentRepository.findById(lead.getStudentId());
            if (existingStudent.isPresent()) {
                log.info("Idempotent check: Lead id={} đã gắn với studentId={}", leadId, lead.getStudentId());
                return toResponse(existingStudent.get());
            }
        }

        Long currentUserId = SecurityUtils.getCurrentAccountId();
        LocalDateTime now = LocalDateTime.now();
        Long effectiveCourseId = request.getCourseId() != null ? request.getCourseId() : lead.getCourseId();

        // 3. Tạo mới Student (học viên mới)
        String studentCode = generateUniqueStudentCode();
        Student student = new Student();
        student.setStudentCode(studentCode);
        student.setLeadId(lead.getId());
        student.setFullName(lead.getFullName());
        student.setEmail(lead.getEmail());
        student.setPhone(lead.getPhone());
        student.setStatus("ACTIVE");

        Student savedStudent = studentRepository.save(student);

        // 4. Cập nhật Lead (gán student_id và converted_at)
        lead.setStudentId(savedStudent.getId());
        lead.setConvertedAt(now);
        leadRepository.save(lead);

        // 5. Thêm bản ghi AcademicHistories ban đầu nếu có khóa học
        if (effectiveCourseId != null) {
            AcademicHistory history = new AcademicHistory();
            history.setStudentId(savedStudent.getId());
            history.setLeadId(lead.getId());
            history.setCourseId(effectiveCourseId);
            history.setClassId(request.getClassId());
            academicHistoryRepository.save(history);
        }

        // 6. Ghi log StudentStatusLogs
        recordStatusLog(savedStudent.getId(), null, "ACTIVE", "Chuyển đổi thành công từ Lead #" + lead.getId(), currentUserId);

        log.info("Chuyển đổi thành công Lead id={} sang Student id={}, studentCode={}", lead.getId(), savedStudent.getId(), savedStudent.getStudentCode());
        return toResponse(savedStudent);
    }

    @Override
    @Transactional
    public StudentResponse update(Long id, StudentUpdateRequest request) {
        Student student = findStudentById(id);

        // Bất biến: Tuyệt đối KHÔNG cho phép sửa mã học viên sau khi cấp
        if (request.getStudentCode() != null && !request.getStudentCode().isBlank()) {
            String requestedCode = request.getStudentCode().trim().toUpperCase();
            if (!requestedCode.equals(student.getStudentCode())) {
                throw new ErrorHandler(HttpStatus.BAD_REQUEST, "Quy tắc nghiệp vụ: Mã học viên (studentCode) là bất biến, không được phép chỉnh sửa sau khi đã cấp");
            }
        }

        if (request.getFullName() != null && !request.getFullName().isBlank()) {
            student.setFullName(request.getFullName().trim());
        }
        if (request.getEmail() != null) {
            student.setEmail(emptyToNull(request.getEmail().toLowerCase()));
        }
        if (request.getPhone() != null) {
            student.setPhone(emptyToNull(request.getPhone()));
        }
        if (request.getDateOfBirth() != null) {
            student.setDob(request.getDateOfBirth());
        }
        if (request.getAddress() != null) {
            student.setAddress(emptyToNull(request.getAddress()));
        }
        if (request.getUserId() != null) {
            student.setUserId(request.getUserId());
        }

        // Cập nhật trạng thái nếu có
        if (request.getStatus() != null && !request.getStatus().isBlank()) {
            String newStatus = request.getStatus().trim().toUpperCase();
            if (!newStatus.equalsIgnoreCase(student.getStatus())) {
                recordStatusLog(student.getId(), student.getStatus(), newStatus, "Cập nhật trạng thái qua API", SecurityUtils.getCurrentAccountId());
                student.setStatus(newStatus);
            }
        }

        Student saved = studentRepository.save(student);
        log.info("Cập nhật học viên id={}, code={}", saved.getId(), saved.getStudentCode());
        return toResponse(saved);
    }

    @Override
    @Transactional
    public StudentResponse updateStatus(Long id, StudentStatusChangeRequest request) {
        Student student = findStudentById(id);
        String oldStatus = student.getStatus();
        String newStatus = request.getStatus().trim().toUpperCase();

        if (!newStatus.equalsIgnoreCase(oldStatus)) {
            student.setStatus(newStatus);
            studentRepository.save(student);
            recordStatusLog(student.getId(), oldStatus, newStatus, request.getReason(), SecurityUtils.getCurrentAccountId());
            log.info("Đổi trạng thái học viên id={} từ {} sang {}", id, oldStatus, newStatus);
        }

        return toResponse(student);
    }

    @Override
    @Transactional
    public StudentResponse lockAccount(Long id, StudentAccountLockRequest request) {
        Student student = findStudentById(id);
        Account account = null;
        if (student.getUserId() != null) {
            account = accountRepository.findById(student.getUserId()).orElse(null);
        }
        if (account == null && student.getEmail() != null) {
            account = accountRepository.findByEmail(student.getEmail()).orElse(null);
            if (account != null && student.getUserId() == null) {
                student.setUserId(account.getId());
                studentRepository.save(student);
            }
        }

        if (account == null) {
            throw new ErrorHandler(HttpStatus.BAD_REQUEST, "Học viên này chưa có tài khoản đăng nhập trên hệ thống");
        }

        boolean isLocked = Boolean.TRUE.equals(request.getIsLocked());
        account.setIsLocked(isLocked);
        if (isLocked) {
            account.setLockedUntil(null);
            authSessionRepository.revokeAllByAccountId(
                    account.getId(),
                    LocalDateTime.now(),
                    "Admin khóa tài khoản: " + (request.getReason() != null ? request.getReason() : "")
            );
            log.info("Khóa tài khoản học viên: studentId={}, accountId={}, reason={}", id, account.getId(), request.getReason());
        } else {
            account.setLockedUntil(null);
            account.setFailedLoginCount(0);
            log.info("Mở khóa tài khoản học viên: studentId={}, accountId={}", id, account.getId());
        }
        accountRepository.save(account);

        String actionDesc = isLocked ? "Khóa tài khoản: " : "Mở khóa tài khoản: ";
        recordStatusLog(student.getId(), student.getStatus(), student.getStatus(),
                actionDesc + (request.getReason() != null ? request.getReason() : "Bởi Admin"),
                SecurityUtils.getCurrentAccountId());

        return toResponse(student);
    }

    @Override
    @Transactional
    public GuardianResponse addGuardian(Long studentId, GuardianRequest request) {
        findStudentById(studentId);

        Guardian guardian = new Guardian();
        guardian.setStudentId(studentId);
        guardian.setFullName(request.getFullName().trim());
        guardian.setRelationship(request.getRelationship().trim());
        guardian.setPhone(request.getPhone().trim());
        guardian.setEmail(emptyToNull(request.getEmail()));

        Guardian saved = guardianRepository.save(guardian);
        log.info("Thêm người giám hộ id={} cho học viên studentId={}", saved.getId(), studentId);
        return toGuardianResponse(saved);
    }

    @Override
    @Transactional(readOnly = true)
    public List<GuardianResponse> getGuardians(Long studentId) {
        findStudentById(studentId);
        return guardianRepository.findByStudentId(studentId)
                .stream().map(this::toGuardianResponse).toList();
    }

    @Override
    @Transactional
    public void deleteGuardian(Long studentId, Long guardianId) {
        findStudentById(studentId);
        Guardian guardian = guardianRepository.findById(guardianId)
                .orElseThrow(() -> new ErrorHandler(HttpStatus.NOT_FOUND, "Không tìm thấy người giám hộ với ID: " + guardianId));

        if (!guardian.getStudentId().equals(studentId)) {
            throw new ErrorHandler(HttpStatus.BAD_REQUEST, "Người giám hộ không thuộc về học viên này");
        }

        guardianRepository.delete(guardian);
        log.info("Đã xóa người giám hộ id={} của học viên studentId={}", guardianId, studentId);
    }

    @Override
    @Transactional(readOnly = true)
    public List<AcademicHistoryResponse> getAcademicHistories(Long studentId) {
        findStudentById(studentId);
        return academicHistoryRepository.findByStudentIdOrderByCreatedAtDesc(studentId)
                .stream().map(this::toAcademicHistoryResponse).toList();
    }

    @Override
    @Transactional
    public void delete(Long id) {
        Student student = findStudentById(id);
        // Soft delete: chuyển trạng thái sang INACTIVE
        if (!"INACTIVE".equalsIgnoreCase(student.getStatus())) {
            recordStatusLog(student.getId(), student.getStatus(), "INACTIVE", "Xóa mềm / ngừng kích hoạt học viên", SecurityUtils.getCurrentAccountId());
            student.setStatus("INACTIVE");
            studentRepository.save(student);
            log.info("Đã xóa mềm học viên id={} (status=INACTIVE)", id);
        }
    }

    // ===== Private Helper Methods =====

    private Student findStudentById(Long id) {
        return studentRepository.findById(id)
                .orElseThrow(() -> new ErrorHandler(HttpStatus.NOT_FOUND, "Không tìm thấy học viên với ID: " + id));
    }

    private void recordStatusLog(Long studentId, String oldStatus, String newStatus, String reason, Long changedBy) {
        StudentStatusLog logEntity = new StudentStatusLog();
        logEntity.setStudentId(studentId);
        logEntity.setOldStatus(oldStatus);
        logEntity.setNewStatus(newStatus);
        logEntity.setReason(reason);
        logEntity.setChangedBy(changedBy);
        statusLogRepository.save(logEntity);
    }

    /**
     * Sinh mã học viên theo định dạng STD + yyyyMM + 4 số ngẫu nhiên (VD: STD2026101234).
     * Thử tối đa 10 lần theo đúng yêu cầu REQ-STD-02.
     */
    private String generateUniqueStudentCode() {
        String prefix = "STD" + LocalDateTime.now().format(DateTimeFormatter.ofPattern("yyyyMM"));
        int attempts = 0;
        String code;
        do {
            int randomNum = ThreadLocalRandom.current().nextInt(1000, 9999);
            code = prefix + randomNum;
            attempts++;
            if (attempts > 10) {
                log.error("CẢNH BÁO QUẢN TRỊ: Đã thử sinh mã học viên 10 lần nhưng bị trùng: prefix={}", prefix);
                throw new ErrorHandler(HttpStatus.INTERNAL_SERVER_ERROR, "Không thể sinh mã học viên duy nhất sau 10 lần thử. Vui lòng liên hệ quản trị hệ thống");
            }
        } while (studentRepository.existsByStudentCode(code));
        return code;
    }

    private void copyPropertiesToResponse(Student student, StudentResponse response) {
        response.setId(student.getId());
        response.setStudentCode(student.getStudentCode());
        response.setLeadId(student.getLeadId());
        response.setUserId(student.getUserId());
        response.setFullName(student.getFullName());
        response.setEmail(student.getEmail());
        response.setPhone(student.getPhone());
        response.setDateOfBirth(student.getDob());
        response.setAddress(student.getAddress());
        response.setStatus(student.getStatus());
        response.setCreatedAt(student.getCreatedAt());
        response.setUpdatedAt(student.getUpdatedAt());

        Account account = null;
        if (student.getUserId() != null) {
            account = accountRepository.findById(student.getUserId()).orElse(null);
        }
        if (account == null && student.getEmail() != null) {
            account = accountRepository.findByEmail(student.getEmail()).orElse(null);
        }
        if (account != null) {
            response.setHasAccount(true);
            response.setIsAccountLocked(Boolean.TRUE.equals(account.getIsLocked()));
            if (student.getUserId() == null) {
                response.setUserId(account.getId());
            }
        } else {
            response.setHasAccount(false);
            response.setIsAccountLocked(false);
        }
    }

    private StudentResponse toResponse(Student student) {
        StudentResponse response = new StudentResponse();
        copyPropertiesToResponse(student, response);
        return response;
    }

    private GuardianResponse toGuardianResponse(Guardian guardian) {
        GuardianResponse response = new GuardianResponse();
        response.setId(guardian.getId());
        response.setStudentId(guardian.getStudentId());
        response.setFullName(guardian.getFullName());
        response.setRelationship(guardian.getRelationship());
        response.setPhone(guardian.getPhone());
        response.setEmail(guardian.getEmail());
        return response;
    }

    private AcademicHistoryResponse toAcademicHistoryResponse(AcademicHistory history) {
        AcademicHistoryResponse response = new AcademicHistoryResponse();
        response.setId(history.getId());
        response.setStudentId(history.getStudentId());
        response.setLeadId(history.getLeadId());
        response.setEnrollmentId(history.getEnrollmentId());
        response.setCourseId(history.getCourseId());
        response.setClassId(history.getClassId());
        response.setFinalGrade(history.getFinalGrade());
        response.setCreatedAt(history.getCreatedAt());
        return response;
    }

    private String emptyToNull(String str) {
        return (str == null || str.trim().isEmpty()) ? null : str.trim();
    }
}

package com.ecms_web_application.OC01.service;

import com.ecms_web_application.OC01.entity.enums.OtpPurpose;

/**
 * Service sinh / verify OTP.
 * Lưu OTP in-memory (chưa persist DB).
 */
public interface OtpService {

    // ============ GENERATE + SEND ============
    void generateAndSend(String email, OtpPurpose purpose);

    // ============ OTP STORAGE ============
    void saveOtp(String email, String otp);

    String getOtp(String email);

    void deleteOtp(String email);

    // ============ VERIFY ============
    boolean verify(String email, String otp, OtpPurpose purpose);

    // ============ VERIFIED STATUS ============
    void markEmailAsVerified(String email);

    boolean isEmailVerified(String email);

    void clearEmailVerified(String email);
}

// ============================================================
// FILE: ./src/main/java/com/ecms_web_application/OC01/service/ProgressCalculationService.java
// ============================================================
package com.ecms_web_application.OC01.service;

/**
 * Tính toán progress % ở cấp COURSE.
 *
 * Công thức:
 *   COMPLETION_BASED (Online) : completed required units (không tính CHAPTER/LESSON) / total required units
 *   SESSION_BASED    (Offline): DONE sessions / sessions != CANCELLED
 *
 * Nguyên tắc:
 *   - % không bao giờ giảm (giữ max giữa mới và cũ)
 *   - hasUncompletedNewContent = true khi HV đã từng 100% nhưng hiện chưa hoàn thành hết required units
 */
public interface ProgressCalculationService {

    /** Batch toàn bộ học viên có Progress COURSE trong khóa. */
    void recalculateForCourse(Long courseId);

    /** 1 học viên cụ thể; tạo Progress COURSE nếu chưa có. */
    void recalculateForStudent(Long accountId, Long courseId);

    /** Hook khi resource thay đổi (create/update/delete). */
    void onResourceChanged(Long courseId);

    /** Hook khi session thay đổi (DONE/CANCELLED/RESCHEDULED). */
    void onSessionChanged(Long courseId);

    /** Hook khi quiz được chấm (pass). */
    void onQuizGraded(Long accountId, Long courseId);
}

// ============================================================
package com.ecms_web_application.OC01.service;

import com.ecms_web_application.OC01.dto.response.course.ProgressResponse;
import org.springframework.data.domain.Page;
import org.springframework.data.domain.Pageable;
import java.util.List;

public interface ProgressService {
    Page<ProgressResponse> getMyCourses(Pageable pageable);
    ProgressResponse getMyProgressInCourse(Long courseId);
    List<ProgressResponse> getResourceProgresses(Long courseId);
    ProgressResponse updateResourceProgress(Long resourceId, Integer percentage, Long timeSpentSeconds);
    ProgressResponse markResourceCompleted(Long resourceId);
    /** FR_CRS_015: set cờ cần học bổ sung cho HV đã 100% khi course có resource mới. */
    void markHasUncompletedNewContent(Long courseId);

    /** FR_CRS_015: tính lại % COURSE, giữ mức cao nhất đã đạt. */
    void recalculateCourseProgress(Long courseId);
}

package com.ecms_web_application.OC01.service;

import com.ecms_web_application.OC01.dto.request.course.SessionCancelRequest;
import com.ecms_web_application.OC01.dto.request.course.SessionCreateRequest;
import com.ecms_web_application.OC01.dto.response.course.SessionResponse;
import java.util.List;

public interface SessionService {
    List<SessionResponse> listByCourse(Long courseId);
    SessionResponse getById(Long courseId, Long id);
    SessionResponse addManual(Long courseId, SessionCreateRequest request);
    SessionResponse cancel(Long id, SessionCancelRequest request);

    /** FR_CRS_009: gán/gỡ GV dạy thay cho 1 buổi. Không đụng session.teacher_id. */
    SessionResponse assignSubstituteTeacher(Long sessionId, Long substituteTeacherId);

    /** FR_CRS_009C: gán/gỡ trợ giảng cho 1 buổi. */
    SessionResponse assignAssistant(Long sessionId, Long assistantId);

    /** FR_CRS_009B: đổi GV chính của khóa + sync session SCHEDULED chưa có dạy thay. */
    void changeCourseMainTeacher(Long courseId, Long newTeacherId);

    /** FR_CRS_006: sinh session tự động từ start_date..end_date theo schedule_days. */
    com.ecms_web_application.OC01.dto.response.course.SessionGenerationResult generateSessions(Long courseId);

    /** Đánh dấu session DONE + trigger recalc Offline progress. */
    SessionResponse markDone(Long sessionId);

    void delete(Long id);
}
